# Bartenura on Mishnah Parah

ברטנורא על משנה פרה

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Tahorot

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Torat-Emet (Segments: 506, License: CC-BY-NC)

Version (En): Bartenura on Mishnah, trans. by Rabbi Robert Alpert, 2020 (Segments: 504)


## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:1)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר עֶגְלָה בַּת שְׁנָתָהּ.** עֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה צָרִיךְ שֶׁתְּהֵא בַת שְׁנָתָהּ וְלֹא יוֹתֵר:


רבי אליעזר אומר עגלה בת שנתה – a heifer whose neck was broken needs to be one year old and no more/older.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:2)


**וּפָרָה.** אֲדֻמָּה:


ופרה – red [heifer].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:3)


**בַּת שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים.** דִּלְשׁוֹן פָּרָה מַשְׁמַע דְּקַשִּׁישָׁא טְפֵי מֵעֶגְלָה:


ופרה בת שתי שתים – that the language of פרה/heifer implies that it is older than a עלגה/heifer (i.e., calf)


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים עֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה כְּשֵׁרָה אֲפִלּוּ בַת שְׁתַּיִם.** וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן בַּת שְׁנָתָהּ, אֶלָּא דַאֲפִלּוּ בַת שְׁתַּיִם עֶגְלָה הִיא. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּקַיְמָא לָן כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר עֵגֶל, בֶּן שָׁנָה הוּא, סָבְרֵי רַבָּנָן שָׁאנֵי עֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה דִּכְתִיב בָּהּ (דברים כא) עֶגְלַת בָּקָר, וְלֹא כְתִיב עֶגְלָה סְתָם:


וחכמים אומרים עלגה ערופה כשרה אפילו בת שתים – and all the more so, when it is one year old, but even if it is two years old, it is a עגלה /calf, and even though that we hold that every place where it states עגל, it is one year old, the Rabbis hold that the heifer whose neck is broken is different, as it is written regarding it (Deuteronomy 21:3): “[The elders of the town nearest to the corpse] shall then take a heifer/עגלת בקר, [which has never been worked, which has never pulled in a yoke],” but it is not written a mere/plain עגלה/heifer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:5)


**שֶׁמָּא תַשְׁחִיר שֶׁלֹּא תִפָּסֵל.** שֶׁאִם יִוָּלְדוּ בָהּ שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת שְׁחוֹרוֹת פְּסוּלָה, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) אֲדֻמָּה תְמִימָה, שֶׁתְּהֵא תְמִימָה בְאַדְמִימוּת. וַהֲלָכָה, דְּפָרָה אֲדֻמָּה בַּת שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים אוֹ בַת אַרְבַּע וּזְקֵנָה כְּשֵׁרָה, אֶלָּא שֶׁאֵין מַמְתִּינִין לָהּ, שֶׁמָּא תַשְׁחִיר וְתִפָּסֵל:


שמא תשחיר שלא תפסל (lest a hair turn black and it should [not] otherwise become unfit) – for if two black hairs would grow in it, it would be unfit, as it written (Numbers 19:2): “[Instruct the Israelite people to bring you] a red [cow] without blemish, that its being without blemish/its purity, is in its reddishness and it went away, for a red cow which is three years old or four [years old] and old is fit/proper, but we don’t wait for it, lest a hair turn black and it should [not] otherwise become unfit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:6)


**לֹא שָׁמַעְתִּי אֶלָּא שְׁלָשִׁית.** כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי שֶׁאֵין כְּשֵׁרָה לְפָרָה אֲדֻמָּה אֶלָּא שְׁלָשִׁית:


לא שמעתי אלא שלשית (I heard only “Shelasheet”) – I heard that a red cow is not kosher/fit other than one which is “Shelasheet” (i.e., in its third year).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:7)


**כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי סְתָם.** וְאֵין לִי לְפָרֵשׁ:


כך שמעתי סתם (such I heard plain) – and I have nothing to explain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:8)


**שְׁלִישִׁית.** מַשְׁמַע שְׁלִישִׁית לַאֲחֵרוֹת בְּמִנְיָן, שֶׁנּוֹלְדוּ שְׁתֵּי פָרוֹת אֲחֵרוֹת לְפָנֶיהָ וְזֹאת שְׁלִישִׁית לָהֶן:


שלישית – implies, third to the others in sequence, that two other cows were born before her and this is the third to them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:9)


**שְׁלָשִׁית.** מַשְׁמַע שֶׁהִיא בַת שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים:


שלשית- implies that she is three years old.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:10)


**רְבִיעִי לַאֲחֵרִים.** כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ אַרְבָּעָה כְרָמִים שַׁיָּךְ לוֹמַר עַל הָאַחֲרוֹן שֶׁהוּא רְבִיעִי לָאֲחֵרִים, אֲבָל רְבָעִי מַשְׁמַע כֶּרֶם בֶּן אַרְבַּע שָׁנִים:


רביעי לאחרים – when there are four vineyards, it belongs to say about the last that it is the fourth to the others, but [the term] רבעי/that which is in the fourth year implies a vineyard that is four years old.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:11)


**הָאוֹכֵל בְּבַיִת הַמְנֻגָּע פְּרָס.** הַשּׁוֹהֶה בְּבַיִת הַמְנֻגָּע כְּדֵי אֲכִילַת פְּרָס, מְטַמֵּא בְגָדִים שֶׁעָלָיו. פְּרָס הוּא חֲצִי כִכָּר מִשָּׁלֹשׁ כִּכָּרוֹת לַקַּב:


האוכל בבית המנוגע פרס – he who tarries in a leprous house in order to eat a piece of bread, defiles that clothing that is upon him. [The word] פרס is half of a loaf, from three loaves to a KAB.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:12)


**אָמְרוּ לוֹ אֱמֹר מִשְּׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה לִסְאָה.** כְּלוֹמַר, הָיָה לְךָ לוֹמַר מִשְּׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה לַסְּאָה, שֶׁהַסְּאָה שִׁשָּׁה קַבִּין, וְשָׁלֹשׁ כִּכָּרוֹת לַקַּב הֵן שְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה לַסְּאָה:


אמרו לו אמור משמונה עשר לסאה – meaning to say, you should have said from eighteen to a Seah, that the Seah is six KABIM, and three loaves to a KAB are eighteen to a Seah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:13)


**כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי.** מֵרַבּוֹתַי, וְאֵין בְּיָדִי לְפָרֵשׁ לָמָּה לֹא אָמְרוּ הֵם מִשְּׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה לַסְּאָה:


כך שמעתי – from my Rabbis/teachers, and it is not in my hands to explain why they didn’t say from eighteen to a Seah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:14)


**אֲנִי אֲפָרֵשׁ.** מַאי נַפְקָא מִנַּהּ בֵּין זֶה לָזֶה:


אני אפרש – what the practical difference is between this and that [and why they didn’t say eighteen to a Seah].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:1:15)


**מִשָּׁלֹשׁ לְקָב אֵין בּוֹ חַלָּה.** דְּחָמֵשׁ רְבִיעִיּוֹת קֶמַח וְעוֹד, חַיָּבִין בְּחַלָּה, וְהַלָּשׁ קַב בִּלְבַד אֵין מַפְרִישׁ מִמֶּנָּה חַלָּה. הִלְכָּךְ כְּשֶׁאַתָּה אוֹמֵר מִשָּׁלֹשׁ כִּכָּרוֹת לַקַּב, מַשְׁמַע שֶׁלֹּא הֻפְרָשׁ מֵהֶן חַלָּה, וּכְשֶׁאַתָּה אוֹמֵר מִשְּׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה לַסְּאָה, נִמְצָא שֶׁכָּל כִּכָּר הוּא פָחוֹת מַה שֶּׁמִעֲטַתּוּ חַלָּתוֹ, שֶׁהַלָּשׁ סְאָה צָרִיךְ לְהַפְרִישׁ מִמֶּנָּה חַלָּה:


משלש לקב אין בו חלה – for five fourths flour and more, are liable for separating Hallah, and a person who kneads only one KAB separates Hallah from it. Therefore, when you say, from three loaves to a KAB, it implies that Hallah had not been separated from them, but when you say “from eighteen to a Seah,” it is found that each loaf is less that what we reduced for Hallah and a person who kneads a Seah must separate Hallah from it.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:2:1)


**וּפַר שֵׁנִי בֶן בָּקָר תִּקַּח לְחַטָּאת.** בְּטָהֳרַת הַלְוִיִּם כְּתִיב [שָׁם ח] בְּפָרָשַׁת בְּהַעֲלֹתְךָ. וְהַאי שֵׁנִי יְתֵרָה הוּא, דְּכֵיוָן דִּכְתִיב בָּתְרֵיהּ וַעֲשֵׂה אֶת הָאֶחָד חַטָּאת וְאֶת הָאֶחָד עֹלָה, מִמֵּילָא יָדַעְנָא דִּתְרֵי נִינְהוּ, אֶלָּא כָתוּב שֵׁנִי לְלַמֵּד שֶׁהוּא בֶן שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים, דְּכִי הֵיכִי דִלְבַת שָׁלֹשׁ קָרֵי שְׁלָשִׁית, הָכִי נַמִּי לְבֶן שְׁתַּיִם קָרֵי שֵׁנִי:


ופר שני בן בקר תקח לחטאת- regarding the purification of the Levities, it I s written in the Torah portion of B’haalotekha (Numbers 8:8), and this "שני"/second is extraneous, for since it is written after it (Numbers 8:12): “One shall be offered to the LORD as a purification offering (i.e., sin-offering) and the other as a burnt offering, automatically one knows that there are two [bulls], but rather, it is written [the word]"שני" to teach that it is two years old, for just as for something three years old, we call שלשית, here also for something two years old it is called "שני".


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:2:2)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים כוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים כו' – and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:3:1)


**מִיּוֹם לְיוֹם.** נוֹלַד בְּאֶחָד בֶּאֱלוּל, לֹא שָׁלְמָה לוֹ שָׁנָה עַד אֶחָד בֶּאֱלוּל שֶׁל שָׁנָה שְׁנִיָּה, דְּבָתַר שְׁנָתוֹ אָזְלִינַן, וְלֹא בָתַר שָׁנִים שֶׁל מִנְיַן הָעוֹלָם. וְאִם נִתְעַבְּרָה שָׁנָה, נִתְעַבְּרָה לוֹ. וְכֵן בְּאֵילִים בְּנֵי שְׁתַּיִם:


מיום ליום – if it was born on the first of Elul, it didn’t complete a full year until the first of Elul of the second year. For we follow after the its year, but not after the years of the counting of the world. But if the year were intercalated, it was intercalated for it. And similarly, with rams that are two years old.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:3:2)


**אֵינוֹ כָשֵׁר לֹא לְאַיִל.** דְּאֵינוֹ נֶחְשָׁב אַיִל עַד שֶׁיִּכָּנֵס שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם מִשָּׁנָה שְׁנִיָּה לוֹ:


אינו כשר לא לאיל – for it is not considered a ram until it enters thirty days from the second year for it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:3:3)


**פַּלְגָּס.** לְשׁוֹן פֶּלֶג, כְּלוֹמַר מְמֻצָּע בֵּין זֶה לָזֶה, יָצָא מִכְּלַל כֶּבֶשׂ וְלִכְלַל אַיִל לֹא בָא:


פלגס (a sheep beyond the age of a כבש and below that of an איל ; a youth in the intermediate stage between boyhood and maturity) – It is the language of פלג/part, middle, meaning to say average between this and that, it has exited from the category of כבש but has not entered the category of איל (i.e., a כבש is a sheep at least one year of age; the איל is at least two years old).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:3:4)


**נוֹקֵד.** לְשׁוֹן אֲשֶׁר הָיָה בַנֹּקְדִים (עמוס א) שֶׁפֵּרוּשׁוֹ בַּעֲלֵי הַצֹּאן:


נוקד (shepherd) – it is the language of (Amos 1:1): "[דברי עמוס] אשר-היה בנקדים מתקוע"/[The words of Amos] a sheep breeder from Tekoa,” whose explanation is the owners of sheet. (Also, possibly the word נוקד (NUKAD) – a stamped, distinct coin).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:3:5)


**פַּרְכָדִיגְמָא.** לְמַטְבֵּעַ שֶׁנִּפְסַל קוֹרִין כֵּן בְּלָשׁוֹן יְוָנִי. אַף זֶה נִפְסַל מִתּוֹרַת אַיִל וּמִתּוֹרַת כֶּבֶשׂ:


פרכדיגמא (false coin, counterfeit) -to a coin that is invalid that they call it PARCHADIGMA in the Greek language. Even this is invalid from the designation/definition of an איל or of a כבש.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:3:6)


**מֵבִיא עָלָיו נִסְכֵּי אַיִל.** שְׁלִישִׁית הַהִין. וְנַפְקָא לָן מִדִּכְתִיב אוֹ לָאַיִל, לְרַבּוֹת אֶת הַפַּלְגָּס לְנִסְכֵּי אַיִל:


מביא עליו נכסי אלי – one-third of a Hin. And we derive it from the fact that it is written (Numbers 7:6-7): “the case of a ram, [you shall present as a grain offering: two-thirds of a measure of choice flour with a third of a Hin of oil mixed in] and a third of a Hin of wine as a libation – [as an offering of pleasing odor to the LORD],” to include the פלגס/sheep beyond the age of a כבש and below that of an איל.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:4:1)


**חַטּאֹת הַצִּבּוּר.** כְּגוֹן שְׂעִירֵי רָאשֵׁי חֳדָשִׁים וְשֶׁל מוֹעֲדוֹת וְעוֹלוֹתֵיהֶן, כֻּלְּהוּ בְנֵי שָׁנָה נִינְהוּ:


חטאות הצבור (see Leviticus 4:14) – as for example the goats of the New Moons and the Festivals and their burnt offerings (see Numbers 28:3 and following) , all of them are a year old.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:4:2)


**חַטַּאת הַיָּחִיד.** כִּשְׂבָּה אוֹ שְׂעִירָה:


חטאת היחיד (see Leviticus 4:28, 32 and 5:6) – a female sheep or a female goat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:4:3)


**וְאִם הִקְרִיבוּם בְּיוֹם שְׁמִינִי כְּשֵׁרִים.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כב) וּמִיּוֹם הַשְּׁמִינִי וָהָלְאָה יֵרָצֶה, וְנֶאֱמַר בִּבְכוֹר (שמות כב) בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁמִינִי תִּתְּנוֹ לִי, מַה בְּכוֹר עָשָׂה יוֹם שְׁמִינִי כִּשְׁמִינִי וָהָלְאָה, אַף בִּשְׁאָר קָרְבָּנוֹת עָשָׂה יוֹם שְׁמִינִי כִּשְׁמִינִי וָהָלְאָה. מִיהוּ בְּמִידִי דְחוֹבָה מִצְוָה מִן הַמֻּבְחָר לְהָבִיא מִיּוֹם שְׁלֹשִׁים וָאֵילָךְ:


ואם הקריבום שיום השמיני כשרים – as it is written (Leviticus 22:27): “and from the eighth day on it shall be acceptable [as a gift to the LORD},” and it is stated regarding a firstborn (Exodus 22:29): “[You shall do the same with your cattle and your flocks: seven days it shall remain with its mother;] on the eighth day you shall give it to Me,” just as the firstborn that he made the eighth day like the eighth [day] and beyond, so also with the rest of the sacrifices he made the eighth day like the eighth [day] and beyond. However, with a matter that is an obligation of the most preferable way of performing a Mitzvah is to bring it from the thirtieth day onward.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 1:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/1:4:4)


**וְהַפֶּסַח.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּחוֹבָה הוּא, לֹא חָשׁוּ חֲכָמִים, מִשּׁוּם דְּנָסְבֵי לְפִי נְפָשׁוֹת, שֶׁלֹּא יָבֹא לִידֵי נוֹתָר כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵינוֹ נֶאֱכָל [אֶלָּא] לַחֲבוּרָה אֶחָת:


והפסח – even though it is an obligation, the Sages were not quiet/silent, because they take it according to the number of souls, so that they don’t come to a case of left over (i.e., part of the offering left over after time permitted for it to be eat. One who eats the leftover portions of an offering is punishable by extirpation – see Tractate Zevahim, Chapter 5, Mishnah 8 which states that the Passover offering an only be consumed until midnight) since it is not eaten [other] than for one fellowship.



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:1:1)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר פָּרָה מְעֻבֶּרֶת כְּשֵׁרָה.** רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וְרַבָּנָן דְּהָכָא קָיְמֵי בְשִׁיטַת רַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְּאָמַר לְקַמָּן בְּפִרְקִין דְּעָלָה עָלֶיהָ זָכָר אֵינָהּ פְּסוּלָה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן עָלָה עָלֶיהָ מִדַּעַת בַּעַל הַפָּרָה, וּפְלִיגֵי הָכָא בִּמְעֻבֶּרֶת שֶׁעָלָה עָלֶיהָ זָכָר מֵעַצְמוֹ בְּלֹא דַעַת הַבְּעָלִים. וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר דְּמַכְשִׁיר סָבַר עֻבָּר יֶרֶךְ אִמּוֹ הוּא וְהָוֵי כְגוּפָהּ דְּפָרָה, וְרַבָּנָן דְּפָסְלֵי סָבְרֵי עֻבָּר לָאו יֶרֶךְ אִמּוֹ הוּא וְנִפְסֶלֶת מֵחֲמַת מְלָאכָה, שֶׁנּוֹשֵׂאת אֶת עֻבָּרָהּ. וַהֲלָכָה שֶׁהִיא פְסוּלָה מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁעָלָה עָלֶיהָ זָכָר אֲפִלּוּ שֶׁלֹּא מִדַּעַת בְּעָלִים וַאֲפִלּוּ אֵינָהּ מְעֻבֶּרֶת, וּדְלֹא כְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי אליעזר אומר פרה מעוברת כשרה – Rabbi Eliezer and the Rabbis of here established according to the approach of Rabbi Yehuda who mentions further on in our chapter (Mishnah 4) that if a bull brought it up upon the cow, it is not invalid unless it went up with the knowledge of the owner of the heifer, but they dispute here regarding a pregnant cow that a male went up on her on his own without the knowledge of the owners, for Rabbi Eliezer who declares it fit holds that fetus is the thigh/haunch of its mother and it is like the body of he cow, but the Rabbis who invalidate I hold that the fetus is not the thigh/haunch of it smother and it is disqualified from the work, that she carries the fetus. And the Halakha is that is invalid from the time when a male came up upon her, even without the knowledge of the owners, and even if she is not pregnant, and not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:1:2)


**אֵינָהּ נִלְקַחַת מִן הַנָּכְרִים.** שֶׁחֲשׁוּדִים עַל הָרְבִיעָה, וְשֶׁמָּא נִרְבַּעַת בְּיָדָם:


אינה נלקחת מן הנכרים – who are suspected the copulation (i.e., carnal connection with beasts/buggery (see Tractate Avodah Zarah, Chapter 2, Mishnah 1), for lest it was copulated at their hands.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:1:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים מַכְשִׁירִין.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַבְּהֵמָה נַעֲשֵׂית עֲקָרָה כְּשֶׁרוֹבְעָהּ, שֶׁהַנָּכְרִי חָס עַל בְּהֶמְתּוֹ שֶׁלֹּא תֵעָקֵר:


וחכמים מכשירין – [to purchase a red heifer from non-Jews] because the cow was made barren/impotent when it copulated with it, for the heathen has compassion on his cattle that it should not become barren/impotent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:1:4)


**וְלֹא זוֹ בִלְבַד.** לֹא בְפָרַת חַטָּאת בִּלְבַד פְּלִיגֵי רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וְרַבָּנָן, אֶלָּא בְכָל קָרְבְּנוֹת הַצִּבּוּר וְהַיָּחִיד פּוֹסֵל רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אִם נִלְקְחוּ מִן הַנָּכְרִים, דְּחָיֵשׁ לִרְבִיעָה. וַחֲכָמִים מַכְשִׁירִין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ולא זו בלבד – not only regarding the Red Heifer that Rabbi Eliezer and the Rabbis disputed, but regarding all of the community sacrifices and those of an individual, Rabbi Eliezer invalidates [them] if they were purchased from the heathens, for he is concerned for copulation/carnal connection with beasts, but the Sages declare them fit. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:1:5)


**בָּאִין מֵהָאָרֶץ וּמִחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ.** כָּל הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת בָּאִין בֵּין מִן הָאָרֶץ בֵּין מִחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ בֵּין מִן הֶחָדָשׁ בֵּין מִן הַיָּשָׁן:


באין מארץ ומחוצה לארץ – (see Tractate Menahot, Chapter 8, Mishnah 1) – all of the sacrifices come whether from the Land [of Israel] or from outside the Land [of Israel], whether from the new [grain] or whether form the old [grain – i.e., from before Passover].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:1:6)


**חוּץ מִן הָעֹמֶר וּשְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם.** דְּאָמַר קְרָא (ויקרא כג) כִּי תָבֹאוּ אֶל הָאָרֶץ וּקְצַרְתֶּם אֶת קְצִירָהּ וַהֲבֵאתֶם אֶת עֹמֶר, וּבִשְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם כְּתִיב (שם) מִמּוֹשְׁבֹתֵיכֶם תָּבִיאוּ לֶחֶם. וְאֵין בָּאִין אֶלָּא מִן הֶחָדָשׁ, דִּכְתִיב בְּהוּ מִנְחָה חֲדָשָׁה:


חוץ מן העומר ושתי הלחם - as Scripture states (Leviticus 23:10): [Speak to the Israelite people and say to them:] When you enter the land [that I am giving to you] and you reap its harvest, you shall bring the first sheaf of your harvest [to the priest],” and with the two bread loaves, it is written (Leviticus 23:17): “You shall bring from your settlements two loaves of bead as an elevation offering.” They do not come other from that which is new (and grown in the Land of Israel), as it is written (Leviticus 23:16): “an offering of new grain to the LORD.”



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:2:1)


**יָגֹד.** יַחְתֹּךְ. כְּמוֹ גֹּדּוּ אִילָנָא (דניאל ד):


יגוד (chop off) – he should cut off/sever. Like (Daniel 4:11): “hew down the tree”/גודו אילנא.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:2:2)


**וְהַנַּנֶּסֶת.** קְטַנָּה בְיוֹתֵר. שֶׁזֶּה מוּם בָּאָדָם וְאֵינוֹ מוּם בַּבְּהֵמָה:


והננסת (dwarf) – the smallest, for that is a blemish in a human but is not a blemish in an animal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:2:3)


**יַבֶּלֶת.** וורוא"ה בְּלַעַ"ז:


יבלת (wart on the skin – VAVROAH in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:2:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה פוֹסֵל.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ור' יהודה פוסל – and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:1)


**יוֹצֵא דֹפֶן פְּסוּלָה.** כִּשְׁאָר קָדָשִׁים. דִּכְתִיב [וַיִּקְרָא כב] כִּי יִוָּלֵד, פְּרָט לְיוֹצֵא דֹפֶן. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּפָרָה לָאו קָדְשֵׁי מִזְבֵּחַ הִיא אֶלָּא קָדְשֵׁי בֶדֶק הַבַּיִת, מִכָּל מָקוֹם יוֹצֵא דֹפֶן פְּסוּלָה, דְּחַטָּאת קַרְיֵהּ רַחֲמָנָא:


יוצא דופן (Caesarian section) – like the rest of Holy Things. As it is written (Leviticus 22:27): “[When an ox or a sheep or a goat] is born,” excluding that by Caesarean section. But even though a heifer/cow is not the Holy Things of the altar but rather the Holy Things of offerings for Temple repair, nevertheless, an animal born by caesarean section is invalid, for the All-Merciful called it a sin-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:2)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מַכְשִׁיר.** בְּאֶתְנָן וּמְחִיר דַּוְקָא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ר' אליעזר מכשיר – especially the fee for a whore and the fee for a dog. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:3)


**כָּל הַמּוּמִין הַפּוֹסְלִים בַּמֻּקְדָּשִׁים.** בִּבְכוֹרוֹת פֶּרֶק עַל אֵלּוּ מוּמִין, מְפֹרָשִׁים הַמּוּמִין הַפְּסוּלִין בַּמֻּקְדָּשִׁים:


כל המומין הפוסלים במוקדשים – In [Tractate] Bekhorot in the [sixth] chapter, “On account of these blemishes”/על אלה מומין (Mishnayot 1-12), the blemishes that invalidate the Holy Things are explained.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:4)


**פּוֹסְלִים בַּפָּרָה.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) אֲשֶׁר אֵין בָּהּ מוּם:


פוסלים כפרה – as it is written (Numbers 19:2): “in which there is no defect”/"אשר אין-בה מום".


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:5)


**עָבַר בָּהּ אֶת הַנָּהָר אָחַז בִּזְנָבָהּ.** נִסְתַּיַּע בָּהּ כְּשֶׁעָבַר אֶת הַנָּהָר:


עבר בה את הנהר אחז בזנבה – assisted/accompanied it when he crossed the river.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:6)


**קִפֵּל עָלֶיהָ אֶת הַמּוֹסֵרָה.** הַחֶבֶל שֶׁקּוֹשֵׁר אוֹתָהּ בּוֹ קִפְּלוֹ וּנְתָנוֹ עַל גּוּפָהּ:


קיפל עליה בת המוסרה (if he threw the reins over her back) – the rope that he ties her with they doubled and placed them on her body (i.e., back).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:7)


**נָתַן טַלִּיתוֹ עָלֶיהָ פְּסוּלָה.** נֶאֱמַר בְּפָרָה (שם) אֲשֶׁר לֹא עָלָה עָלֶיהָ עֹל, וְנֶאֱמַר בְּעֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה (דברים כא) אֲשֶׁר לֹא עֻבַּד בָּהּ אֲשֶׁר לֹא מָשְׁכָה בְּעֹל, מָה עֹל הָאָמוּר בְּעֶגְלָה עָשָׂה שְׁאָר עֲבוֹדָה פְסוּלָה כְּעֹל, אַף עֹל הָאָמוּר בְּפָרָה עָשָׂה שְׁאָר עֲבוֹדָה פְסוּלָה כְּעֹל:


מתן טליתו עליה פסולה – it states in [the portion of the] of the [Red] Heifer (Numbers 19:2): “and on which no yoke has been laid”/אשר לא-עלה עליה על", and it states regarding the heifer whose neck is broken (Deuteronomy 21:3): “a heifer which has never been worked, which has never pulled in a yoke”/"עגלת בקר אשר לא-עבד בה אשר לא-משכה בעל". Just as the yoke that is stated with the heifer (in Deuteronomy 21:3 and following) it did all of the rest of invalid work while yoked, even the yoke that is mentioned with the [Red] Heifer, it is the rest of the invalid work while yoked.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:8)


**בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁלֹּא תַחֲלִיק.** שֶׁלֹּא יַחֲלִיקוּ רַגְלֶיהָ וְתִפֹּל:


בשביל שלא תחליק – so that her feet should not slip and she would fall.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:3:9)


**לְצֹרֶךְ אַחֵר פְּסוּלָה.** וַאֲפִלּוּ לְצָרְכּוֹ וּלְצָרְכָּהּ פְּסוּלָה:


לצורך אחר פסולה – and even for his needs and for her needs it is invalid.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:4:1)


**עָלָה עָלֶיהָ זָכָר פְּסוּלָה.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא נִיחָא לֵיהּ, מִשּׁוּם דְּדָמֶיהָ יְקָרִים וּמַפְסִיד טוּבָא, וּבַעֲבוֹדָה דְלָא נִיחָא לֵיהּ לֹא מִפַּסְּלָה, דִּכְתִיב בְּעֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה [שָׁם] אֲשֶׁר לֹא עֻבַּד בָּהּ, כְּתִיב עָבַד וְקָרִינַן עֻבַּד, מָה עָבַד דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ, אַף עֻבַּד דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ, וְיָלְפִינַן פָּרַת חַטָּאת מִנַּהּ. מִכָּל מָקוֹם הוֹאִיל וּבִשְׁאָר פָּרוֹת הֲוָה נִיחָא לֵיהּ, חָשְׁבִינַן לְגַבֵּי פָרַת חַטָּאת נַמִּי נִיחָא לֵיהּ. וְעוֹד אִם בָּאתָ לְהַכְשִׁיר אִם כֵּן הֲוָה נִיחָא לֵיהּ, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי פְּסוּלָה:


עלה עליה זכר פסולה – even though it is not what it prefers, because its costs are expensive and [the owner) loses a lot, and regarding the work it is not what he prefers it does not invalidate it, as it written regarding the heifer whose neck is broken (Deuteronomy 21;3); “a heifer which has never been worked, it is written as עבד – but it is read as עובד. Just as עבד is preferable to him, even עובד is preferable to him, and we derive the sin-offering of the heifer from it. Nevertheless, since that with the rest of the cows it was preferable to him (i.e., the owner), we consider that regarding the sin-offering of the heifer is also preferable to him (i.e., the owner). And further, if you came to validate it, if so, it is also preferable to him (i.e., the owner), and because of this it is invalidated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:4:2)


**הֶעֱלָהוּ פְּסוּלָה.** לָאו דַּוְקָא הֶעֱלָהוּ, אֶלָּא אִם עָלָה עָלֶיהָ לְדַעַת הַבְּעָלִים, אֲפִלּוּ עָלָה מֵעַצְמוֹ פְּסוּלָה. וְאִם שֶׁלֹּא לְדַעַת הַבְּעָלִים, כְּשֵׁרָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


העלהו פסולה – Not exactly “brought it up” [upon the cow], but rather, if it went up upon her (i.e. the cow) with the knowledge of the owners, even if it went up on its own, it is invalid. But if it went up [on the cow] without the knowledge of the owners, it is fit/appropriate. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:5:1)


**הָיוּ בָהּ שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת שְׁחוֹרוֹת פְּסוּלָה.** דִּכְתִיב אֲדֻמָּה תְּמִימָה, אִי בְמוּמִין הֲרֵי כְבָר אָמוּר אֲשֶׁר אֵין בָּהּ מוּם. אֶלָּא שֶׁתְּהֵא תְמִימָה בְאַדְמִימוּת:


היו בה שתי שערות שחורות פסולה – as it is written (Numbers 19:2): “[This is the ritual that the LORD has commanded: Instruct the Israelite people to bring you] a red cow,” if there are blemish, it is already stated [in that Biblical verse]: “in which thee is no defect,” but rather that it will be pure in its redness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:5:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר בְּתוֹךְ כּוֹס אֶחָד.** גָּרְסִינַן, וְלֹא גָרְסִינַן אֲפִלּוּ. בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁהָיָה שׁוֹנֶה תַנָּא קַמָּא גֻּמָּא, הָיָה שׁוֹנֶה רַבִּי יְהוּדָה כּוֹס. גֻּמָּא וְכוֹס אֶחָד הוּא, אֶלָּא שֶׁחַיָּב אָדָם לוֹמַר בִּלְשׁוֹן רַבּוֹ, שֶׁרַבּוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי יְהוּדָה הָיָה קוֹרֵא לְגֻמָּא כּוֹס. הָכִי מוּכָח בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא:


רבי יהודה אומר בתוך כוס אחד – we have this reading. But we don’t have the reading: "ואפילו"/and even, and in place that the first Tanna would teach "גומא"/follicle, Rabbi Yehuda would teach a "כוס" /follicle (i.e., cavity). [The words]"גומא" and "כוס" are one [and the same], and it is obligatory for a person to state them in the language of his Rabbi/teacher (see Tractate Eduyot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 3 for an enunciation of this rule), that the Rabbi/teacher of Rabbi Yehuda would call a "גומא" a "כוס" , this is proven in the Tosefta (see Tractate Parah, Chapter 2, Halakha 7).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:5:3)


**מוֹכִיחוֹת.** מְכֻוָּנוֹת וּסְמוּכוֹת זוֹ בְצַד זוֹ:


מוכיחות – corresponding and adjacent, this one (i.e., hair follicle) on the side of that one (i.e., hair follicle).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:5:4)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר אֲפִלּוּ חֲמִשִּׁים.** כְּלוֹמַר אֲפִלּוּ טוּבָא וְהֵן מְפֻזָּרוֹת, יִתְלֹשׁ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא:


ר' אליעזר אומר אפילו חמשים – meaning to say, even more and they are scattered, one should uproot them (and the heifer would be fit/appropriate). But the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:5:5)


**אַחַר הַנִּרְאֶה.** הַיְנוּ אַחַר רֹאשָׁן שֶׁל שְׂעָרוֹת:


אחר הנראה – that is after the heads of the hairs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 2:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/2:5:6)


**אַחַר הָעִקָּר.** וְאִם עִקָּרָן מַאְדִּים וְרֹאשָׁן מַשְׁחִיר וְיֵשׁ בָּעִקָּר שֶׁמַּאְדִּים כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּנָּטֵל בְּפִי הַזּוּג, יִגְזֹז רֹאשָׁן הַמַּשְׁחִיר וְהִיא כְשֵׁרָה. אֲבָל אִם אֵין בָּעִקָּר הַמַּאְדִּים שִׁעוּר שֶׁיּוּכְלוּ לְהִנָּטֵל בְּפִי הַזּוּג, פְּסוּלָה אֲפִלּוּ לְדִבְרֵי חֲכָמִים. שֶׁשִּׁעוּר שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת הָאֲמוּרוֹת בְּכָל מָקוֹם, הוּא כְדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיוּ נִטָּלוֹת בְּפִי הַזּוּג. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


אחר העיקר – but if their roots grow red and their heads grow black, and there are in the roots that grows red in order that it can be removed with scissors , he should cut their heads that grow black and it is fit/appropriate/kosher. But if there aren’t in the root that grows red a measurement that they would be able to be removed with scissors, it is invalid even according to the words of the Sages. For the measurement of two hairs that is stated in every place, is in order that they could be removed with scissors. And this is the Halakha.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:1)


**שִׁבְעַת יָמִים כוּ'‏ מַפְרִישִׁין כֹּהֵן הַשּׂוֹרֵף אֶת הַפָּרָה.** דִּכְתִיב בְּפָרָשַׁת מִלֻּאִים (ויקרא ח) כַּאֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה בַּיּוֹם הַזֶּה צִוָּה ה'‏ לַעֲשׂת לְכַפֵּר עֲלֵיכֶם, וְדָרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ, לַעֲשׂת, זֶה מַעֲשֵׂה פָרָה. לְכַפֵּר עֲלֵיכֶם, זֶה מַעֲשֵׂה יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים. שֶׁהַכֹּהֵן הַשּׂוֹרֵף אֶת הַפָּרָה וְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל הָעוֹבֵד בְּיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים טָעוּן פְּרִישָׁה שִׁבְעָה כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיוּ טְעוּנִים אַהֲרֹן וּבָנָיו בְּשִׁבְעַת יְמֵי הַמִּלֻּאִים, דִּכְתִיב (שם) וּמִפֶּתַח אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד לֹא תֵצְאוּ שִׁבְעַת יָמִים. וְהָיוּ מַפְרִישִׁין אוֹתוֹ מֵאִשְׁתּוֹ שֶׁמָּא יָבֹא עַל אִשְׁתּוֹ וְתִמָּצֵא נִדָּה, וְהַבּוֹעֵל נִדָּה טָמֵא שִׁבְעַת יָמִים וְלֹא יוּכַל לִשְׂרֹף הַפָּרָה:


שבעת ימים כו' מפרישין כהן השורף את הפרה – as it is written in the portion of investment/inauguration [of the Kohanim] (Leviticus 8:34): "כאשר עשה ביום הזה צוה ה' לעשת לכפר עליכם"/”Everything done today, the LORD has commanded to be done [seven days], to make expiation for you,” and our Rabbis/teachers expounded (see Talmud Tractate Yoma 2a): "לעשת" /to be done – this is the act of the [Red] Heifer, "לכפר עליכם"/to make expiation for you – this is the act of Yom Kippur. That the Kohen who burns the heifer and the High Priest/Kohen Gadol who serves on the Day of Atonement requires separation for seven days] like Aaron and his sons would require in the seven days of investment/inauguration, as is written (Leviticus 8:33): “You shall not go outside the entrance of the Tent of Meeting for seven days,” And they would separate him from his wife, lest he come upon his wife and she would be found to be a menstruant woman and someone who has sexual relations with a menstruant woman is impure for seven days and is not able to burn the cow/heifer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:2)


**הַבִּירָה.** אִית דְּאָמְרֵי מָקוֹם הָיָה בְהַר הַבַּיִת וּבִירָה שְׁמוֹ. וְאִית דְּאָמְרֵי כָּל הַמִּקְדָּשׁ קָרוּי בִּירָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר אֶל הַבִּירָה וְגוֹ'‏ [דִּבְרֵי הַיָּמִים א יט]:


הבירה – there are those that say that this place was on the Temple Mount and “Birah” was its name. But there are those who say that the entire Temple is called “Birah,” as it states (I Chronicles 29:19: “[As to my son, Solomon, give him a whole heart to observe Your commandments, Your admonitions, and Your laws, and to fulfill them all,] and to build this temple for which I have made provision.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:3)


**צָפוֹנָה מִזְרָחָה.** טַעֲמָא דְמַפְרִישִׁים הַכֹּהֵן הַשּׂוֹרֵף אֶת הַפָּרָה לַלִּשְׁכָּה שֶׁהִיא צְפוֹנִית מִזְרָחִית, כֵּיוָן דְּחַטָּאת הִיא וְחַטָּאת טְעוּנָה צָפוֹן, תַּקִּינוּ לֵיהּ רַבָּנָן בְּצָפוֹנָה מִזְרָחָה כִּי הֵיכִי דְלֶהֱוֵי לַהּ הֶכֵּרָא:


צפונה מזרחה – the reason that they separate the Kohen who burns the Heifer/cow to a chamber that is in the northeast, since it is a sin offering [being offered] and the sin-offering requires the north (see Numbers 19:9), the Rabbis established for him in the northeast just that there will be for him recognition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:4)


**וּבֵית אֶבֶן הָיְתָה נִקְרֵאת.** לְפִי שֶׁכָּל מַעֲשֶׂיהָ הָיוּ בִכְלֵי גְלָלִים כְּלֵי אֲבָנִים כְּלֵי אֲדָמָה שֶׁאֵינָן מְקַבְּלִין טֻמְאָה, וּבָהֶם בִּלְבַד הָיָה מִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ כָּל שִׁבְעַת יְמֵי הַפְּרִישָׁה, מִשּׁוּם דִּטְבוּל יוֹם כָּשֵׁר בְּפָרָה, וְהָיוּ מְטַמְּאִין הַכֹּהֵן הַשּׂוֹרֵף אֶת הַפָּרָה וּמַטְבִּילִין אוֹתוֹ מִיָּד, לְהוֹצִיא מִלִּבָּן שֶׁל צְדוֹקִים שֶׁאוֹמְרִים בִּמְעֹרָבֵי שֶׁמֶשׁ הָיְתָה נַעֲשֵׂית, תַּקִּינוּ לַהּ רַבָּנָן מַעֲלוֹת יְתֵרוֹת כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יְזַלְזְלוּ בָּהּ:


ובית אבן היתה נקראת – because all of its acts were with vessels of untrimmed stone, vessels of stone and vessels of earth that are not susceptible to receive defilement, and in them alone he would use all seven of the days of separation, because someone who is a person who immersed himself that day/טבול יום is fit/kosher [to offer up] the heifer, and they would defile the Kohen that burns the heifer and would immerse him immediately to remove (the false opinion) out of the heart of the Sadducees (i.e., to demonstrate, by practice, the rejection of their opinion) who state that it was done at sunset, the Rabbis established for it extra preference so that they would not disregard/despise it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:5)


**כָּל שִׁבְעַת הַיָּמִים.** שֶׁל יְמֵי הַפְּרִישָׁה:


כל שבעת הימים – of the days of separation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:6)


**מִכָּל חַטָּאוֹת שֶׁהָיוּ שָׁם.** מִכָּל הַפָּרוֹת שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ מִימוֹת מֹשֶׁה רַבֵּנוּ וְעַד אוֹתוֹ זְמַן, שֶׁמִּכָּל פָּרָה וּפָרָה הָיוּ נוֹתְנִין קְצָת לְמִשְׁמֶרֶת, וְיוֹם רִאשׁוֹן מַזִּין עָלָיו מֵאֵפֶר פָּרָה אַחַת וְיוֹם שֵׁנִי מֵאַחֶרֶת וְיוֹם שְׁלִישִׁי מֵאַחֶרֶת:


מכל חטאות שהיו שם – all the heifers that were made from the days of Moses our Teacher/Rabbi and until that time. For each and every Red Heifer that they would give a bit for protection, and on the first day they would sprinkle upon him from the ashes of one heifer and on the second day from another and the third day from another.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:1:7)


**בַּשְּׁלִישִׁי וּבַשְּׁבִיעִי.** לִפְרִישָׁתוֹ. וּבֵין תַּנָּא קַמָּא וּבֵין רַבִּי יוֹסֵי תַּרְוַיְהוּ סְבִירָא לְהוּ טְבִילָה בִזְמַנָּהּ מִצְוָה, וְקָא מִפַּלְּגִי בְהַזָּאָה אִי מַקְּשִׁינַן לַהּ לִטְבִילָה אִי לֹא. תַּנָּא קַמָּא מַקִּישׁ הַזָּאָה לִטְבִילָה, וְהַזָּאָה בִזְמַנָּהּ מִצְוָה, וְכָל יוֹמָא מִשְּׁלֹשֶׁת יָמִים הָרִאשׁוֹנִים אִיכָּא לְסַפּוֹקֵי שֶׁמָּא הַיּוֹם יוֹם שְׁלִישִׁי לְטֻמְאָתוֹ, וְהַשְּׁלֹשָׁה אַחֲרוֹנִים כָּל יוֹמָא מְסַפְּקִינַן שֶׁמָּא שְׁבִיעִי. וּרְבִיעִי לֹא בָעֵי הַזָּאָה, דְּלָאו בַּשְּׁלִישִׁי אִיכָּא לְסַפּוֹקֵי וְלֹא בַשְּׁבִיעִי אִיכָּא לְסַפּוֹקֵי, שֶׁאֵין הַזָּאָה בַשְּׁבִיעִי עוֹלָה מִשּׁוּם הַזָּאַת שְׁבִיעִי עַד שֶׁיַּזֶּה בַשְּׁלִישִׁי שֶׁלְּפָנָיו. וְכָל שִׁבְעָה לָאו דַּוְקָא, דְּעַל כָּרְחֲךָ הָא קַיְמָא לָן הַזָּאָה שְׁבוּת וְאֵין דּוֹחָה אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת. וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי לֹא מַקִּישׁ הַזָּאָה לִטְבִילָה, וְהַזָּאָה בִזְמַנָּהּ לָאו מִצְוָה, הִלְכָּךְ לֹא בָעֵי הַזָּאָה אֶלָּא שְׁלִישִׁי וּשְׁבִיעִי בִּלְבַד. וְרַבִּי חֲנִינָא סְגַן הַכֹּהֲנִים נַמִּי כְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי סְבִירָא לֵיהּ דְּהַזָּאָה בִזְמַנָּהּ אֵינָהּ מִצְוָה, וְהַכֹּהֵן הַשּׂוֹרֵף אֶת הַפָּרָה מַעֲלָה בְעָלְמָא הוּא, כִּי הֵיכִי דְלָא לִזַלְזְלוּ בָהּ מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהִיא נַעֲשֵׂית בִּטְבוּל יוֹם:


בשלישי ובשביעי – [on the third and seventh days] of his separation. And between the first Tanna/teacher and Rabbi Yossi, both of them hold that immersion at its appropriate time is a Mitzvah, but they differ with regard to sprinkling if they make an analogy to immersion or not, the first Tanna/teacher makes an analogy between sprinkling and ritual immersion and sprinkling at its appropriate time is a Mitzvah, and each day from the first seven days there is doubt lest today is their third day of his defilement, and the three concluding days – each day we have doubt that perhaps it is the seventh [day]. But on the fourth [day], it does not require sprinkling, for it is not on the third [day], one can doubt, but one cannot doubt on the seventh [day], for the sprinkling on the seventh [day] does not count because sprinkling on the seventh [day] until he actually sprinkled on the third [day] that is prior to it. And all of the seven [days] are not exclusively, for perforce we hold that sprinkling is שבות /a rabbinic degree to enhance the character of the Shabbat as a day of rest, and does not supersede the Sabbath. But Rabbi Yossi does not make an analogy between sprinkling and ritual immersion, and sprinkling at the appropriate time is not a Mitzvah, therefore, he doesn’t require sprinkling other than on the third and on the seventh [days] alone. But Rabbi Hanina the Prefect of the Kohanim also holds like Rabbi Meir, that sprinkling at its appropriate time is not a Mitzvah, and the Kohen who burns the Heifer is a mere preference, just as they would not disregard/despise it because it is done with a person who is immersed himself that day/טבול יום.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:1)


**בְּנוּיוֹת עַל גַּבֵּי הַסֶּלַע.** צוּר הַחַלָּמִישׁ שֶׁנִּכָּר שֶׁהָיָה שָׁם מִתְּחִלַּת בְּרִיָּתוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם. וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן הֶחְמִירוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת תַּחְתָּיו חָלָל, מִפְּנֵי קֶבֶר הַתְּהוֹם, שֶׁמָּא יֵשׁ תַּחְתָּיו קֶבֶר וְאֵין לוֹ לַמֵּת אֹהֶל חָלָל טֶפַח, וְטֻמְאָה שֶׁהִיא רְצוּצָה בּוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה, לְפִיכָךְ הָיוּ בוֹנִים אוֹתוֹ עַל גַּבֵּי כִפִּין, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ יֵשׁ קֶבֶר תַּחְתֵּיהֶן הֶחָלָל מַפְסִיקוֹ. וְכָל לְשׁוֹן טֻמְאַת הַתְּהוֹם שֶׁבַּגְּמָרָא, לְשׁוֹן סָפֵק וּמְכֻסֶּה הוּא, כַּתְּהוֹם הַזֶּה שֶׁאֵינוֹ גָלוּי:


בנויות על גבי הסלע – The flinty rock that was known that was there from the beginning of the Creation of the world. But nevertheless, they were stringent to make a cavity underneath it, because of the grave in the depth, lest there is underneath it a grave and there no tent for the corpse that is a handbreadth hollow, and the defilement that is pressed ritual impurity (i.e., a corpse or part of a corpse located beneath a roof or a covering imparts ritual impurity only to objects beneath that covering – if there is a handbreadth of space between the corpse and the roof or covering) and it burst forth and rises, therefore, they would build it on top of arches, for even if there is a grave underneath them, the cavity interrupts it. And all the language of defilement of the depths that is in the Gemara, is the language of doubt and covered, like this depth that is not revealed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:2)


**וּמְבִיאִים נָשִׁים מְעֻבָּרוֹת וְיוֹלְדוֹת שָׁם.** כָּל אֵלּוּ מַעֲלוֹת יְתֵרוֹת עָשׂוּ בָהּ כִּי הֵיכִי דְלָא לְזַלְזוֹלֵי בָהּ, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹשִׂים אוֹתָהּ בִּטְבוּל יוֹם:


ומביאים נשים מעוברות ויולדות שם - all of these are additional preferences that they made, just as so that they would not disregard/despise it, because hey would do it with a person who immersed himself that day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:3)


**וּמְגַדְּלוֹת שָׁם אֶת בְּנֵיהֶן.** עַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ בֶן שְׁמֹנֶה [שָׁנִים], וְלֹא יוֹתֵר, שֶׁלֹּא יִרְאוּ קֶרִי:


ומגדלות שם את בניהן – until they would be eight [years] old, but not more so that they don’t see a nocturnal emission.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:4)


**וּמְבִיאִים שְׁוָרִים.** שֶׁכְּרֵסָן רְחָבָה כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ רַגְלָיו שֶׁל תִּינוֹק הַיּוֹשֵׁב עֲלֵיהֶן מַאֲהִילוֹת עַל הָאָרֶץ מִפְּנֵי קֶבֶר הַתְּהוֹם:


ומביאים שוורים – whose stomachs are wide in order that the feet of the young children who sit upon them overshadow over the land because of the grave of the depths.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:5)


**וְעַל גַּבֵּיהֶן דְּלָתוֹת.** וְעוֹד זֹאת מַעֲלָה יְתֵרָה כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ רַגְלָיו שֶׁל תִּינוֹק מַאֲהִילוֹת עַל הָאָרֶץ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:6)


**וְכוֹסוֹת שֶׁל אֶבֶן בְּיָדָם.** שֶׁכָּל מַעֲשֵׂה הַפָּרָה בִּכְלֵי גְלָלִים וּכְלֵי אֲבָנִים וּכְלֵי אֲדָמָה שֶׁאֵין מְקַבְּלִין טֻמְאָה:


וכוסות של אבן בידם – for all of the act of the “heifer” are in vessels of untrimmed stone and vessels of stone and vessels of earth that are not susceptible to receive defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:7)


**יָרְדוּ.** תִּינוֹקוֹת הַלָּלוּ לְתוֹךְ נְהַר שִׁלּוֹחַ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:8)


**וּמִלְאוּם.** מִמֶּנּוּ מַיִם לְקַדֵּשׁ מֵי חַטָּאת שֶׁמַּזִּין עַל הַשּׂוֹרֵף אֶת הַפָּרָה כָּל שִׁבְעָה. וּבְתוֹךְ הַנָּהָר לֵיכָּא לְסַפּוֹקֵי לְקֶבֶר הַתְּהוֹם, דְּלָא עֲבִידֵי דְקָבְרֵי בַנְּהָרוֹת:


ומלאו – [and they filled] from it water to sanctify the waters of the sin offering that they sprinkle on the [Kohen] who burns the heifer all seven [days]. But within the river there is no [reason] to doubt about the grave of the depths, for they don’t make graves in the rivers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:2:9)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר.** מֵעַל גַּבֵּי הַשְּׁוָרִים הָיוּ מְשַׁלְשְׁלִים חֶבֶל וּמְמַלְּאִים מַיִם, וְלֹא הָיוּ יוֹרְדִים לְתוֹךְ הַשִּׁלּוֹחַ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


רבי יוסי אומר – from on top of the oxen they would lover a rope and fill up [the cups] with water, but they would not descend into [the waters of] the Siloam. And the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:3:1)


**בָּאוּ לְהַר הַבַּיִת.** הַתִּינוֹקוֹת שֶׁעַל גַּבֵּי הַשְּׁוָרִים עִם הַכּוֹסוֹת מְלֵאִים מַיִם:


באו להר הבית – the young children that are upon the bulls with the cups filled with water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:3:2)


**בְּפֶתַח הָעֲזָרָה.** שֶׁל נָשִׁים, הַסְּמוּכָה לַחֵיל:


בפתח העזרה – of the Women’s Court, that is near the Hel (a place within the fortification of the Temple; see Tractate Middot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 5).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:3:3)


**הָיָה מְתֻקָּן קָלָל שֶׁל חַטָּאת.** כְּלִי שֶׁמֻּנָּח בּוֹ אֵפֶר חַטָּאת מִכָּל פָּרָה שֶׁהָיוּ שׂוֹרְפִים, כְּדִכְתִיב (במדבר יט) וְהָיְתָה לַעֲדַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמִשְׁמֶרֶת:


היה מתוקן קלל של חטאת (and there set up a flask/pitcher containing the ashes of the red cow) – a vessel that was placed in it the ashes of the sin-offering from each cow that they would burn, as it is written (Numbers 19:9): “[A man who is clean shall gather up the ashes of the cow and deposit them outside the camp in a clean place,] to be kept for water of lustration for the Israelite community.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:3:4)


**קָלָל.** כַּד קָטָן. תַּרְגּוּם וְכַדָּהּ עַל שִׁכְמָהּ (בראשית כד) וְקוּלְתַּהּ עַל כַּתְפַּהּ:


קלל – a small pitcher. It is the [Aramaic] translation of (Genesis 24:15): “[He had scarcely finished speaking, when Rebekah, who was born to Bethuel, the son of Milcah, the wife of Abraham’s brother Nahor], came out with her jar on her shoulder,” – and her jar/small pitcher on her shoulder.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:3:5)


**וְזוֹרְקוֹ לְתוֹךְ הַקָּלָל.** וּמִתּוֹךְ כָּךְ כְּשֶׁהָיָה הַזָּכָר נִרְתָּע לַאֲחוֹרָיו וְהָיָה הַמַּקֵּל יוֹצֵא מִן הַקָּלָל קוֹלֵט מִן הָאֵפֶר עִמּוֹ וְנִשְׁפָּךְ עַל גַּבֵּי הַשְּׁוָרִים וְהַתִּינוֹק נוֹטְלוֹ וְאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְהוֹצִיא יָדוֹ לַחוּץ. וּבַתּוֹסֶפְתָּא מַשְׁמַע שֶׁלֹּא הֻצְרְכוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת כֵּן אֶלָּא כְשֶׁעָלוּ מִן הַגּוֹלָה שֶׁהָיוּ כֻּלָּם טְמֵאִין וְאֵין יְכוֹלִים לִגַּע, שֶׁאִם הָיוּ נוֹגְעִים בְּאֵפֶר הַפָּרָה הָיוּ מְטַמְּאִין אוֹתוֹ. וּמַה שֶּׁהָיוּ זוֹרְקִים הַמַּקֵּל לְתוֹךְ הַקָּלָל וְלֹא בְדֶרֶךְ הַנָּחָה, שֶׁלֹּא יִטַּמָּא הָאֵפֶר בְּהֶסֵּט מִשּׁוּם סָפֵק זִיבָה:


וזורקו תוך הקלל – and through this, the male sheep is startled and moves backward and the stick/staff goes out from the small pitcher/jar and absorbs/retains from the ashes with it and it spills on top of the bulls and the child takes it and doesn’t need to remove his hand outward. But in the Tosefta (Tractate Parah, Chapter 3, Halakha 5) it implies that they didn’t need to do this, but rather, when they came up [back to the Land of Israel] from the Diaspora (i.e., Babylonia) when all of them were ritually impure, and they were not able to touch it, for if they would touch the ashes of the heifer, they would defile it. And that they would throw the stick/staff into the small pitcher and not through placing it in there, so that they would not defile the ashes through movement/היסט because of doubt of a woman with flux [with them] (i.e., a manner in which ritual impurity may be imparted – that whenever a person with gonorrhea or a menstruating woman or a woman with a lux or a woman after childbirth – causes the object to move from its place, even though he/she does not actually come into contact with it – e.g., by means of a lever – the object becomes ritually impure).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:3:6)


**אַל תִּתְּנוּ מָקוֹם לַצְּדוֹקִים לִרְדּוֹת.** לִמְשֹׁל בָּנוּ בְּדִבְרֵי שְׂחוֹק וְהִתּוּל, דְּסָבַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי כְּשֶׁעָלוּ בְנֵי הַגּוֹלָה הָיוּ בָהֶם טְהוֹרִים שֶׁטִּהֲרוּם בְּאֵפֶר הַפָּרָה שֶׁהוֹרִידוּ עִמָּהֶם לְבָבֶל וְהֶעֱלוּ מִמֶּנּוּ עִמָּם, וְלֹא נִטְמְאוּ מִשּׁוּם אֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים דְּלֹא גָזְרוּ טֻמְאָה עַל אֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים אֶלָּא לְאַחַר שֶׁעָלוּ מִן הַגּוֹלָה. לְפִיכָךְ הָיָה אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁאִישׁ טָהוֹר יִהְיֶה נוֹטֵל אֶת אֵפֶר הַפָּרָה, וְלֹא הֻצְרְכוּ לְזָכָר שֶׁל רְחֵלִים. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


אל תתנו במקום לצדוקים לרדות – to rule over us with their wors of laughter and derision. For Rabbi Yossi holds that when the people from the Diaspora (i.e., Babylonia) came up [to the Land of Israel], there were among them that were ritual pure that they had purified with the ashes of the heifer/cow that they brought down with them to Babylonia and carried up with them, and they were not defiled because of the Land of the Nations (i.e., any place outside of the Land of Israel), they did not decree defilement on the Lands of he Nations until after they ascended from the Diaspora. Therefore, it was possible that a pure person would take the ashes of the heifer, and they would not have a need for a male sheep, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:4:1)


**לֹא הָיוּ עוֹשִׂים חַטָּאת עַל גַּבֵּי חַטָּאת.** אִם טִהֲרוּ עַצְמָן לְפָרַת חַטָּאת אַחַת וְאֵרְעָהּ פְּסוּל, לֹא יַעֲשׂוּ אַחֶרֶת עַל גַּבֵּי זוֹ. אֶלָּא צְרִיכָה שִׁמּוּר כְּבַתְּחִלָּה:


לא היו עושים חטאת על גבי חטאת – if they had purified themselves with the one sin-offering of a cow and it became invalid, they should not do another on top of this one. But rather, it requires guarding as at the outset.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:4:2)


**וְלֹא תִינוֹק עַל גַּבֵּי תִינוֹק.** שֶׁאִם הָיוּ שְׁנֵי תִינוֹקוֹת, אֶחָד נִשְׁמָר לְפָרַת חַטָּאת וְאֶחָד נִשְׁמָר סְתָם אַגַּב זֶה הַתִּינוֹק אֲבָל לֹא לְצֹרֶךְ פָּרַת חַטָּאת, לֹא מִתְכַּשַּׁר הַאי אַגַּב הַאי:


ולא תינוק על גבי תינוק – for if there were two young children, one is protected for the heifer of a sin-offering and the other is merely protected on account of this child, but not for the need of a heifer sin-offering, he one does not adapt oneself with this one on account of that one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:4:3)


**צְרִיכִים הָיוּ תִינוֹקוֹת לְהַזּוֹת.** וְאַף עַל גַּב שֶׁנִּשְׁמְרוּ כָל הַשְּׁמִירוֹת הַלָּלוּ, חָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא אַיְתֵּי עוֹרֵב כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת וּשְׁדָא עֲלַיְהוּ. וְכָל הָנֵי מַעֲלוֹת יְתֵרוֹת נִינְהוּ:


צריכים היו תינוקות להזות – and even though all of these being guarded were protected, we are concerned that perhaps a raven would bring an olive’s bulk from a corpse and cast it upon them, and all of these are greater gradations/preferences.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:4:4)


**לֹא הָיוּ צְרִיכִים לְהַזּוֹת.** דִּלְטֻמְאַת מֵת לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן. אֲבָל טְבִילָה הָיוּ צְרִיכִים לְכֻלֵּי עָלְמָא, שֶׁמָּא נִטְמְאוּ בְשֶׁרֶץ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


לא היו צריכים להזות – for we are not concerned about defilement through contact with a corpse, but ritual immersion would be required for everyone, lest hey were defiled by an unclean reptile or moving creature/שרץ. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiba.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:5:1)


**לֹא מָצְאוּ מִשֶּׁבַע.** מִשּׁוּם דַּאֲמַרַן דְּמִכָּל חַטָּאוֹת שֶׁהָיוּ שָׁם מַזִּין עָלָיו, מִשּׁוּם הָכִי קָאָמַר דְּאִם לֹא מָצְאוּ מִשֶּׁבַע, מַזִּין אֲפִלּוּ מֵאֶחָת:


לא מצאו משבע – because as we stated that from all the sin-offerings that they sprinkle upon him, because of this it is stated, that if they did not find [the residue of ash] from seven [cows of purification], they sprinkle even from one [cow of purification].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:5:2)


**וְחָמֵשׁ מֵעֶזְרָא וָאֵילָךְ.** רַבִּי מֵאִיר לֹא מָנֵי הָאַרְבַּע שֶׁעָשׂוּ שִׁמְעוֹן הַצַּדִּיק וְיוֹחָנָן כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל אֶלָּא לִשְׁתַּיִם, מִשּׁוּם דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ שֶׁעַל יְדֵי פְסוּל שֶׁאֵרַע בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה נַעֲשֵׂית הַשְּׁנִיָּה:


וחמש מעזרא ואילך – Rabbi Meir did not count the four that were made by Shimon HaTzaddik/The Righeous and Yohanan the High Priest but rather for two, because he holds that as a result of invalidation that occurred at first, the second was made.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:5:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים שֶׁבַע מֵעֶזְרָא וָאֵילָךְ.** דִּסְבִירָא לְהוּ שֶׁכֻּלָּן כְּשֵׁרוֹת הָיוּ:


וחכ"א שבע מעזרא ואילך – for they hold that all of them were fit/appropriate.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:6:1)


**וְכִפָּה כְנֶגֶד הָאֹטֶם.** רַגְלֵי הַכִּפָּה שֶׁהַכִּפָּה נִשְׁעֶנֶת עֲלֵיהֶם נִקְרָאִין אֹטֶם. וְאֵין רַגְלֵי הַכִּפָּה הָעֶלְיוֹנָה כְּנֶגֶד רַגְלֵי הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה, אֶלָּא כְנֶגֶד הַכִּפָּה עַצְמָהּ, דִּכְנֶגֶד אֹטֶם הָעֶלְיוֹנָה עוֹמֵד חֲלַל הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה:


וכיפה כנגד האוטם (and an arch corresponding to the foundation/substructure -filled with earth) – the feet of the arch that the arch is supported on them is called an אוטם/foundation. But the legs of the upper arch do not correspond with the legs of the lower [arch] but rather correspond to the arch itself, that opposite the upper foundation stands the lower cavity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:6:2)


**מִפְּנֵי קֶבֶר הַתְּהוֹם.** שֶׁלֹּא תִהְיֶה שָׁם טֻמְאָה רְצוּצָה שֶׁהִיא בוֹקַעַת וְעוֹלָה:


מפני קבר התהום – so that there would not be there pressed ritual impurity which bursts forth and rises.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:6:3)


**וּפָרָה וְכָל מְסַעֲדֶיהָ.** סוֹמְכֶיהָ וְעוֹזְרֶיהָ. לְשׁוֹן ה'‏ יִסְעָדֶנּוּ עַל עֶרֶשׂ דְּוָי (תהלים מא). וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּפָרָה אֵינָהּ מְקַבֶּלֶת טֻמְאָה מֵחַיִּים, מַעֲלָה יְתֵרָה עָשׂוּ בָהּ:


ופרה וכל מסעדיה – those who support and aid it. It is the language of (Psalms 41:4): “The LORD will sustain him on his sickbed.” But even though the heifer is not susceptible to receive defilement while alive, they made a greater preference with it.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:7:1)


**וְלֹא אֲדֻמָּה.** לֹא הָיוּ מוֹצִיאִין פָּרָה אֲדֻמָּה אַחֶרֶת עִמָּהּ בַּכֶּבֶשׁ:


ולא אדומה – they would not remove another red cow/heifer with her on the ramp/causeway.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:7:2)


**שֶׁלֹּא יֹאמְרוּ שְׁתַּיִם שָׁחֲטוּ.** וְרַחֲמָנָא אָמַר וְשָׁחַט אֹתָהּ, וְלֹא אוֹתָהּ וְאֶת חֲבֶרְתָּהּ:


שלא יאמרו שתים שחטו – for the All-Merciful said (Numbers 19:3): “[You shall give] it (i.e., one cow) [to Eleazar the priest. It shall be taken outside the camp [and slaughtered in his presence],” but not her and her fellow [cow].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:7:3)


**וּבֵית הַטְּבִילָה הָיָה שָׁם.** וְאוֹתוֹ בֵית הַטְּבִילָה נַמִּי חָלוּל הָיָה עַל גַּבֵּי כִפִּין מִפְּנֵי קֶבֶר הַתְּהוֹם. וְאַף מְקוֹם מַעֲרַכְתָּהּ וְגִתָּהּ הַשְּׁנוּיִין לְקַמָּן, כֻּלָּן עַל גַּבֵּי כִפִּין הָיוּ:


ובית טבילה היה שם – and that same house for immersion also was hollow on top of arches because of the grave in the depth (i.e., a covered-up uncleanness discovered). But even the place of its pile of wood on the altar of the Temple and its pit that are taught further on (see Mishnayot 9 and 10 of this chapter), all of them were on top of arches.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:7:4)


**וּמְטַמְּאִים הָיוּ אֶת הַכֹּהֵן.** בְּמַה שֶּׁהָיוּ סוֹמְכִים יְדֵיהֶם עָלָיו כְּדִתְנַן לְקַמָּן, בְּכָךְ הָיָה מִטַּמֵּא, דְּבִגְדֵי אוֹכְלֵי קֹדֶשׁ מִדְרָס לְפָרַת חַטָּאת. וְהָרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ, שֶׁהָיוּ מְטַמְּאִין אוֹתוֹ בְשֶׁרֶץ אוֹ בִשְׁאָר טֻמְאוֹת שֶׁמְּטַמְּאִים טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב:


ומטמאים היו את הכהן – in that they would place their hands upon him, as it is taught in the Mishnah further on (see Mishnah 8 of this chapter), through this he would become defiled, for the clothes of those who consume Holy Things are unclean through treading/leaning against to the heifer of a sin-offering. But Maimonides explained that they would defile him with a creeping thing or with other defiling things that defile with ritual impurity until the evening.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:7:5)


**בִּמְעֹרְבֵי שֶׁמֶשׁ הָיְתָה נַעֲשֵׂית.** שֶׁהָיוּ דוֹרְשִׁים וְאָסַף אִישׁ טָהוֹר, אֵין טָהוֹר אֶלָּא שֶׁהֶעֱרִיב שִׁמְשׁוֹ, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר (ויקרא כב) וּבָא הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ וְטָהֵר. וַחֲכָמִים קִבְּלוּ דְּטָהוֹר הָאָמוּר בְּפָרָשַׁת חַטָּאת, טָהוֹר לְמַעֲשֵׂר קָאָמַר, דְּהַיְנוּ טְבוּל יוֹם:


במעורבי שמש היתה נעשית – that they (i.e., the Sadducees) would expound (Numbers 19:9): “A man who is pure shall gather up [the ashes of the cow and deposit them outside the camp in a pure place, to be kept for water of lustration for the Israelite community],” there isn’t someone pure other than someone whose sun has set, and similarly it states (Leviticus 22:7): “As soon as the sun sets, he shall be pure, [and afterward he may eat of the sacred donations , for they are his food].” But the Sages received the teaching that [the word] “pure” that is stated in the portion of the sin-offering (I.e., Numbers 19:9, see above) it is stated is pure for tithes, which is a טבול יום/one who immersed himself that day.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:1)


**אִישִׁי כֹהֵן גָּדוֹל.** לְפִי שֶׁפְּעָמִים נַעֲשֵׂית עַל יְדֵי כֹהֵן גָּדוֹל. אֲבָל בְּכָל כֹּהֵן הִיא כְשֵׁרָה, בֵּין בְּכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל, בֵּין בִּסְגָן בֵּין בְּהֶדְיוֹט:


אישי כהן גדול- because that sometimes it is done through the High Priest, but any Kohen is fit/appropriate for it, whether with the Kohen Gadol whether with a Deputy High Priest or whether with a common Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:2)


**וְנִסְתַּפֵּג.** וְנִתְקַנַּח:


ונסתפק (dried himself off) – and wiped himself off.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:3)


**עֵצִים.** לַעֲשׂוֹת מֵהֶן מַעֲרָכָה לַשְּׂרֵפָה, מְסֻדָּרִים שָׁם:


עצים – to make from them a pile of wood on the altar in the Temple for burning. They were set up/laid out there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:4)


**וָאֳרָנִים.** לְשׁוֹן נָטַע אֹרֶן וְגֶשֶׁם יְגַדֵּל הָאָמוּר בִּישַׁעְיָה, וּמִין אֶרֶז הוּא, וְכֵן בְּרוֹשִׁים. וְכָל הָנָךְ עֵצִים דְּקָחֲשִׁיב, עֲדִיפֵי מִשְּׁאָר עֵצִים לְצֹרֶךְ שְׂרֵפָתָהּ:


וארנים (pines, cedar) – it is the language of “Or plants firs, and the rain makes them grow” that is stated in Isaiah (44:14), and it is a species of cedar, and also cypress. And all of these trees are considered preferable from the rest of the trees for the needs of their burning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:5)


**כְּמִין מִגְדָּל.** רְחָבָה מִלְּמַטָּה וְצָרָה מִלְמַעְלָה:


כמין מגדל – wide from the bottom and narrow from the top.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:6)


**פּוֹתְחִין בָּהּ חַלּוֹנוֹת.** לְהַכְנִיס בָּהֶן הָאֵשׁ לְהַבְעִיר הָעֵצִים:


פותחין בהן חלונות – to bring in through them the fire to kindle the wood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:8:7)


**וַחֲזִיתָהּ מַעֲרָבָה.** פְּנֵי הַמַּעֲרָכָה וּמַרְאִיתָהּ, וְהוּא הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁמַּאֲחִיזִין בּוֹ אֶת הָאוּר, הָיָה פוֹנֶה לְצַד מַעֲרָב דְּהַיְנוּ כְּלַפֵּי הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, שֶׁהַמִּקְדָּשׁ הָיָה לְמַעֲרָבוֹ שֶׁל הַר הַזֵּיתִים:


וחזיתה מערבה – the face of the pile of wood on the altar in the Temple and its appearance, and it is the place that ignites the fire with kindling wood, he would face to the western side, that is, towards the Temple, as the Temple was to west of the Mount of Olives.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:1)


**בְּחֶבֶל שֶׁל מֶגֶג.** חֶבֶל עָשׂוּי מִמִּין שֶׁל גֶּמִי, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כָל מַעֲשֶׂיהָ בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה:


בחבל של מגג (rope made of a species of reed) – rope made from a species of reed-grass. In order that all of it actions in the matter will be that it is not susceptible to receive defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:2)


**וּפָנֶיהָ לַמַּעֲרָב.** לְצַד הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


ופניה למערב – to the side of the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:3)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר בִּימִינוֹ הָיָה מְקַבֵּל.** כִּשְׁאָר קַבָּלַת הַדָּם שֶׁל קָדָשִׁים שֶׁאִם קִבֵּל בִּשְׂמֹאל פָּסוּל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה, שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵין דָּמָהּ נִזְרָק עַל גַּבֵּי הַמִּזְבֵּחַ אֵינָהּ כִּשְׁאָר קָדָשִׁים לְעִנְיָן זֶה:


ר' יהודה אומר בימינו היה מקבל – like the rest of the reception of the blood of Holy Things that if he received it in his left [hand], it is invalid. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda, for since its blood is not sprinkled on the Altar it is not like the other Holy Things in this matter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:4)


**וּמַזֶּה בִימִינוֹ.** דִּכְתִיב [בְּמִדְבָּר יט] וְלָקַח אֶלְעָזָר הַכֹּהֵן מִדָּמָהּ בְּאֶצְבָּעוֹ. הָרְגִילָה שֶׁבָּאֶצְבָּעוֹת, כְּלוֹמַר אֶצְבַּע הַיָּד הַיְמָנִית שֶׁאָדָם מְמַשְׁמֵשׁ בָּהּ תָּמִיד:


ונזה בימינו – as it is written (Numbers 19:4): “Eleazar the priest shall take some of its blood with his finger [and sprinkle it seven times toward the front of the Tent of Meeting],” the usual that is of fingers, meaning to say, the finger of the right hand that a person handles with it always.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:5)


**כְּנֶגֶד בֵּית קֹדֶשׁ הַקֳּדָשִׁים.** כְּדִכְתִיב (במדבר יט) וְהִזָּה אֶל נֹכַח פְּנֵי אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד:


כנגד בית קודש הקדשים – as it is written (Numbers 19:4): “and sprinkle It [seven times] towards the front of the Tent of Meeting.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:6)


**עַל כָּל הַזָּיָה טְבִילָה.** עַל כָּל הַזָּיָה שֶׁהָיָה מַזֶּה הָיָה טוֹבֵל אֶצְבָּעוֹ בַּדָּם, דִּשְׁיָרֵי הַדָּם שֶׁבָּאֶצְבַּע פְּסוּלִין לְהַזָּיָה:


על כל הזייה טבילה – on each sprinkling that he would make, he would immerse his finger in the blood, that the remainder of the blood that is on his finger is invalid for sprinkling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:7)


**גָּמַר מִלְּהַזּוֹת קִנַּח יָדוֹ בְּגוּפָהּ שֶׁל פָּרָה.** כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּשָּׂרֵף הַדָּם עִמָּהּ, כְּדִכְתִיב [שָׁם] וְשָׂרַף אֶת הַפָּרָה אֶת עֹרָהּ וְאֶת בְּשָׂרָהּ וְאֶת דָּמָהּ. וּבֵין הַזָּאָה לְהַזָּאָה מְקַנֵּחַ אֶצְבָּעוֹ בִשְׂפַת הַמִּזְרָק וְלֹא בְגוּפָהּ שֶׁל פָּרָה, מִשּׁוּם נִימִין הַנִּדְבָּקִין בְּאֶצְבָּעוֹ דִּגְנַאי לַעֲשׂוֹת בָּהֶן הַזָּיָה:


גמר מלהזות קינח ידו בגופה של פרה – in order that he would burn the blood with It, as it is written (Numbers 19:5): “The cow shall be burned in his sight – its hide, flesh, and blood shall be burned,” and between sprinkling to sprinkling, he would wipe his finger in the rim of the bowl out of which the sprinkling is done, but not in the body of the cow, because of cords/string that were attached on his finger that it is a disgrace to do sprinkling with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:8)


**אֲלִיתוֹת.** עֵצִים קְטַנִּים שֶׁמַּלְהִיבִין בָּהֶם עֲצֵי הַמַּעֲרָכָה. אֲלִיתוֹת, לְשׁוֹן אַלְיָה. כְּמוֹ זַנְבוֹת הָאוּדִים [יְשַׁעְיָה ז]:


אליתות (wood of the fig-tree) - small pieces of wood that they kindle the pile of wood on the altar. [The word] אליתות is the language of fat-tail. Like (Isaiah 7:4): “[Do not be afraid and do not lose heart on account of those two] smoking stubs of firebrands.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:9:9)


**בַּחֲרִיּוֹת.** זְמוֹרוֹת שֶׁל דֶּקֶל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


בחריות (dried branches, twigs used for fuel) – vine-shoots of palm-trees. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Akiba.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:10:1)


**נִבְקְעָה.** הַפָּרָה מֵחֲמַת הָאוּר. אֲבָל אִם לֹא נִבְקְעָה לֹא, דִּכְתִיב [בְּמִדְבָּר יט] אֶל תּוֹךְ שְׂרֵפַת, לֹא קֹדֶם שֶׁיֻּצַּת הָאוּר בְּרֻבָּהּ, וְלֹא אַחַר שֶׁתֵּעָשֶׂה אֵפֶר. וְהוּא הַדִּין אִם קְרָעָהּ בְּסַכִּין אַחַר שֶׁיֻּצַּת הָאוּר בְּרֻבָּהּ:


נבקעת – the cow/heifer [burst open] on account of the fire. But if it didn’t burst open, he doesn’t [take the cedar wood, hyssop and scarlet wool], as it is written (Numbers 19:6): “into the fire consuming the cow,” but not prior to the fire is attached to most of it, but not after it becomes ash. And the same law applies if he tore it with a knife after the fire became attached to most of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:10:2)


**וְעָמַד.** הַכֹּהֵן:


ועמד – the Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:10:3)


**חוּץ מִגִּתָּהּ.** גֻּמָּא שֶׁהָיְתָה בַקַּרְקַע כְּמִין גַּת עֲשׂוּיָה לִשְׂרֵפַת הַפָּרָה, וּכְשֶׁהָיָה מַשְׁלִיךְ עֵץ אֶרֶז וְאֵזוֹב וּשְׁנִי תוֹלַעַת צָרִיךְ שֶׁיַּעֲמֹד חוּץ לְאוֹתָהּ גֻּמָּא:


חוץ מגיתה – a hole that was in the ground like a vat/marked off space for the burning of the cow/heifer, and when he would cast the cedar woo, hyssop and scarlet wool, he needs to stand outside that particular hole.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:10:4)


**עֵץ אֶרֶז.** אֲגֻדָּה שֶׁיֶּשׁ בָּהּ עֵץ אֶרֶז וְאֵזוֹב כְּרוּכִים עִם שְׁנִי תוֹלַעַת:


עץ ארז – a bundle that there is in it cedar wood and hyssop tied up with scarlet wool.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:10:5)


**עֵץ אֶרֶז זֶה.** לְפִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ מִינִים הַרְבֵּה שֶׁדּוֹמִין לְאֶרֶז וְאֵינָן כְּשֵׁרִין, לְכָךְ צָרִיךְ לְהוֹדִיעַ שֶׁזֶּהוּ שֶׁצִּוְּתָה עָלָיו תּוֹרָה. וְכֵן בְּאֵזוֹב. וְכֵן בִּשְׁנִי תוֹלַעַת. וְאֵזוֹב הוּא שֶׁקּוֹרִין לוֹ בָעֲרָבִי צעת"ר. וּשְׁנִי תוֹלַעַת הוּא צֶמֶר צָבוּעַ בְּצֶבַע הַנִּקְרָא בָעֲרָבִי כרמ"ז וּבְלַעַ"ז קרמז"י:


עץ ארז זה - because there are many species that are similar to cedar and are not kosher/fit, therefore, one must announce that this what the Torah commanded about. And similarly with the hyssop. And similarly with the scarlet wool. And hyssop is what they call in Arabic ZATAR. And scarlet wool is wool that is dyed with color that is called in Aramac KARMAZ and in the foreign language KARMAZI.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:1)


**כְּרָכָן.** לְעֵץ אֶרֶז וְאֵזוֹב בִּשְׁיָרֵי לָשׁוֹן שֶׁל צֶמֶר שֶׁהוּא צָבוּעַ בִּשְׁנִי תוֹלַעַת שֶׁהָיְתָה אֲרֻכָּה, וּבְמַה שֶּׁהָיָה עוֹדֵף מִמֶּנָּה עַל עֵץ הָאֶרֶז וְאֵזוֹב הָיָה כוֹרֵךְ שְׁלָשְׁתָּן יַחַד, כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא לָהֶן כֹּבֶד וְיִפְּלוּ לְתוֹךְ שְׂרֵפַת הַפָּרָה וְלֹא תִקְלֹט הַשַּׁלְהֶבֶת אֶת הַלָּשׁוֹן. וְאִם לֹא כְרָכָן וְהִשְׁלִיךְ כָּל אֶחָד בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ, כְּשֵׁרָה:


כרכן – to the cedar wood and hyssop with remnants of the strip of wool that was dyed in the scarlet wool which was long, and with what remained from it on the cedar wood and the hyssop, he would bind all three of them together, in order that they would have weight and would fall into burning of the cow, but not flame would not retain the strip. But if he didn’t bind them and he (i.e., the Kohen) cast each of one of them separately, it is fit/kosher.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:2)


**בִּכְבָרוֹת שֶׁל אֶבֶן.** אֲבָנִים הַמְנֻקָּבִים נְקָבִים דַּקִּים כִּכְבָרָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל:


בכברות של אבן (stone sieves) – stones that are perforated with thin holes like a sieve. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yishmael.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:3)


**שָׁחוֹר שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ אֵפֶר.** פֶּחָם שֶׁאִם יִכְתְּשׁוּ אוֹתוֹ יֵעָשֶׂה אֵפֶר:


שחור שיש בו אפר – charcoal that if they pounded/crushed it, it would make ash.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:4)


**כּוֹתְשִׁין אוֹתוֹ.** וְאִם לֹא יַחֲזֹר כְּמוֹ אֵפֶר, מַנִּיחִין אוֹתוֹ:


כותשין אותו- but if it doesn’t return like ash, we leave it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:5)


**בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ.** בֵּין שֶׁיַּחֲזֹר אֵפֶר בֵּין שֶׁלֹּא יַחֲזֹר אֵפֶר:


בין כך ובין כך – whether it comes back as ash or whether it doesn’t come back as ash.
