###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:6)


**חוֹלְקִין אוֹתוֹ לִשְׁלֹשָׁה חֲלָקִים.** לְאַחַר שֶׁכָּבְרוּ אֶת כָּל הָאֵפֶר, בֵּין אֵפֶר הַפָּרָה בֵּין אֵפֶר עֵצִים שֶׁנִּשְׂרְפוּ עִמָּהּ, חוֹלְקִין אֶת הָאֵפֶר לִשְׁלֹשָׁה חֲלָקִים:


חולקין אותו לשלשה חלקים – after they have sifted all of the ash, whether it is ash of the cow/heifer, whether it is the ash of the wood that was burned with it, we divide the ash into three parts.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:7)


**אֶחָד נִתָּן בַּחֵיל.** לְמִשְׁמֶרֶת, כְּדִכְתִיב וְהָיְתָה לַעֲדַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמִשְׁמֶרֶת:


אחד ניתן בחיל – for preventative measures, as it is written (Numbers 19:9): “[A man who is pure shall gather up the ashes of the cow and deposit them outside the camp in a pure place,] to be kept for water of lustration for the Israelite community.” (see Mishnah Middot 2:3 for the purpose of the חיל )


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:8)


**וְאֶחָד נִתָּן בְּהַר הַמִּשְׁחָה.** וּבָהּ הָיוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים מְקַדְּשִׁין:


ואחד ניתן בהר המשחה – and there the Kohanim would sanctify.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 3:11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/3:11:9)


**וְאֶחָד הָיָה מִתְחַלֵּק לְכָל הַמִּשְׁמָרוֹת.** וּמִמֶּנּוּ הָיוּ מַזִּין כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


ואחד היה מתחלק לכל המשמרות – and from it, they would sprinkle all of Israel.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:1)


**פָּרַת חַטָּאת.** שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָהּ פְּסוּלָה. דְּאָמַר קְרָא חַטָּאת הִיא, וּמִדְּקַרְיַהּ רַחֲמָנָא חַטָּאת לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁהִיא טְעוּנָה שְׁחִיטָה לִשְׁמָהּ כְּחַטָּאת:


פרת חטאת שלא לשמה פסולה (the cow for purification) – as Scripture states (Numbers 19:9): "חטאת היא"/It is for purification. And since the All-Merciful calls it “a purification,” we learned that it requires slaughter for its own sake like a sin-offering (see also Tractate Zevahim, Chapter 1, Mishnayot 1 and 4) – that a sin offering that was slaughtered not for its own sake or any of the other actions concerning it were performed not for its own sake, it is invalid).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:2)


**וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מַכְשִׁיר.** הוֹאִיל וְהִיא נִשְׁחֶטֶת בַּחוּץ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ורבי אליעזר מכשיר- since it is slaughtered outside the Sanctuary. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:3)


**וְשֶׁלֹּא רְחוּץ יָדַיִם וְרַגְלַיִם פְּסוּלָה.** דְּחַטָּאת קַרְיַהּ רַחֲמָנָא. וּלְהָכִי נָקַט רְחוּץ יָדַיִם וְרַגְלַיִם וְלֹא תָנָא קִדֵּשׁ, דְּלֹא שְׁנָא קִדֵּשׁ בִּפְנִים אוֹ בַחוּץ, בֵּין בִּכְלִי שָׁרֵת בֵּין בִּכְלִי חֹל יוֹצֵא:


ושלא רחוץ ידים ורגלים פסולה – for since it is called a "חטאת"/for purification (Numbers 19:9) by the All-Merciful, and for this it (i.e., the Mishnah) takes the term "רחוץ ידים ורגלים"/whose hand and feet were washed, and it (i.e., the Mishnah) does not teach "קידש"/sanctified, for it does not teach sanctified, inside or outside, whether with vessels dedicated to the Temple service or non-sacred vessels, he fulfills his religious obligation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:4)


**וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מַכְשִׁיר.** דִּבְבֹאָם אֶל אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד וְגוֹ'‏ אוֹ בְגִשְׁתָּם אֶל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ יִרְחֲצוּ כְּתִיב. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ור' אליעזר מכשיר – for it is written (Exodus 30:20-21): that “when they enter the Tent Meeting [they shall wash with water, [that they may not die;] or when they approach the altar to serve…they shall wash their hands and feet, [that they may not die]. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:5)


**שֶׁלֹּא בְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל פְּסוּלָה.** דִּכְתִיב וּנְתַתֶּם אֹתָהּ אֶל אֶלְעָזָר, אוֹתָהּ אֶל אֶלְעָזָר, וְאֵין שְׁאָר הַפָּרוֹת נַעֲשׂוֹת אֶלָּא בְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל:


שלא בכהן גדול פסולה – as it is written (Numbers 19:3): “You shall give it to Elzezar the priest,” it (the cow/heifer) to Eleazar, but the rest of the cows/heifers are not used, other than by the Kohen Gadol/High Priest.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:6)


**וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַכְשִׁיר.** דְּדָרֵישׁ, אוֹתָהּ בִּסְגָן, וּשְׁאָר כָּל הַפָּרוֹת בֵּין בְּכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל בֵּין בְּכֹהֵן הֶדְיוֹט. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ור' אליעזר מכשיר- and the rest of all the cows, wither by the High Priest or whether by a common priest. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:1:7)


**וּבְבִגְדֵי לָבָן הִיא נַעֲשֵׂית.** דִּכְתִיב גַּבֵּי פָרָה וְהָיְתָה לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְלַגֵּר הַגָּר בְּתוֹכָם לְחֻקַּת עוֹלָם, וּבְיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים כְּתִיב (ויקרא טז) וְהָיְתָה זֹּאת לָכֶם לְחֻקַּת עוֹלָם, מָה עֲבוֹדַת יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים בְּבִגְדֵי לָבָן, אַף מַעֲשֵׂה פָרָה בְּבִגְדֵי לָבָן:


ובבגדי לבן הוא נעשית- that it is written regarding the cow/heifer (Numbers 19:10): “This shall be a permanent law for the Israelites and the strangers who reside among you,” and regarding Yom Kippur it is written (Leviticus 16:34): “This shall be to you a law for all time,” just as the Divine Service of Yom Kippur is with white clothes, so also is the action of the Cow/Heifer with white clothes.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:2:1)


**חוּץ מִגִּתָּהּ.** מָקוֹם הָיוּ עוֹשִׂים לִשְׂרֵפָתָהּ כְּמִין גַּת כְּנֶגֶד פִּתְחוֹ שֶׁל הֵיכָל, וְאִם שְׁחָטָהּ שֶׁלֹּא כְנֶגֶד פִּתְחוֹ שֶׁל הֵיכָל, פְּסוּלָה:


חוץ מגיתה – the place that they make for its burning, like a species of a marked off place (i.e., selected for this purpose) which is corresponding to the opening of the hall containing the Golden Altar, but if he slaughtered it where it was not corresponding to the opening of the hall containing the Golden Altar, it is invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:2:2)


**אוֹ בִשְׁתֵּי גִתּוֹת.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁחִלְּקָהּ לִשְׁתַּיִם וְשָׂרַף חֶצְיָהּ בְּגַת זוֹ וְחֶצְיָהּ בְּגַת זוֹ:


או בשתי גיתות – as for example, that he divided it into two and burned half of it in this marked off place and half of it in that marked off place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:2:3)


**אוֹ שֶׁשָּׂרַף שְׁתַּיִם בְּגַת אַחַת.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוֹצִיא כָל אַחַת בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָהּ מִפַּסְלָא מֵחֲמַת מְלָאכָה, שֶׁזּוֹ פּוֹסֶלֶת אֶת זוֹ. אֲבָל בְּזוֹ אַחַר זוֹ, כְּשֵׁרָה:


או ששרף שתים בגת אחת – even though he removed each one on its own/separately we invalidate them because of the labor, that this one invalidates the other one. But with that one after this one, it is fit/kosher.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:2:4)


**וְלֹא כִוֵּן כְּנֶגֶד הַפֶּתַח פְּסוּלָה.** דְּאֶל נֹכַח פְּנֵי אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד כְּתִיב:


ולא כיון כנגד הפתח פסולה (and he did not aim at the door – of the Holy of Holies – it is invalid) – for it is written (Numbers 19:4): “[Eleazar the priest shall take some of its blood with his finger] and sprinkle it [seven times] toward the front of the Tent of Meeting.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:2:5)


**מִשִּׁשִּׁית שְׁבִיעִית.** דְּשֶׁבַע פְּעָמִים כְּתִיב, כְּסִדְרָן, וְהוּא מָנָה שְׁבִיעִית קֹדֶם שִׁשִּׁית, וְחָזַר וְהִזָּה שְׁבִיעִית, פְּסוּלָה, דְּבָעִינַן שֶׁיִּקְרָא לַשִּׁשִּׁית שִׁשִּׁית וְלַשְּׁבִיעִית שְׁבִיעִית. אֲבָל מִשְּׁבִיעִית שְׁמִינִית, כְּשֵׁרָה, דְּאֵין שְׁמִינִית בְּהַזָּאוֹת שֶׁל פָּרָה וְזֶה טָעָה בְּדִבּוּר דְּעָלְמָא:


מששית שביעית – for seven times (Numbers 19:4) is written in their natural order, but he counted the seventh prior to the sixth, and went back and sprinkled for the seventh [time], it is invalid, for we require that he would call for the sixth, “the sixth,” and for the seventh, “the seventh.” But from the seventh for the eighth, it is fit/kosher, for there isn’t an eighth [time] in sprinklings of [the blood] of the cow/heifer, and this one erred in mere speech.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:2:6)


**וְחָזַר וְהִזָּה שְׁמִינִית.** לָאו דַּוְקָא, דִּבְכָל עִנְיָן כְּשֵׁרָה, בֵּין חָזַר וְהִזָּה בֵּין לֹא חָזַר וְהִזָּה. וְאַגַּב רֵישָׁא נַקְטֵיהּ. אִי נַמִּי הָא קָמַשְׁמַע לָן דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּהוֹסִיף בַּהַזָּאוֹת לֹא פָסַל, דְּמִכִּי גַמְרִינְהוּ לְשֶׁבַע הַזָּאוֹת נִגְמְרָה מִצְוָתָהּ, וְתוּ לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן לְמַה שֶּׁהוּא מוֹסִיף:


וחזר והזה שמינית – not exactly, for in each matter it is fit/kosher, whether he went back and sprinkled or whether he didn’t go back and sprinkle. But on account of/subsidiary to the first clause [of the Mishnah), it (i.e., the Mishnah) took it. Alternatively, it surely teaches us that even though he added in the sprinklings, he did not invalidate it, for when he completed the seven sprinklings, its Mitzvah was completed, and furthermore we don’t concern ourselves to what he adds.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:3:1)


**שְׂרָפָהּ שֶׁלֹּא בְעֵצִים.** שֶׁהִצִּית הָאוּר בְּגוּפָהּ שֶׁל פָּרָה:


שרפה שלא בעצים – that he kindled the fire with the body of the cow/heifer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:3:2)


**כְּשֵׁרָה.** שֶׁלֹּא נִכְתַּב בַּתּוֹרָה עֵצִים, אֶלָּא וְשָׂרַף סְתָם:


כשרה – that “wood” is not written in the Torah, but [only] "ושרף"/”and he shall burn [the cow in his sight]” (Numbers 19:5) plainly/merely.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:3:3)


**אוֹ בְכָל עֵצִים.** שֶׁלֹּא שְׂרָפָהּ בַּאֲרָזִים אֳרָנִים וּבְרוֹשִׁים וַעֲצֵי תְאֵנָה הַשְּׁנוּיִים בְּפִרְקִין דִּלְעֵיל:


או בכל עצים – that he didn’t burn it (i.e., the cow) with cedar wood, pine and cypress and wood of fig trees that are taught [in the Mishnah] in the chapter above (see Tractate Parah, Chapter 3, Mishnah 8).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:3:4)


**הִפְשִׁיטָהּ וְנִתְּחָהּ כְּשֵׁרָה.** דְּאֶת עֹרָהּ וְאֶת בְּשָׂרָהּ כְּתִיב, בֵּין בְּהֶפְשֵׁט וְנִתּוּחַ בֵּין בִּמְחֻבָּר:


הפשיטה ונתחה כשרה (if he stripped/flayed it and dissected it) – it is written (Numbers 19:5): “[The cow shall be burned in its sight -] its hide, flesh [and blood shall be burned],” whether through flaying and dissecting it or whether attached.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:3:5)


**עַל מְנָת לֶאֱכֹל מִבְּשָׂרָהּ אוֹ לִשְׁתּוֹת מִדָּמָהּ כְּשֵׁרָה.** דַּוְקָא לֶאֱכֹל וְלִשְׁתּוֹת הוּא דְמַכְשִׁיר תַּנָּא קַמָּא, דִּבְכָל הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת נַמִּי כִּי הַאי גַּוְנָא כָּשֵׁר, כְּדִתְנַן בְּפֶרֶק כָּל הַפְּסוּלִים [זְבָחִים לה] חָשַׁב לֶאֱכֹל דָּבָר שֶׁאֵין דַּרְכּוֹ לֶאֱכֹל, כָּשֵׁר, כְּגוֹן מֵאֲכִילַת מִזְבֵּחַ לְאָדָם. אֲבָל חָשַׁב עַל מְנָת לְהַזּוֹת מִדָּמָהּ לְמָחָר, פָּסַל תַּנָּא קַמָּא, דְּחַטָּאת קַרְיַהּ רַחֲמָנָא. וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר סָבַר דְּאֵין מַחֲשָׁבָה פוֹסֶלֶת בַּפָּרָה כְּלָל, אֲפִלּוּ בַהַזָּאוֹת. וְאָזְדָא לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דִּבְשֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָהּ מַכְשִׁיר בְּרֵישׁ פִּרְקִין, שֶׁאֵין לָהּ דִּין מִדִּינֵי קָדָשִׁים, הוֹאִיל וְהִיא נַעֲשֵׂית בַּחוּץ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


על מנת לאכול מבשרה או לשתות מדמה כשרה – and specifically to eat and to drink that the first Tanna/teacher validates it, that with all of the sacrifices also in that manner it is kosher/fit, as it is taught in the Mishnah in the [Third] Chapter “All the Invalid”/כל הפסולים (Mishnah 3, [folio 35a], he thought about eating something that he generally doesn’t eat, it is kosher/fit, as for example, from the food of the Altar for a person, but if he thought on the condition to sprinkle from its blood on the morrow, the first Tanna/teacher invalidates, for the All-Merciful calls it a “sin-offering.” But Rabbi Eliezer holds that thought does not invalidate with regard to a cow at all, even with sprinklings. But he goes according to his reasoning that what is done not for its own sake he validates at the beginning of our chapter (see Chapter 4, Mishnah 1), which has no law from the laws of Holy Things, since it was performed outside [the Temple walls]. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:1)


**כָּל הָעֲסוּקִין בַּפָּרָה.** אֲדֻמָּה, בְּאַחַת מִכָּל עֲבוֹדוֹתֶיהָ, בֵּין בַּתְּחִלָּה בֵּין בָּאֶמְצַע בֵּין בַּסּוֹף:


כל העסוקין בפרה – the red [cow] in one of its acts of Divine Service, whether at the beginning, whether at the middle whether at the end.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:2)


**מְטַמְּאִין בְּגָדִים.** דִּכְתִיב וְהִשְׁלִיךְ אֶל תּוֹךְ שְׂרֵפַת הַפָּרָה וּסְמִיךְ לֵיהּ וְכִבֶּס בְּגָדָיו הַכֹּהֵן, וּבִשְׂרֵפָתָהּ כְּתִיב וְהַשּׂרֵף אֹתָהּ יְכַבֵּס בְּגָדָיו, וְאִם הַמַּשְׁלִיךְ אֶת הָאֵזוֹב מְטַמֵּא בְגָדִים, הַשּׂוֹרֵף לֹא כָל שֶׁכֵּן, אֶלָּא בָא הַכָּתוּב וְלִמֶּדְךָ עַל כָּל הָעוֹסְקִים בַּפָּרָה מִתְּחִלָּה וְעַד סוֹף שֶׁיִּהְיוּ טְעוּנִים כִּבּוּס בְּגָדִים:


מטמאין בגדים – as it is written (Numbers 19:6): “[And the priest shall take cedar wood, hyssop and crimson stuff, [and throw them into the fire consuming the cow,” and near/adjacent to it (Numbers 19:7): “The priest shall wash his garments,” but in its burning, it is written (Numbers 19:8): “He who performed the burning shall also wash his garments,” but the person who person who throws the hyssop defiles clothing, doesn’t the person who does all the more so [defiles his clothing]? But rather, the verse comes and teaches us on all who engage with the cow/heifer from the beginning to the end require the washing of clothing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:3)


**וּפוֹסְלִים אוֹתָהּ בִּמְלָאכָה.** דִּכְתִיב וְשָׁחַט אֹתָהּ, וְלֹא שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה מְלָאכָה אַחֶרֶת עִמָּהּ בִּשְׁעַת שְׁחִיטָתָהּ. וְכֵן בִּשְׂרֵפָתָהּ כְּתִיב וְשָׂרַף אֶת הַפָּרָה, וְלֹא שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה מְלָאכָה אַחֶרֶת עִם שְׂרֵפָתָהּ, לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁמְּלָאכָה פּוֹסֶלֶת בָּהּ מִשְּׁעַת שְׁחִיטָה עַד שֶׁתֵּעָשֶׂה אֵפֶר:


ופוסלין אותה במלאכה – as it is written (Numbers 19:3): “[It shall be taken outside the camp] and slaughtered [in his presence,” but not that another labor should be done with it at the time of is slaughtered. And similarly with regard to its slaughter it is written (Numbers 19:5): “[The cow shall be burned – in his sight], but not that another labor should be done with its burning, we learned that the labor invalidates it from the time of its slaughter until it becomes ashes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:4)


**אֵרַע בָּהּ פְּסוּל בִּשְׁחִיטָתָהּ.** לֹא שֶׁנִּתְנַבְּלָה בַשְּׁחִיטָה, דְּאִם כֵּן מְטַמְּאָה בְגָדִים מִשּׁוּם נְבֵלָה, אֶלָּא פְסוּל מֵחֲמַת מְלָאכָה, דּוּמְיָא דְאֵרַע בָּהּ פְּסוּל בְּהַזָּאָתָהּ:


אירע בה פסול בשחיטתה – not that it became ritually forbidden by unskillful slaughtering, for if so, it defiles clothing because of being an animal not slaughtered according to the ritual rules, but rather it is invalid on account of the labor, similar that if an invalidation occurred to it through its sprinkling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:5)


**אֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה בְגָדִים.** דְּכֵיוָן דִּשְׁחִיטָתָהּ בִּפְסוּל, אִם כֵּן לֹא נַעֲשָׂה בָהּ עֲדַיִן לְשֵׁם פָּרָה כְּלוּם בְּכַשְׁרוּת. אֲבָל אֵרַע בָּהּ פְּסוּל בַּהַזָּאָה, כְּבָר נַעֲשֵׂית הַשְּׁחִיטָה בְּהֶכְשֵׁר:


אינה מטמאה בגדים – for since that its slaughter was with an invalidation, if so, nothing was yet done to it for the sake of the heifer’s fitness, but if an invalidation happened to it while sprinkling, the slaughtered had already taken place in fitness/being kosher.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:6)


**לְעוֹלָם מוֹעֲלִין בָּהּ.** הַנֶּהֱנֶה מִמֶּנָּה בֵּין לִפְנֵי שְׂרֵפָתָהּ בֵּין לְאַחַר שְׂרֵפָתָהּ, חַיָּב קָרְבַּן מְעִילָה, עַד שֶׁתֵּעָשֶׂה אֵפֶר. אֲבָל לְאַחַר שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית אֵפֶר, אֵין בָּאֵפֶר מִשּׁוּם מְעִילָה, דִּכְתִיב חַטָּאת הִיא. חַטָּאת, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁמּוֹעֲלִין בָּהּ. הִיא, בָּהּ מוֹעֲלִין, בְּאֶפְרָהּ אֵין מוֹעֲלִין:


לעולם מועלין בה – a person who benefits from it whether prior to its burning whether after its burning, is liable for the sacrifice of unlawful use of sacred property, until the ashes are made, but after the ashes are made, there is nothing in the ashes coming as a result of unlawful use of sacred property, as it is written (Numbers 19:9): “it is for purification”/"חטאת היא".


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:7)


**וּמַרְבִּין לָהּ עֵצִים.** נַמִּי דַּוְקָא עַד שֶׁתֵּעָשֶׂה אֵפֶר:


ומרבין לה עצים- also especially until the ashes are made.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:8)


**וּמַעֲשֶׂיהָ בַיּוֹם.** בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא מְפָרֵשׁ, חוּץ מֵאֲסִיפַת אֶפְרָהּ וְהַמִּלּוּי וְהַקִּדּוּשׁ. שֶׁיְּכוֹלִים לֶאֱסֹף אֵפֶר הַפָּרָה בַּלַּיְלָה, וְלִשְׁאֹב הַמַּיִם מִן הַבְּאֵר וְהוּא הַנִּקְרָא מִלּוּי, וְלָשִׂים בְּתוֹכָהּ הָאֵפֶר וְהוּא הַנִּקְרָא קִדּוּשׁ, כָּל זֶה מֻתָּר לַעֲשׂוֹת בַּלַּיְלָה. וְכָל מַעֲשֵׂה פָרָה, בְּכֹהֵן, חוּץ מֵאֲסִיפַת אֶפְרָהּ וְהַמִּלּוּי וְהַקִּדּוּשׁ וְהַהַזָּאָה, שֶׁהֵם כְּשֵׁרִים בְּזָר. אֲסִיפַת אֶפְרָהּ, דִּכְתִיב וְאָסַף אִישׁ טָהוֹר, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁאֲסִיפַת הָאֵפֶר כְּשֵׁרָה בְכָל אָדָם. וּבַהַזָּאָה הוּא אוֹמֵר וְהִזָּה הַטָּהֹר עַל הַטָּמֵא, בֵּין שֶׁהוּא כֹהֵן בֵּין שֶׁאֵינוֹ כֹהֵן:


ומעשיה ביום – In the Tosefta (Tractate Parah, Chapter 4, Halakha 11) it explains, except for the gathering of its ashes and drawing [of water] and the mixing [of the ashes and the water], that they are able to gather the ashes of the cow/heifer at night, and to draw the water from the well and this is called the drawing of water/מילוי, and the putting into the ashes and this is called קידוש/mixing of the ashes in the waters of lustration, and all of this is permitted to be done at night. And all the actions concerning the cow/heifer are with a Kohen [only], except for the gathering of the ashes, and the drawing of the water and the mixing of the ashes in the waters of lustration and the sprinkling, which are appropriate for a non-Kohen/foreigner. The gathering of the ashes, as it is written (Numbers 19:9): “A man who is pure [shall gather up the ashes of the cow],” teaches that the gathering of the ashes is appropriate for any man. And [regarding] sprinkling, it states (Numbers 19:19): “The pure person shall sprinkle it upon the impure person [on the third day and on the seventh day],” whether he is a Kohen or whether he is not a Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:9)


**וְהַמְּלָאכָה פוֹסֶלֶת בַּמַּיִם.** דִּכְתִיב וְהָיְתָה לַעֲדַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמִשְׁמֶרֶת לְמֵי נִדָּה [חַטָּאת הִיא], מַה חַטָּאת נִפְסֶלֶת בִּמְלָאכָה כִּדְכָתְבִינַן לְעֵיל, כָּךְ הַמַּיִם נִפְסָלִים בִּמְלָאכָה מִשְּׁעַת הַמִּלּוּי:


והמלאכה פוסלת במים – as it is written (Numbers 19:9): “to be kept for water of lustration for the Israelite community. [It is for purification],” just as the sin-offering is invalidated with work [done by those involved in the ritual], as we have written above, so also the waters are invalidated through work [done by those involved in the ritual] from the time of the drawing of the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 4:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/4:4:10)


**עַד שֶׁיַּטִּילוּ אֶת הָאֵפֶר.** שֶׁמַּטִּילִים בַּמַּיִם מֵאֵפֶר הַפָּרָה כְּשִׁעוּר שֶׁתֵּרָאֶה הָאֵפֶר בַּמַּיִם וּמֵהֶם מַזִּין עַל הַטָּמֵא, וְאִם לְאַחַר שֶׁהִטִּילוּ הָאֵפֶר בַּמַּיִם עָשָׂה מְלָאכָה אַחֶרֶת עִמָּהּ בִּשְׁעַת הַזָּאָה, לֹא פָסַל:


עד שיטילו את האפר – that they cast in the water from the ashes of the cow/heifer according to the measure that the ashes will be seen in the water and from them he sprinkles upon the impure individual, but if after they cast the ashes into the water he performed another task at the time of the sprinkling, he did not invalidate it.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:1:1)


**הַמֵּבִיא כְּלִי חֶרֶס לַחַטָּאת.** שֶׁהוֹלֵךְ אֵצֶל הַיּוֹצֵר לִקְנוֹת מִמֶּנּוּ כְּלִי חֶרֶס לְהַנִּיחַ בּוֹ אֵפֶר חַטָּאת אוֹ מֵי חַטָּאת:


המביא כלי חרס לחטאת – that he goes near the potter to buy from him an earthenware vessel to place in it the ashes of the sin-offering or the water of the sin-offering/lustration.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:1:2)


**טוֹבֵל וְלָן עַל הַכִּבְשָׁן.** מַתְחִיל לְשָׁמְרָן בְּטָהֳרָה שֶׁלֹּא יִגַּע בָּהֶן עַם הָאָרֶץ מִקֹּדֶם שֶׁיְּצָרְפוֹ בַכִּבְשָׁן, דְּהוּא גְמַר מְלַאכְתָּן לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה:


טובל ולן על הכבשן – he begins to guard them in purity so that someone who doesn’t observe certain religious customs regarding tithes and Levitical cleanness (i.e., עם הארץ) wouldn’t touch them prior to their smelting them in a kiln/furnace, which is the conclusion of their labor to be susceptible to receive defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:1:3)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אַף מִן הַבַּיִת הוּא מֵבִיא וְכָשֵׁר.** דִּמְהֵימְנִינַן לֵיהּ לְעַם הָאָרֶץ אִם אוֹמֵר שֶׁשְּׁמָרוֹ עַל טָהֳרַת חַטָּאת, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּפֶרֶק חֹמֶר בַּקֹּדֶשׁ דְּעַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ נֶאֱמָנִים עַל טָהֳרַת יַיִן לִנְסָכִים וְשֶׁמֶן לִמְנָחוֹת, שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא כָל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד בּוֹנֶה בָמָה לְעַצְמוֹ וְשׂוֹרֵף פָּרָה אֲדֻמָּה לְעַצְמוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ר' יהודה אומר אף מן הבית הוא מביא וכשר – we believe an person who doesn’t observe certain religious customs regarding tithes and Levitical cleanness if he says that he guarded it on the purity of a sin-offering, as we stated in the Chapter “A More Stringent Rule Applies to Holy Things”/"חומר בקודש" (Tractate Hagigah 22a), that people who don’t observe certain religious customs regarding tithes and Levitical uncleanness (עמי הארץ) are believed on the ritual purity of wine for libations and oil for meal-offerings, so that each and every person would not build a platform for himself and burn the Red Cow/Heifer for himself. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:1:4)


**וּבַתְּרוּמָה.** אִם מֵבִיא כְלִי חֶרֶס לְהַנִּיחַ בּוֹ תְּרוּמָה:


ובתרומה – if he brings an earthenware vessel to place heave-offering in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:1:5)


**פּוֹתֵחַ אֶת הַכִּבְשָׁן וְנוֹטֵל.** אַף עַל גַּב שֶׁנִּגְמַר צֵרוּפָן וְלֹא שְׁמָרָן. דְּכֵיוָן דַּעֲדַיִן לֹא נִפְתַּח וּמָלֵא אָבָק עַל גַּבָּיו, לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא נָגַע בּוֹ עַם הָאָרֶץ, דְּלֹא הֶחְמִירוּ בַתְּרוּמָה:


פותח את הכבשן וטוטל – even though the its smelting was completed but not their guarding, that since [the oven] was not yet opened and is filled with dust on top of it, we are not concerned that perhaps a person who doesn’t observe certain religious customs regarding tithes and Levitical uncleanness touched it, for they were not stringent regarding heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:1:6)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר מִסֵּדֶר הַשֵּׁנִי.** אִם פָּתַח עַם הָאָרֶץ אֶת הַכִּבְשָׁן וְנָטַל מִמֶּנּוּ כֵּלִים, אֵין נוֹטֵל מִסֵּדֶר הָרִאשׁוֹן, דְּיַד הַכֹּל מְמַשְׁמְשִׁין בּוֹ, אֲבָל מִסֵּדֶר הַשֵּׁנִי שֶׁתַּחַת הָרִאשׁוֹן שָׁרֵי. וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי מַחְמִיר וְאָסַר אַף מִסֵּדֶר הַשֵּׁנִי. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה לֹא כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן וְלֹא כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי, אֶלָּא אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמָּצָא הַכִּבְשָׁן פָּתוּחַ הֲרֵי הַכֹּל בְּחֶזְקַת טָהֳרָה, וְלוֹקֵחַ אַף מִסֵּדֶר הָרִאשׁוֹן:


Rabbi Shimon states: “From the second row”/מן הסדר השני. If a person who doesn’t observe certain religious customs regarding tithes and Levitical uncleanness/עם הארץ open the kiln and took vessels from it, he doesn’t take from the first row, for the hand of all handles them, but from the second row which is underneath the first row, he is permitted. But Rabbi Yossi is stringent and forbade even from the second row. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon nor according to Rabbi Yossi, but even though he found the kiln/furnace open, everything is in the presumption of ritual purity, and he can take even from the first row.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:2:1)


**הַמַּטְבִּיל כְּלִי לְחַטָּאת.** כְּלִי שֶׁטֶף שֶׁהִטְבִּילוֹ כְּדֵי לְמַלְּאוֹת בּוֹ מַיִם וּלְקַדְּשָׁם בָּאֵפֶר, אִם הִטְבִּילוֹ בְמַיִם שֶׁאֵין רְאוּיִין לְקַדֵּשׁ, כְּגוֹן בְּמֵי מִקְוֶה שֶׁאֵינָם מַיִם חַיִּים:


המטביל כלי לחטאת (a person who immerses a vessel for a purification rite) – a vessel which requires only rinsing in order to be restored to Levitical cleanness that he immersed it in order to fill it with water and to mix them with ashes, if he filled it with water that is not appropriate for mixing, as for example, the water of a Mikveh that are not potable, running spring water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:2:2)


**צָרִיךְ לְנַגֵּב.** הַכְּלִי. שֶׁמָּא יִתְעָרְבוּ שֶׁאֵינָם רְאוּיִים בָּרְאוּיִין. אֲבָל אִם הִטְבִּילוֹ בְמַיִם חַיִּים הָרְאוּיִין לְקַדֵּשׁ, אֵין צָרִיךְ לְנַגֵּב:


צריך לנגב – [he needs to wipe off] the vessel, for perhaps that which was appropriate would be mixed with that which is inappropriate. But if he immersed it in potable, running spring water waters, they are appropriate for mixing, one doesn’t need to dry it off.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:2:3)


**וְאִם לֶאֱסֹף לְתוֹכוֹ מַיִם מְקֻדָּשִׁים.** וְאִם הִטְבִּילוֹ לְהַכְנִיס לְתוֹכוֹ מַיִם שֶׁכְּבָר נָתַן עֲלֵיהֶם אֶת הָאֵפֶר, אֲפִלּוּ הִטְבִּילוֹ בְמַיִם חַיִּים, צָרִיךְ לְנַגֵּב, שֶׁלֹּא יִתְעָרְבוּ שֶׁאֵינָם מְקֻדָּשִׁים בַּמְקֻדָּשִׁים, וּתְנַן לְקַמָּן צְלוֹחִית שֶׁל חַטָּאת שֶׁנָּפַל לְתוֹכָהּ מַיִם פָּסוּל:


ואם לאסוף לתוכו מים מקודשים – but if he immersed it in order to bring into water that he had already placed in it the ashes, even if he had immersed it in potable, running spring water, he needs to dry it, so that water that is not mixed becomes combined with water that is mixed, and it is taught in the Mishnah further on (Chapter 9, Mishnah 1 of Tractate Negaim) a flask of lustration that invalid water fell into it.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:3:1)


**קֵרוּיָה.** דְּלַעַת יְבֵשָׁה חֲלוּלָה שֶׁשּׁוֹאֲבִים בָּהּ מַיִם:


קרויה (pumpkin-shell used as a drawing vessel, cooler) – a dry, hollow gourd/pumpkin that absorbs water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:3:2)


**שֶׁהִטְבִּילוּהָ.** מַעֲלָה בְעָלְמָא לְחַטָּאת, וְלֹא מִשּׁוּם טֻמְאָה. דְּהָא בַחֲדָשָׁה מַיְרֵי שֶׁלֹּא נִטְמֵאת מֵעוֹלָם:


שהטבילוה – a mere preference for lustration, but not because of defilement, for we are speaking of something new that was never defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:3:3)


**בְּמַיִם שֶׁאֵין רְאוּיִין לְקַדֵּשׁ מְקַדְּשִׁים בָּהּ.** לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּגְּבָהּ. וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא בָלְעָה מַיִם שֶׁאֵין רְאוּיִין לְקַדֵּשׁ וְתַחֲזֹר וְתִפְלֹט:


במים שאין ראויין לקדש מקדשים בה – after it was wiped off. But we don’t concern ourselves that perhaps it absorbed water that are not appropriate for mixing, and it will return and emit them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:3:4)


**נִטְמְאָה אֵין מְקַדְּשִׁים בָּהּ.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁחָזַר וְהִטְבִּילָהּ, דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא תִפְלֹט מַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִים הַבְּלוּעִים בָּהּ. דְּקָסָבְרֵי רַבָּנָן דְּמַחְמְרִינַן טְפֵי בְּמַיִם טְמֵאִים יוֹתֵר מִבְּמַיִם שֶׁאֵינָן רְאוּיִין לְקַדֵּשׁ. וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ מַשְׁוֶה מִדּוֹתָיו. דְּאִי חָיְשַׁת, הָכָא וְהָכָא תֵּחוּשׁ. וְאִי לֹא חָיְשַׁת, הָכָא וְהָכָא לֹא תֵחוּשׁ. אֶלָּא בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ, בֵּין בַּתְּחִלָּה אִם הִטְבִּילָהּ בְּמַיִם שֶׁאֵין רְאוּיִין לְקַדֵּשׁ וְנִגְּבָהּ, בֵּין בַּסּוֹף כְּשֶׁנִּטְמֵאת וְהִטְבִּילָהּ אֲפִלּוּ בִרְאוּיִין לְקַדֵּשׁ וְנִגְּבָהּ, לֹא יוֹסִיף לְתוֹכָהּ מַיִם מְקֻדָּשִׁים, דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא תִפְלֹט. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


נטמאה אין מקדשים בה – but even though he returned and immersed it, for we are concerned that perhaps it would emit impure liquids that had been absorbed in it. For the Rabbis hold that we should be stricter with impure waters more than with waters that are not appropriate for mixing. The Rabbi Yehoshua harmonized his measures. But if you are concerned, here and there you should be concerned. But if you are not concerned, here and there you should not be concerned. But rather, whether one way or the other, whether at the beginning he immersed it in water that was not appropriate for mixing and dried it off, or whether at the end when it had become defiled and he immersed it even with appropriate [waters] for mixing and dried it off, he should not add into it mixed waters, for we are concerned that perhaps he will emit them. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:4:1)


**שְׁפוֹפֶרֶת.** שֶׁל קָנֶה:


שפופרת (tube) – of reed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:4:2)


**שֶׁחֲתָכָהּ.** תִּקְּנָהּ לָשׂוּם בָּהּ מֵי חַטָּאת אוֹ אֵפֶר חַטָּאת, אֲפִלּוּ חֲתָכָהּ חָבֵר שֶׁאֵינוֹ חָשׁוּד עַל הַטֻּמְאָה:


שחתכה – he prepared it to place in it the waters of lustration or the ashes of lustration (i.e., of the sin-offering), even if a חבר/member of a group dedicated to the precise observance of the commandments who is not suspect regarding defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:4:3)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר יַטְבִּיל מִיָּד.** וְאִיכָּא הֶכֵּרָא. דְּפָרָה נַעֲשֵׂית בִּטְבוּל יוֹם, מִשּׁוּם דְּכֵלִים הַנִּגְמָרִים בְּטָהֳרָה צְרִיכִין טְבִילָה לַקֹּדֶשׁ, שֶׁמָּא נִשְׁאַר בַּכְּלִי רֹק הַיּוֹצֵא מִפִּיו שֶׁל עַם הָאָרֶץ וַעֲדַיִן הוּא לַח בִּשְׁעַת גְּמָרוֹ שֶׁל כְּלִי וּמְטַמְּאוֹ. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּטֻמְאָה זוֹ אֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה הֶעֱרֵב שֶׁמֶשׁ אֶלָּא טְבִילָה בִּלְבַד, מִכָּל מָקוֹם אִיכָּא הֶכֵּרָא, מִשּׁוּם דִּלְגַבֵּי פָרַת חַטָּאת עֲשָׂאוּהָ כִטְמֵא מֵת בַּשְּׁבִיעִי שֶׁלּוֹ וּמְטַמֵּא אָדָם וְכֵלִים, וְאִי אִיתָא דְּפָרָה הָיְתָה צְרִיכָה הֶעֱרֵב שֶׁמֶשׁ, גַּם הִיא הָיְתָה צְרִיכָה:


ר' אליעזר אומר יטביל מיד – and there is recognition, that the [rituals surrounding] the cow/heifer are performed by someone who had immersed himself that day, because vessels that have been completed in ritual purity require ritual immersion for consecrated objects, lest there remain in the vessel the spittle that comes out from the mouth of a person who is not punctilious in his observance of the laws of tithing and ritual purity (i.e., a common, uneducated person/עם הארץ), and it is moist at the time at the time when he completes the vessel and he defiles it. But even though that this defilement does not require [waiting until] sunset but only ritual immersion, nevertheless, there is recognition, because regarding the cow/heifer of lustration (i.e., the sin offering), it makes him like someone who has been defiled by a corpse on his seventh day and he defiles people and vessels, and if there is of a heifer, he would require a sunset [to pass], and it (i.e., the tube of the reed) would also require it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:4:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר יְטַמֵּא וְיַטְבִּיל.** דִּבְעִנְיָן אַחֵר לֵיכָּא הֶכֵּרָא, כֵּיוָן דְּכֵלִים הַנִּגְמָרִים בְּטָהֳרָה לֹא בָעוּ הֶעֱרֵב שֶׁמֶשׁ לַקֹּדֶשׁ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


ר' יהושע אומר יטמא ויטביל – that in another matter there isn’t any recognition, since the vessels that are completed in ritual purity do not require [the passing of] a sunset to mix them. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:4:5)


**הַכֹּל כְּשֵׁרִים לְקַדֵּשׁ.** כָּל הַטְּהוֹרִים כְּשֵׁרִים לְהַשְׁלִיךְ הָאֵפֶר עַל גַּבֵּי הַמַּיִם, וַאֲפִלּוּ נָשִׁים. דִּכְתִיב גַּבֵּי אֲסִיפַת אֵפֶר הַפָּרָה, וְאָסַף אִישׁ טָהוֹר אֵת אֵפֶר הַפָּרָה וְהִנִּיחַ מִחוּץ לַמַּחֲנֶה, וְדָרְשִׁינַן אִישׁ, לְהַכְשִׁיר אֶת הַזָּר. טָהוֹר, וַאֲפִלּוּ אִשָּׁה. וְהִנִּיחַ, מִי שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ דַעַת לְהַנִּיחַ, פְּרָט לְחֵרֵשׁ שׁוֹטֶה וְקָטָן שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶן דַּעַת לְהַנִּיחַ. וּבָתַר [אֲסִיפַת אֶפְרָהּ] כְּתִיבָא הַךְ עֲבוֹדָה דְקִדּוּשׁ, וְאֵין עֲבוֹדָה מַפְסֶקֶת בֵּינְתַיִם. לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁכָּל הַכָּשֵׁר לַאֲסִיפַת אֶפְרָהּ כָּשֵׁר לְקִדּוּשׁ:


הכל כשרים לקדש - all those who are poor are fit/appropriate to cast the ashes into the mater, and even women. As is it written concerning the collection of the ashes of the cow/heifer (Numbers 19:9): “A man/person who is pure shall gather up the ashes of the cow and deposit them outside the camp [in a pure place, to be kept for water of lustration of the Israelite community].,” and we expound [on the word] "איש"/a person or man, to permit the non-Kohen/”foreigner.” [The word] טהור/pure [means] even a woman; [the word] "והניח"/he shall deposit [implies] a person that has cognitive awareness/temperament to place it, excluding the deaf-mute, the imbecile and a minor who lack cognitive awareness. And after [the collection of its ashes], if is written that this Divine service of mixing – and there is no Divine Service that interrupts in the meanwhile. We learned that all who are fit/appropriate for the collection of its ashes are fit/appropriate for mixing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:4:6)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַכְשִׁיר בְּקָטָן וּפוֹסֵל בְּאִשָּׁה.** דְּאִם אִיתָא דְּכָל הַכָּשֵׁר בַּאֲסִיפָה כָּשֵׁר בְּקִדּוּשׁ, לִכְתּוֹב וְלָקַח לְשׁוֹן יָחִיד בַּקִּדּוּשׁ, כִּי הֵיכִי דְכָתַב וְאָסַף לְשׁוֹן יָחִיד בַּאֲסִיפָה, מַאי וְלָקְחוּ, דַּאֲפִלּוּ הָנָךְ דִּפְסוּלִים הָתָם כְּשֵׁרִים הָכָא, כְּגוֹן קָטָן. אֲבָל אִשָּׁה לֹא, דִּכְתִיב וְנָתַן, וְלֹא וְנָתְנָה:


ר' יהודה מכשיר בקטן ופוסל באשה – but if there is that everyone who is fit for the collection of [ashes] is fit for mixing, it (i.e., the Torah) should write, "ולקח"/and he takes, in the singular with regard to mixing, for just as (i.e., the Torah) wrote, "ואסף"/and he collected – in the singular for collecting [of the ashes], why does it write, "ולקחו"/and they shall take (see Numbers 19:17 – “Some of the ashes from the fire of purification shall be taken for he impure person,”/"ולקחו לטמא מעפר שרפת החטאת", that even those that are invalid there are fit/appropriate here, such as a minor. But not a woman, for it is written (Numbers 19:17): "ונתן עליו מים חיים אל-כלי"/and fresh water shall be added to them in a vessel), "ונתן"/and he shall give, and not "ונתנה"/and she gave.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:5:1)


**בְּכָל הַכֵּלִים מְקַדְּשִׁים.** בֵּין שֶׁל עֵץ בֵּין שֶׁל עֶצֶם בֵּין שֶׁל זְכוּכִית, וַאֲפִלּוּ בִכְלֵי גְלָלִים דְּלָא חֲשִׁיבֵי כְלִי לְעִנְיַן טֻמְאָה לֹא מִדִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה וְלֹא מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים, דִּכְתִיב מַיִם חַיִּים אֶל כֶּלִי וְלֹא כְתִיב אֶל הַכֶּלִי:


בכל הכלים מקדשים – whether of wood, whether of bone, whether of glass, and even with vessels of dung, that are not considered a vessel in regard to defilement, not from the words of the Torah nor from the words of the Scribes, as I is written (Numbers 19:17): “And fresh water shall be added to them in a vessel,” but it is not written, “to the vessel.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:5:2)


**מַחַץ.** כְּלִי גָדוֹל שֶׁל חֶרֶס שֶׁקּוֹרִין בַּעֲרָבִי אלמחגי"ר. וּבְקָצֶה הָאֶחָד מִמֶּנּוּ עָשׂוּי כִּדְמוּת בֵּית יָד, וְהוּא שׁוּלֵי הַמַּחַץ. כָּךְ פֵּרֵשׁ הָרַמְבַּ"ם:


מחץ (ladle) – a large earthenware vessel that they call ALMAKHDIR in Arabic. And on one end of it is made in the form of a handle and it the sides of a broken ladle. Such is how Maimonides explained it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:5:3)


**וְאֵין מַזִּין אֶלָּא בְכֶלִי.** כְּלוֹמַר, שֶׁצָּרִיךְ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ הַמַּיִם בִּכְלִי בִּשְׁעַת הַהַזָּאָה:


ואין מזין אלא בכלי – meaning to say, that the water must be in the vessel at the time of the sprinkling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:5:4)


**אֵין מַצִּילִין.** כָּל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ מִטֻּמְאַת אֹהֶל הַמֵּת עַל יְדֵי צָמִיד פָּתִיל, אֶלָּא כֵלִים:


אין מצילין – all what is inside it [is protected] from the defilement of the tent of corpse through a tightly fitting cover, except [whole] vessels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:5:5)


**שֶׁאֵין מַצִּילִין מֵאֲוִיר כְּלֵי חֶרֶס אֶלָּא כֵלִים.** שֶׁאִם יִפֹּל שֶׁרֶץ בַּאֲוִיר כְּלִי חֶרֶס, טָמֵא כָּל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נָגַע בָּהֶן, חוּץ מִכֵּלִים, שֶׁאֵין מִטַּמְּאִין מֵאֲוִיר כְּלִי חֶרֶס, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יא) כֹּל אֲשֶׁר בְּתוֹכוֹ יִטְמָא מִכָּל הָאֹכֶל אֲשֶׁר יֵאָכֵל, אֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִין מִטַּמְּאִין מֵאֲוִיר כְּלֵי חֶרֶס, וְאֵין כֵּלִים מִטַּמְּאִין מֵאֲוִיר כְּלֵי חֶרֶס:


שאין מצילין מאויר כלי חרס אלא כלים – that if a creeping reptile would fall into the airspace of an earthenware vessel, everything that is within it is impure even though it did not come in contact with them, except for utensils that are not defiled from the airspace of an earthenware vessels, as it is written (Leviticus 11:33-34): “[And if any of those falls into an earthen vessel,] everything inside it shall be impure…as to any food that may be eaten,” food and liquids become defiled from the airspace of earthenware vessels, but utensils/vessels are not defiled from the airspace of earthenware vessels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:5:6)


**שֶׁאֵין מַצִּילִין.** כְּמוֹ וְאֵין מַצִּילִין. דֻּגְמָתוֹ בְּרֵישׁ פֶּרֶק קַמָּא דְבֵיצָה, שֶׁאֵפֶר כִּירָה מוּכָן הוּא, וְאָמַר רַבָּה בַגְּמָרָא [דַּף ח] הָכִי קָאָמַר, וְאֵפֶר כִּירָה מוּכָן הוּא:


שאין מצילין – like, and they do not protect/ואין מצילין , and an example of this is at the beginning of the first chapter of [Tractate] Betza [8a] that the ashes of a portable stove on feet with caves for two pots is prepared, and Rabbah stated in the Gemara (Tractate Betza 8a), that this is what he said, that the ashes of a portable stove on feet with caves for two pots is ready/prepared.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:6:1)


**בֵּיצַת הַיּוֹצְרִים.** כָּל כְּלִי שֶׁמַּתְחִיל יוֹצֵר חֶרֶס לַעֲשׂוֹת, עוֹשֶׂה תְחִלָּה הַחֹמֶר עָגֹל כְּצוּרַת בֵּיצָה וְעוֹשֶׂה לָהּ בֵּית קִבּוּל:


ביצת היוצרים – every vessel that the potter of earthenware begins to make, he first makes the material round like the form of an egg and makes for it a receptacle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:6:2)


**כְּשֵׁרָה.** לְקַדֵּשׁ בָּהּ מֵי חַטָּאת:


כשרה – to mix in it the waters of lustration/sin-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:6:3)


**וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי פוֹסֵל.** דְּלָא חֲשִׁיב לֵיהּ כֶּלִי. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ור' יוסי פוסל – for it is not considered a vessel to him. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:6:4)


**בֵּיצַת הַתַּרְנְגֹלֶת.** קְלִפָּתָהּ הַחִיצוֹנָה:


ביצת התרנגולת – its outer shell.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:6:5)


**וַחֲכָמִים פּוֹסְלִים.** שֶׁאֵינָהּ חֲשׁוּבָה כֶלִי. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים פוסלים – for it is not considered a vessel. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:1)


**הַשֹּׁקֶת שֶׁבַּסֶּלַע.** שֹׁקֶת הִיא אֶבֶן חֲלוּלָה שֶׁעַל שְׂפַת הַמַּעְיָן, וְהַמַּיִם נִכְנָסִים לָהּ דֶּרֶךְ חוֹר שֶׁבְּדָפְנָהּ, וּמַשְׁקִין בָּהּ הַבְּהֵמוֹת. וְהָכָא מַיְרֵי בְסֶלַע מְחֻבָּר שֶׁחֲקָקוֹ, אֵין נֶחְשָׁב כְּלִי הוֹאִיל וְלֹא הָיָה עָלָיו שֵׁם כְּלִי בְּתָלוּשׁ. וַאֲפִלּוּ הָיָה תָלוּשׁ [אֶלָּא] שֶׁחִבְּרוֹ וּלְבַסּוֹף חֲקָקוֹ:


השוקת שבסלע (the trough which [is hewn] in the rock) – A שוקת/trough is a hollowed rock that is on the rim of a spring and the water enters in it through a hole that is in its wall and waters the animals. But here we are speaking of a rock that is attached that was hollowed out, it is not considered a utensil since it didn’t have the designation of a vessel when it was detached. But even that it was detached [other than] that they attached it and at the end hollowed it out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:2)


**וְאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה צָמִיד פָּתִיל.** אִם הָיְתָה בְאֹהֶל הַמֵּת מַצֶּלֶת עַל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָן בְּכִסּוּי לְבַד בְּלֹא צָמִיד פָּתִיל, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָהּ חֲשׁוּבָה כְלִי אֶלָּא כְבוֹר וְדוּת שֶׁמַּצִּילִין עַל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ בְּכִסּוּי בְּלֹא צָמִיד פָּתִיל, כְּדִתְנַן בְּפֶרֶק ה'‏ דְּאֳהָלוֹת [מִשְׁנָה ו]:


ואינה צריכה צמיד פתיל – if it was in the tent of a corpse it protects on what is within it with the covering alone without the tightly fitted lid, because it is not considered a vessel but rather like a pit or a cistern that protects over what is inside of it with a cover without a tightly fitted lid/stopper, as is taught in Chapter Five of [Tractate] Ahilot [Mishnah 6].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:3)


**וְאֵינָהּ פּוֹסֶלֶת אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה.** אִם [נָפְלוּ] מֵי גְשָׁמִים מִתּוֹכָהּ לַמִּקְוֶה, אֵין נֶחְשָׁבִים מַיִם שְׁאוּבִים לִפְסֹל אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה:


ואינה פוסלת את המקוה – if rain water [fell] from it to the Mikveh/ritual bath, they are not considered drawn water to invalidate the Mikveh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:4)


**וּפְקָקָהּ.** סְתָמָהּ:


ופקקה (inserted a stop-gap) – stopped it up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:5)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵינָם עֲגֻלִּים כֶּלִי.** כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁאֵין כְּלִי מְסַבֵּב אֶת הַמַּיִם, דְּנֶקֶב שֶׁלְּמַטָּה מְבַטְּלוֹ מִתּוֹרַת כְּלִי, דִּסְתִימָה שֶׁל סְמַרְטוּט לֹא מְשַׁוֵּי לֵיהּ כְּלִי. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁהַנֶּקֶב מִן הַצַּד, יֵשׁ תּוֹרַת כְּלִי עָלָיו וּמְקַדְּשִׁים בַּמַּיִם שֶׁבְּתוֹכָם, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַכְּלִי מְסַבְּבָן. וְהַיְנוּ עֲגֻלִּים כֶּלִי:


מפני שאינם עגולים כלי (because it is not wholly enclosed by the vessel) – meaning to say, that the vessel does not surround the water, that the perforation that is from the bottom neutralizes it from the category of a vessel, for the closing up of a rag does not make it into a vessel. But when the perforation/hole is from the side, the category of a vessel is upon it and we mix with the water that is within it, since the vessel surrounds them (i.e., the waters). And that is that the vessel is round.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:6)


**עָשָׂה לָהּ עֲטָרָה.** שֹׁקֶת תְּלוּשָׁה שֶׁחִבְּרָהּ בְּסִיד, שֶׁהִיא חֲשׁוּבָה כְלִי, וְעָשָׂה שָׂפָה שֶׁל טִיט לְפִיהָ סָבִיב וְנִתְמַלְּאָה הַשֹּׁקֶת מַיִם עַד שֶׁנִּתְמַלֵּאת גַּם הַשָּׂפָה, הַמַּיִם פְּסוּלִים לְקַדֵּשׁ, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין הַתּוֹסֶפֶת נֶחְשֶׁבֶת כְּלִי:


עשה לה עטרה (made for it a molding of mud) – a detached trough that was attached with plaster/lime, which is considered a vessel and made a rim of mud/plaster for its fmoutjh and the trough was filled with water until it also filled the rim, the waters are invalid to mix because the addition is not considered a vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:7:7)


**וְאִם הָיָה בָרִיא.** כְּלוֹמַר אִם הַשָּׂפָה הַזֹּאת חֲזָקָה וּמְהֻדֶּקֶת בַּשֹּׁקֶת עַד שֶׁאִלּוּ תִנָּטֵל הַשֹּׁקֶת תִּנָּטֵל גַּם הַשָּׂפָה עִמָּהּ, כְּשֵׁרִים:


ואם היה בריא (but if it was firm) – meaning to say, if this rim was strong and attached to the trough until that if he were to take the trough, he would also take the rim with it, they are fit/appropriate.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:8:1)


**שְׁתֵּי שְׁקָתוֹת שֶׁבְּאֶבֶן.** הָכָא מַיְרֵי בְאֶבֶן הַמִּטַּלְטֶלֶת שֶׁאֵינָהּ סֶלַע מְחֻבָּר, וּשְׁתֵּי שְׁקָתוֹת הַלָּלוּ תּוֹרַת כְּלִי יֵשׁ לָהֶן:


שתי שקתות שבאבן- here we are speaking of a stone that is carried that is not an attached rock, and these two troughs have the designation of a vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:8:2)


**קִדֵּשׁ אַחַת מֵהֶן.** שֶׁנָּתַן אֵפֶר פָּרָה בְּמַיִם שֶׁבְּאַחַת מֵהֶן:


קדש אחת מהן – that he placed ashes fo the heifer/cow in the water that is in one of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:8:3)


**כִּשְׁפוֹפֶרֶת הַנּוֹד.** כְּמִין קָנֶה שֶׁנָּתַן עַל פִּי הַנּוֹד שֶׁבּוֹ מְרִיקִים הַיַּיִן אוֹ הַמַּיִם בְּתוֹךְ הַנּוֹד. וְשִׁעוּרוֹ, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיוּ שְׁתֵּי אֶצְבָּעוֹת הָרִאשׁוֹנוֹת שֶׁל פַּס הַיָּד חוֹזְרוֹת בּוֹ בְרֶוַח, לֹא הַגּוּדָל, אֶלָּא הָאֶצְבָּעוֹת הַסְּמוּכוֹת לוֹ:


כשפופרת הנוד (tube/mouth-piece of the leather bottle) – like a kind of reed that he placed on the mouth of the leather bottle that through it he empties out the wine or the water within the bottom. And Is measure, in order that there would be the first two fingers of the palm of the hand are able to go back in through the space, not the thumb, but the fingers adjacent to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:8:4)


**אוֹ שֶׁהָיוּ מַיִם צָפִין עַל גַּבֵּיהֶן.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁכֹּתֶל הָאֶמְצָעִי הַמַּפְסִיק בֵּין שְׁתֵּי הַשְּׁקָתוֹת נָמוּךְ מִשְּׁאָר הַכְּתָלִים:


או שהיו מים צפין על גביהן – as for example that the intermediate wall interrupts between the two trough lower than the other walls.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:8:5)


**כִּקְלִפַּת הַשּׁוּם.** כְּשֶׁנִּתְמַלְּאוּ שְׁתֵּיהֶן, צָפִין הַמַּיִם עַל הַמְּחִצָּה הָאֶמְצָעִית כִּקְלִפַּת הַשּׁוּם:


כקליפת השום ([a film of water] of the thickness of a garlic peel) – that when both of them are filled, the water floats on the middle partition like the garlic peel.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:9:1)


**שֶׁהִקִּיפָן.** סְמָכָן זוֹ אֵצֶל זוֹ. לְשׁוֹן [וְאֵין] מַקִּיפִין שְׁתֵּי חָבִיּוֹת דְּמַסֶּכֶת בֵּיצָה:


הקיפן – made them adjacent/next one to the other. It is the language of two jugs/jars together [to set a pot upon] in Tractate Betzah [Chapter 4, Mishnah 5 and folio 32b].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:9:2)


**הַמַּיִם שֶׁבֵּינֵיהֶן.** שֶׁבֵּין שְׁתֵּי עֲרֵבוֹת וְשֶׁבֵּין סֶדֶק שֶׁל שֹׁקֶת. אֲבָל שְׁאָר הַמַּיִם מְקֻדָּשִׁין. אֶלָּא דְאוֹתָן שֶׁבַּסֶּדֶק לֹא נִכְנַס בָּהֶן הַקִּדּוּשׁ:


המים שביניהן – that is between two kneading troughs and that which is between the fissure of the trough. But the rest of the water is mixed, but those that are in the fissure, the mixture of water [and ashes] does not enter into them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 5:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/5:9:3)


**גִּפְסִים.** מִין סִיד הוּא וְלָבָן בְּיוֹתֵר. וְקוֹרִין לוֹ בְּלַעַ"ז ייס"ו גיפ"ס:


גיפסים – a kind of lime/plaster but it is the whitest. And we call it YISO JIPIS in the foreign language.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:1)


**הַמְקַדֵּשׁ.** מֵי חַטָּאת. הַבָּא לִתֵּן מֵאֵפֶר הַפָּרָה לְתוֹךְ הַמַּיִם. וְהוּא קִדּוּשׁ הָאָמוּר בְּכָל מְכִלְתִּין:


המקדש -the waters of lustration (i.e., sin-offering. A person who comes to place from the ashes of the heifer/cow into the water. This is the קידוש/mixing that is stated through our Tractate.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:2)


**אוֹ עַל הַצַּד.** עַל צִדּוֹ שֶׁל כֶּלִי:


או על הצד – on the side of the vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:3)


**וְאַחַר כָּךְ נָפַל לַשֹּׁקֶת.** עַל הַמַּיִם שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַשֹּׁקֶת. פְּסוּלִים אוֹתָן הַמַּיִם לְהַזָּאָה, דִּכְתִיב [בְּמִדְבָּר יט] וְלָקְחוּ וְנָתַן, עַד שֶׁיָּבֹא הָאֵפֶר בְּתוֹךְ הַמַּיִם מִכֹּחַ אָדָם:


ואחר כך נפל לשוקת – on the water that is within the trough, those waters are invalid for sprinkling, as it is written (Numbers 19:17): "ולקחו [לטמא מעפר שרת החטאת] ונתן [עליו מים חיים]/”[Some of the ashes from the fire of purification] shall be taken [for the impure person, and fresh water] shall be added to them [in a vessel].” Until the ashes would be would bring within the water from the power (i.e., direct actions) of the person.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:4)


**נָטַל מִן הַשְּׁפוֹפֶרֶת וְכִסָּה.** נָטַל אֵפֶר מִן הַשְּׁפוֹפֶרֶת וְכִסָּה הַשְּׁפוֹפֶרֶת קֹדֶם שֶׁיְּקַדֵּשׁ, דְּהַיְנוּ קֹדֶם שֶׁיִּתֵּן הָאֵפֶר בַּמַּיִם:


נטל מן השפופרת וכיסה – he took the ashes from the tube/reed and covered the tube/reed prior to his mixing it, that is prior to his putting the ashes in the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:5)


**אוֹ שֶׁהֵגִיף אֶת הַדֶּלֶת.** קֹדֶם שֶׁיְּקַדֵּשׁ:


או שהגיף את הדלת (or shut the door) – prior to his mixing [the ashes in the water].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:6)


**הַקִּדּוּשׁ כָּשֵׁר.** דְּהַיְנוּ הָאֵפֶר שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ, לְפִי שֶׁמְּלָאכָה אֵינָהּ פּוֹסֶלֶת בָּאֵפֶר, כְּדַאֲמַרַן:


הקידוש כשר – that is the ashes that are in his hands, because the work does not invalidate the ashes, as we have stated (see also the end of Chapter 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:7)


**וְהַמַּיִם פְּסוּלִים.** דְּנַעֲשֵׂית מְלָאכָה עִם הַמַּיִם, וּלְעֵיל [סוֹף] פֶּרֶק פָּרַת חַטָּאת שָׁנִינוּ שֶׁהַמְּלָאכָה פּוֹסֶלֶת בַּמַּיִם עַד שֶׁיַּטִּילוּ אֶת הָאֵפֶר:


והמים פסולים – that work was done with the water, and above [at the end] of the Chapter פרת חטאת/the cow of purification (chapter 4) we taught that the work [done by those involved in the ritual] renders the water unfit until they put the ashes into it/"והמלאכה פוסלת במים עד יטילו את האפר".


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:8)


**זְקָפָהּ בָּאָרֶץ.** לְאַחַר שֶׁנָּטַל הָאֵפֶר מִן הַשְּׁפוֹפֶרֶת הִנִּיחַ הַשְּׁפוֹפֶרֶת בָּאָרֶץ קֹדֶם שֶׁיִּתֵּן הָאֵפֶר בַּמַּיִם:


זקפה בארץ – after he took the ashes from the tube/reed, he placed the read in the ground prior to placing the ashes in the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:9)


**פָּסוּל.** הַמִּלּוּי דְּהַיְנוּ הַמַּיִם, לְפִי שֶׁעָשָׂה מְלָאכָה עִם הַמַּיִם. וְאִם הִנִּיחַ הַשְּׁפוֹפֶרֶת בְּיָדוֹ וְכָפָה יָדוֹ עָלֶיהָ כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִתְפַּזֵּר הָאֵפֶר שֶׁבְּתוֹכָהּ וְאַחַר כָּךְ נָתַן הָאֵפֶר עַל הַמַּיִם, כָּשֵׁר הַמִּלּוּי דְּהַיְנוּ הַמַּיִם, דְּאֵין זוֹ חֲשׁוּבָה מְלָאכָה:


פסול – the filling/drawing, that is the water, because he performed the task with the water. But if he placed the tube/reed in his hand and covered his hand upon it in order that the ashes don’t scatter that are within it and afterwards placed the ashes on the water, the filling/drawing, that is, the water, is not considered labor/work.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:1:10)


**לְפִי שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר.** לִשְׁמֹר הָאֵפֶר שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַשְּׁפוֹפֶרֶת אִם לֹא יָכֹף יָדוֹ עַל פִּיהָ, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְהָשִׁיב הַפְּקָק עַל פִּיהָ עַד שֶׁיְּקַדֵּשׁ. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, זְקָפָהּ בָּאָרֶץ, הִנִּיחַ הָאֵפֶר בָּאָרֶץ, פָּסוּל הָאֵפֶר, דִּכְתִיב לְמִשְׁמֶרֶת, [וּבָאָרֶץ אֵינָהּ לְמִשְׁמֶרֶת]. וְאִם נָתַן הָאֵפֶר לְתוֹךְ יָדוֹ כָּשֵׁר, דְּאֶפְשָׁר לְשָׁמְרוֹ וְשַׁפִּיר קָרִינַן בֵּיהּ לְמִשְׁמֶרֶת. וּלְפִי זֶה גָּרְסִינַן לְפִי שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר:


לפי שאי אפשר – to preserve the ashes that are within the reed/tube if he will not cover his hand on its mouth, for he is not able to restore the stop-gap/stopper on its mouth until he mixes [the ashes in the water]. Another explanation"זקפה בארץ"/he placed the ashes in the ground, the ashes are invalid, as it is written (Numbers 19:9): “[A man who is pure shall gather up the ashes of the cow and deposit them outside the camp in a pure place,] to be kept for water of lustration for the Israelite community,” [but in the ground, it is not kept/preserved]. But if he placed the ashes into his hand, it is fit/appropriate, for it is possible to protect it, and it is well that we call it [in the Torah] "למשמרת" – and for that reason, we have the reading, "לפי שאפשר"/because it is possible [to do so without distractions].



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:2:1)


**הָיָה קִדּוּשׁ צָף עַל פְּנֵי הַמַּיִם.** שֶׁנָּתַן עַל הַמַּיִם אֵפֶר הַרְבֵּה. וְלָאֵפֶר עַצְמוֹ קָרֵי קִדּוּשׁ:


היה קדוש צף על פני המים – that he placed upon the water many ashes. And the ash itself is called קידוש/putting ashes in the water of lustration.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:2:2)


**נוֹטֵל.** מֵאוֹתוֹ אֵפֶר:


נוטל – he takes from that ash.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:2:3)


**וּמְקַדֵּשׁ.** בּוֹ מַיִם אֲחֵרִים, וַאֲפִלּוּ בְאוֹתוֹ שֶׁנָּגַע בַּמַּיִם מְקַדֵּשׁ וְחוֹזֵר וּמְקַדֵּשׁ כַּמָּה פְעָמִים, אֶלָּא שֶׁמְּנַגְּבוֹ:


ומקדש – [and mixes it] in other waters, and even with that (i.e., the ashes) that touched the water, he mixes and returns and mixes several times, but he dries them off.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:2:4)


**זָלַף אֶת הַמַּיִם.** הִזָּה מִן הַמַּיִם הַמְקֻדָּשִׁים עַל הָאָדָם וְעַל הַכֵּלִים עַד שֶׁכָּלוּ כָל הַמַּיִם, וְהָאֵפֶר נִשְׁאַר בְּשׁוּלֵי הַכְּלִי:


זלף את המים (if he used all the water for sprinkling) – he sprinkled from the water that were mixed upon a person and upon vessels until all the water was completed, and the ashes remain in the rim at the bottom o fthe vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:2:5)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים וְכוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כַחֲכָמִים בְּתַרְוַיְהוּ:


וחכמים אומרים וכו' – And the Halakha is according to the Sages in both of these things (i.e., whatever ash has touched the water, they do not mix [another preparation] with it and whatever has touched he water, they do not mix with it.).



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:3:1)


**טְפִי.** פַּךְ קָטָן שֶׁפִּיו צַר וְהַמַּיִם הַיּוֹרְדִים מִמֶּנּוּ יוֹרְדִים טִיף טִיף:


טפי (vessel with a narrow neck/dripper) – a small flask/jar (with a narrow neck) whose mouth is narrow and the water that descends from it goes down drop by drop.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:3:2)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁפִּיו צַר.** פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁפוֹפֶרֶת הַנּוֹד:


אע"פ שפיו צר – less than the tube (i.e., mouthpiece) of the leather bottle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:3:3)


**הַמַּיִם שֶׁבְּתוֹכוֹ מְקֻדָּשִׁים.** וּכְשֵׁרִים לְהַזּוֹת אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִכְנַס שָׁם מֵאֵפֶר הַפָּרָה כְּלָל, דַּהֲווּ כְּאִלּוּ מְחֻבָּרִים לַמַּיִם שֶׁבַּשֹּׁקֶת:


המים שבתוכו מקודשים – and are appropriate/fit to sprinkle even though nothing came in there from the ashes of the cow/heifer at all, for they were as if they were attached to the water in the trough.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:3:4)


**הָיָה סְפוֹג.** אִם בִּשְׁעַת מִלּוּי הָיָה סְפוֹג בַּשֹּׁקֶת, הַמַּיִם הַבְּלוּעִים בַּסְּפוֹג פְּסוּלִים, דְּמַיִם חַיִּים אֶל כֶּלִי בָּעִינַן, וְאֵין הַסְּפוֹג כְּלִי:


היה ספוג – if at the time of the filling the sponge was in the trough, the water that was absorbed in the sponge are invalid, for we require “fresh water shall be added to them in a vessel” (Numbers 19:17), but the sponge is not a vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:3:5)


**יְזַלֵּף עַד שֶׁהוּא מַגִּיעַ לַסְּפוֹג.** כְּשֶׁבָּא לִטֹּל הַמַּיִם וְלִתְּנֵם בַּצְּלוֹחִית, שׁוֹפֵךְ כָּל הַמַּיִם עַד שֶׁמַּגִּיעַ לַסְּפוֹג, אֲבָל לֹא יִשְׁפֹּךְ מִן הַמַּיִם שֶׁבַּסְּפוֹג כְּלָל, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִתְעָרְבוּ הַמַּיִם שֶׁבַּסְּפוֹג שֶׁהֵם פְּסוּלִים עִם הַמַּיִם שֶׁבַּשֹּׁקֶת שֶׁהֵם כְּשֵׁרִים וְיִפָּסְלוּ כֻלָּם:


יזלף עד שהוא מגיע לספוג – when he comes to take the water and to put them into a bottle with a wide belly and a narrow neck/flash, he pours all the water until it reaches the sponge, but he should not pour from the water that is in the sponge at all, in order that the waters that are in the sponge don’t mix which are invalid with the waters that are in the trough that are fit/appropriate and all of them will become invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:3:6)


**נָגַע בַּסְּפוֹג.** נִסְחָטִים מֵי הַסְּפוֹג לְתוֹךְ הַשֹּׁקֶת וְנִתְעָרְבוּ הַפְּסוּלִים בַּכְּשֵׁרִים, וְנִפְסְלוּ כֻלָּם:


נגע בספוג – the waters of the sponge were wrung out into the trough and the invalid ones were mixed up with the fit/appropriate ones, and all of them were invalidated.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:4:1)


**נָתַן יָדוֹ אוֹ רַגְלוֹ.** הֲרֵי שֶׁהָיָה קִלּוּחַ יוֹרֵד מֵהַר מְעֻקָּם וְאֵינוֹ יוֹרֵד לְתוֹךְ הֶחָבִית שֶׁרוֹצֶה לְמַלְּאוֹתָהּ לְקִדּוּשׁ, וְנָתַן יָדוֹ לְמַעְלָה בְרֹאשׁ הָהָר לַעֲשׂוֹתָהּ כְּעֵין צִנּוֹר כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּעַבְרוּ הַמַּיִם דֶּרֶךְ יָדוֹ לֶחָבִית שֶׁל קִדּוּשׁ, וְכֵן עֲלֵי יְרָקוֹת הָרְאוּיִין לַאֲכִילָה וּמְקַבְּלִין טֻמְאָה:


נתן ידו או רגלו [under running water] – behold that the spouting forth/continuous flow descends rapidly from the curve, but it doesn’t descend into the jar when he wants to fill it for mixing [with the ashes], but if he placed his hands above at the tope of the mountain to make it like a kind of spout/duct in order that the waters would pass through his hand to the jar of mixing of waters and ashes. And similarly, the vegetable greens which are appropriate for eating and are susceptible to receiving ritual defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:4:2)


**פְּסוּלִים.** הַמַּיִם. כִּדְיָלְפִינַן בְּפֶרֶק ב'‏ דִּזְבָחִים (דף כה) מִדִּכְתִיב [וַיִּקְרָא יא] מִקְוֵה מַיִם יִהְיֶה טָהוֹר, הֲוָיָתָן עַל יְדֵי טָהֳרָה תְהֵא. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּהַאי קְרָא בְּמִקְוֶה הוּא דִכְתִיב, יָלְפִינַן מִנֵּיהּ כָּל הֲוָיוֹת. וְהָכִי הָוֵי מַשְׁמָעוּת דִּקְרָא, הֲוָיָתָן שֶׁל כָּל מַיִם הַמְטַהֲרִים, עַל יְדֵי טָהֳרָה תְהֵא, בֵּין שֶׁאַתָּה מְהַוֶּה אוֹתָן לִהְיוֹת מִקְוֶה אוֹ מִלּוּי הֲוֵי מְהַוֶּה אוֹתָן עַל יְדֵי דָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה:


פסולים – the waters [are invalid]. As we derived in the second chapter of [Tractate] Zevakhim (folio 25b) from what is written (Leviticus 11:36): “[However, a spring or cistern] in which water is collected shall be pure, [but whoever touches such a carcass in it shall be impure],” they will be through purity (i.e., in their original status), and even though that this Scriptural verse is written concerning a Mikveh/ritual bath, we derive from it all the possibilities. And this is the implication of the Scriptural verse, that will be with all the waters that purify, that through purity it will be, whether one brings them to become a Mikveh or the filling of water that will be through something that is not susceptible to receive ritual defilement.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:5:1)


**הַמְפַנֶּה אֶת הַמַּעְיָן לְתוֹךְ הַגַּת.** שֶׁעָשָׂה חָרִיץ מִן הַמַּעְיָן עַד הַגַּת כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּפְנוּ שָׁם מֵי הַמַּעְיָן, וְאַחַר כָּךְ הִפְסִיקָן:


המפנה את המעין לתוך הגת (he diverts/empties the spring into the wine vat) – that he made a trench from the spring until the wine vat in order that the he could divert there the waters of the spring, and afterwards stop them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:5:2)


**גַּת.** בּוֹר שֶׁעוֹשִׂין לִפְנֵי הַגַּת כְּדֵי לְקַבֵּל אֶת הַיַּיִן, קָרוּי גַּת:


גת – a cistern that they make in front of the wine vat to receive the wine which is called a גת/marked off space for serving as a wine vat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:5:3)


**גֵּבִים.** לְשׁוֹן עָשֹׂה הַנַּחַל הַזֶּה גֵּבִים הָאָמוּר בְּסֵפֶר מְלָכִים [ב ג]. וְהוּא רְקָק שֶׁל מַיִם:


גבים (cavity for collecting water, cistern) – it is the language of “This wadi shall be full of pools,” that is stated in the [Second] Book of Kings [Chapter 3, Verse 16]. And it is a pool/diluvium of water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:5:4)


**פְּסוּלִים.** לַזָּבִים לִטְבִילָה, דְּלָאו מַיִם חַיִּים נִינְהוּ:


פסולים- for those with gonorrhea for ritual immersion, for they are not fresh water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:5:5)


**וְלַמְצֹרָעִים.** דְבָעִינַן מַיִם חַיִּים לְהַזּוֹת עָלָיו עִם דַּם הַצִּפּוֹר:


ולמצורעים – that we require fresh water to sprinkle upon him with the blood of the bird.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 6:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/6:5:6)


**וּלְקַדֵּשׁ בָּהֶן מֵי חַטָּאת.** בָּעִינַן מַיִם חַיִּים, וְהָנֵי כֵּיוָן דְּהִפְסִיקָן לָאו מַיִם חַיִּים נִינְהוּ. וְהָא דְקָתָנֵי מִפְּנֵי שֶׁלֹּא נִתְמַלְּאוּ בִכְלִי, כְּדֵי לְפָסְלָן לְקִדּוּשׁ בְּכָל עִנְיָן אֲפִלּוּ בָא לְקַדֵּשׁ בְּתוֹךְ הַגַּת אוֹ בְתוֹךְ הַגֵּבִים עַצְמָן:


ולקדש מי חטאת – that we require fresh water, and these since they have been stopped up are not fresh water. And that which is taught in the Mishnah because they were not filled up by means of a utensil, in order to invalidate them for mixing in every matter even if he came to mix within the vat or within the pools of water themselves.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:1)


**חֲמִשָּׁה.** לְקַדְּשָׁן חֲמִשָּׁה קִדּוּשִׁין. כָּל חָבִית וְחָבִית בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָהּ:


חמשה. לקדשן חמשה קידושין – each and every jar on its own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:2)


**וְנִמְלְכוּ לְקַדְּשָׁן קִדּוּשׁ אֶחָד.** לְעָרוֹת חֲמִשְׁתָּן בְּתוֹךְ כְּלִי גָדוֹל וּלְקַדֵּשׁ הַכֹּל בְּבַת אַחַת:


ונמלכו לקדשן קידוש אחד – to empty five of them into one large vessel and to mix the [the water with the ashes] at once/simultaneously.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:3)


**הֲרֵי כֻלָּן כְּשֵׁרִים.** דְּהוֹאִיל וְכָל אֶחָד מְמַלֵּא הַכְּלִי שֶׁלּוֹ אֵין כָּאן מְלָאכָה פוֹסֶלֶת בַּמַּיִם בֵּין מִלּוּי לְהַטָּלַת הָאֵפֶר בַּמַּיִם:


הרי כולן כשרים – for since each pone fills his vessel there isn’t here labor invalidating in water whether in filling/drawing water to casting the ashes in the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:4)


**הַיָּחִיד שֶׁמִּלֵּא חָמֵשׁ חָבִיּוֹת כוּ'‏ אֵין כָּשֵׁר אֶלָּא אַחֲרוֹן.** שֶׁהַמִּלּוּי הָרִאשׁוֹן נִפְסַל בַּמְּלָאכָה שֶׁעָשָׂה כְּשֶׁמִּלֵּא אֶת הַשֵּׁנִי קֹדֶם שֶׁנָּתַן אֶת הָאֵפֶר בָּרִאשׁוֹן, וְכֵן הַשֵּׁנִי נִפְסַל כְּשֶׁמִּלֵּא אֶת הַשְּׁלִישִׁי, וְכֵן כֻּלָּם. וְאִם מִתְּחִלָּה הָיָה מְמַלֵּא חָמֵשׁ חָבִיּוֹת זוֹ אַחַר זוֹ אוֹ אֲפִלּוּ מֵאָה חָבִיּוֹת כְּדֵי לְקַדְּשָׁן קִדּוּשׁ אֶחָד, כְּגוֹן שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לְהַזּוֹת עַל כֵּלִים הַרְבֵּה, לֹא הָיוּ פוֹסְלִים זֶה עַל זֶה וְכֻלָּן כְּשֵׁרִים, דְּהַכֹּל נֶחְשָׁבִים מִלּוּי אֶחָד. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁמִּלֵּא מִתְּחִלָּה לְשֵׁם חֲמִשָּׁה קִדּוּשִׁין, כֻּלָּן פּוֹסְלִין זֶה עַל זֶה מִטַּעַם מְלָאכָה, שֶׁהִיא פוֹסֶלֶת בַּמַּיִם עַד שֶׁיַּטִּיל הָאֵפֶר, הִלְכָּךְ אֵין כָּשֵׁר אֶלָּא אַחֲרוֹן:


היחיד שמליא חמש חביות כו' – nothing is fit/appropriate other than the last. For the first filling is invalidated with the work that he did when he filled the second [jar] prior to putting the ashes in the first [jar], and similarly, the second [filling] is invalidated when he fills the third [jar], and similarly all of them. But if from the outset he would fill five jars one after another or even one-[hundred jars in order to mix ashes and water in one mixing, as for example, that he needs to sprinkle upon many vessels, they would not invalidate each other and all of them are fit/appropriate, for all are considered one filling/drawing water. But when he filled from the outset for the sake of five mixings, all of them invalidate each other for reason of the labor, for it invalidates in the water until he casts in the ashes, therefore, nothing is fit/appropriate other than the last.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:5)


**וְנִמְלַךְ לְקַדְּשָׁן חֲמִשָּׁה קִדּוּשִׁין.** שֶׁקִּדֵּשׁ זוֹ לְעַצְמָהּ וְזוֹ לְעַצְמָהּ:


ונמלך לקדשן חמשה קדושין – that he mixed this one on its own and that one on its own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:6)


**אֵין כָּשֵׁר אֶלָּא זוֹ שֶׁקִּדֵּשׁ רִאשׁוֹן.** לְפִי שֶׁקִּדּוּשׁ רִאשׁוֹן חֲשִׁיב מְלָאכָה לָאֲחֵרִים שֶׁהָיוּ מְמֻלָּאִים וְלֹא נָתַן לְתוֹכָן אֵפֶר, דְּעַד שֶׁיִּתֵּן אֶת הָאֵפֶר, מְלָאכָה פּוֹסֶלֶת בַּמַּיִם:


אין כשר אלא זו שקידש ראשון – for the first mixing [of ashes and water] is considered labor for the others that would be filled ‘with water] but he did not place the ashes into them, for until he places the ashes, the labor invalidates in the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:7)


**אָמַר לְאֶחָד קַדֵּשׁ לְךָ אֶת אֵלּוּ.** הַיָּחִיד שֶׁמִּלֵּא חָמֵשׁ חָבִיּוֹת לְקַדְּשָׁן קִדּוּשׁ אֶחָד, וְאָמַר לְאַחֵר קַדֵּשׁ לָךְ אֶת אֵלּוּ וְקִדְּשָׁן הָאַחֵר חֲמִשָּׁה קִדּוּשִׁים, אֵין כָּשֵׁר אֶלָּא רִאשׁוֹן. דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ קַדֵּשׁ לָךְ וּנְתָנָן לוֹ, נַעֲשׂוּ שֶׁלּוֹ וּכְאִלּוּ מִלְּאָן הוּא, וְהַקִּדּוּשׁ שֶׁקִּדֵּשׁ רִאשׁוֹן נַעֲשָׂה מְלָאכָה לִפְסֹל אֶת אֲחֵרִים:


אמר לאחד קדש לך את אלו – the individual that filled five jars to perform one mixing [of ashes and water], and he said to another person: “Mix for yourself these,” and he mixed [ashes and water] for five mixings, nothing is fit/appropriate other than the first, for since he said to him: “Mix for yourself and he gave them (i.e., the ashes and the water) to him, they became his and it is as if he filled them, and the mixing [of ashes and water] that he first did became [extrinsic] work to invalidate the others.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:1:8)


**אֲבָל אִם אָמַר קַדֵּשׁ לִי אֶת אֵלּוּ, כֻּלָּן כְּשֵׁרִים.** שֶׁהַמְמַלֵּא לֹא עָשָׂה מְלָאכָה, וְהַמְקַדֵּשׁ אֵינוֹ שֶׁלּוֹ כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּפָּסְלוּ עַל יָדוֹ:


אבל אם אמר קדש לי את אלו כולן כשרים – that the person who fills [the jars] did not do work, and the person who mixes [ashes and water] that is not his in order that they will become invalidated by [the actions of] his hand.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:2:1
