[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:2:1)


**וְעָשָׂה מְלָאכָה בְּיָדוֹ אַחַת.** פָּסוּל הַמִּלּוּי מִטַּעַם מְלָאכָה:


ועשה מלאכה בידו אחת – the filling/drawing water is invalid for reason of the [extrinsic] work.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:2:2)


**לוֹ וּלְאַחֵר** כוּ'‏ כְּאַחַת:


לו ולאחר – (etc. at the same time).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:2:3)


**שְׁנֵיהֶם פְּסוּלִים.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּלִתְרֵי גַבְרֵי קָא מְמַלֵּא, נֶחְשָׁב הָאֶחָד מְלָאכָה לַחֲבֵרוֹ:


שניהם פסולים – for since that for two people he is filling water, it is considered that one does work for his fellow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:2:4)


**בֵּין לוֹ בֵּין לְאַחֵר.** דְּאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין הַמַּיִם שֶׁלּוֹ, פּוֹסְלָן בַּמְּלָאכָה שֶׁבִּשְׁעַת מִלּוּי:


בין לו בין לאחר – that even though the water is not his, he has invalidated them with the [extrinsic] work/labor that he did at the time of the drawing of the water.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:3:1)


**אִם לוֹ פָּסוּל.** אִם הַקִּדּוּשׁ שֶׁלּוֹ, פָּסוּל. דְּשֶׁמָּא הַמְּלָאכָה קָדְמָה לַקִּדּוּשׁ וְנִפְסְלוּ הַמַּיִם:


אם לו פסול - if the mixing [of ashes and water] is his, it is invalid, for perhaps, the labor/work preceded the mixing [of the ashes and water] and the water was invalidated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:3:2)


**וְאִם לְאַחֵר.** הוּא הַקִּדּוּשׁ:


ואם לאחר – is the mixing [of ashes and water].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:3:3)


**כָּשֵׁר.** שֶׁהֲרֵי בַעַל הַמַּיִם לֹא עָשָׂה מְלָאכָה:


כשר – for the owner of the water did not perform any labor/work.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:3:4)


**הַמְקַדֵּשׁ לוֹ וּלְאַחֵר.** שְׁנֵיהֶם כְּאֶחָת. אִי נַמִּי קִדֵּשׁ לַחֲבֵרוֹ קֹדֶם שֶׁלּוֹ:


המקדש לו ולאחר – both of them are as one. Alternatively, he mixed [ashes and water] for his fellow prior to his own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:3:5)


**שֶׁלּוֹ פָּסוּל.** דְּקִדּוּשׁ שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ נֶחְשָׁב מְלָאכָה לִפְסֹל מֵימָיו:


שלו פסול – for the mixing [of ashes and water] of his fellow is considered work to invalidate his waters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:3:6)


**שְׁנֵיהֶם כְּשֵׁרִים.** שֶׁאֵין הַמַּיִם שֶׁלּוֹ שֶׁיִּפָּסְלוּ עַל יָדוֹ:


שניהם כשרים – for the water is not his that it can be invalidated through him.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:4:1)


**קַדֵּשׁ לִי וַאֲקַדֵּשׁ לָךְ.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁכָּל אֶחָד מִלֵּא מַיִם לְעַצְמוֹ, וּבָא זֶה וְקִדֵּשׁ מֵימָיו שֶׁל זֶה, וְזֶה קִדֵּשׁ מֵימָיו שֶׁל זֶה, אוֹתוֹ שֶׁקִּדֵּשׁ רִאשׁוֹן כָּשֵׁר מַה שֶּׁקִּדֵּשׁ לַחֲבֵרוֹ, דַּחֲבֵרוֹ לֹא עָשָׂה מְלָאכָה אֶלָּא עַד אַחַר שֶׁנִּתְקַדְּשׁוּ מֵימָיו, וּמִשֶּׁנִּתְקַדְּשׁוּ אֵין מְלָאכָה פוֹסֶלֶת. אֲבָל מַה שֶּׁקִּדֵּשׁ הַשֵּׁנִי לָרִאשׁוֹן פָּסוּל, שֶׁהָרִאשׁוֹן פְּסָלָהּ בְּמַה שֶּׁקִּדֵּשׁ מֵימֵי הַשֵּׁנִי קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּתְקַדְּשׁוּ מֵימָיו:


קדש לי ואקדש לך – as for example, that each one filled water for himself, and this one came and mixed [with ashes] the water of that one, and that one mixed the water [with ashes] of this one, the one who mixed [the water with ashes] first what he mixed for his fellow is kosher/fit, for his fellow did not do any work other than until after his waters were mixed [with ashes], but when they (i.e., the waters) were mixed with ashes, no work/labor invalidates it. But what the second [person] who mixed [ashes in the water] for the first [person] is invalid, for the first invalidated in that he mixed [water and ashes] in the water of the second ‘person] before his waters were mixed [with ashes].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:4:2)


**מַלֵּא לִי וַאֲמַלֵּא לָךְ.** הַמִּלּוּי הָאַחֲרוֹן כָּשֵׁר. אֲבָל מִלּוּי רִאשׁוֹן פָּסוּל, הַיְנוּ הַמַּיִם שֶׁנִּתְמַלְּאוּ רִאשׁוֹנָה, דְּנִפְסְלוּ בַמְּלָאכָה בַּמִּלּוּי שֶׁמִּלֵּא לַחֲבֵרוֹ:


מלא לי ואמלא לך – the latter drawing of water/filling is kosher/fit, but the first drawing of water/filling is invalid, that is, that the waters that were filled were invalidated through the work of filling/drawing that he filled for his fellow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:4:3)


**קַדֵּשׁ לִי וַאֲמַלֵּא לָךְ.** שֶׁהָיוּ לוֹ מַיִם מְמֻלָּאִים וְאָמַר לַחֲבֵרוֹ קַדֵּשׁ לִי מֵימַי וְאַחַר כָּךְ אֲמַלֵּא לָךְ:


קדש לי ואמלא לך – that he had the waters filled/drawn and said to his fellow, “mix for me my waters [with ashes] and afterwards, I will draw [water] for you.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:4:4)


**שְׁנֵיהֶם כְּשֵׁרִים.** דְּאֵין הַמְּלָאכָה פוֹסֶלֶת אַחַר קִדּוּשׁ. וְזֶה נַמִּי שֶׁנִּתְמַלְּאוּ לוֹ הַמַּיִם מֵימָיו כְּשֵׁרִים, דְּמִשֶּׁנִּתְמַלְּאוּ לוֹ הַמַּיִם לֹא עָשָׂה מְלָאכָה:


שניהם כשרים – for the work/labor does not invalidate after the mixing [of ashes with water]. And this also when the waters were drawn/filled for him, his waters are fit/kosher, that from when the waters were filled/drawn for him, he did not do any work.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:4:5)


**מַלֵּא לִי וַאֲקַדֵּשׁ לָךְ.** שֶׁהָאַחֵר הָיוּ לוֹ מַיִם מְמֻלָּאִים וְאָמַר לוֹ זֶה מַלֵּא לִי תְחִלָּה וְאַחַר כָּךְ אֲקַדֵּשׁ לָךְ:


מלא לי ואקדש לך – that the other that had waters filled/drawn and he said to him: “Fill for me [waters] first, and afterwards I will mix [the ashes in the water] for you.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:4:6)


**שְׁנֵיהֶם פְּסוּלִים.** זֶה, שֶׁמִּלֵּא עַד שֶׁלֹּא נִתְקַדְּשׁוּ מֵימָיו. וְזֶה, שֶׁקִּדֵּשׁ לַחֲבֵרוֹ מִשֶּׁנִּתְמַלְּאוּ מֵימָיו:


שניהם פסולים – this one who filled until his waters were not mixed [with ashes], and this one who mixed [ashes with water] for his fellow from when his waters were filled up/drawn.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:5:1)


**הַמְמַלֵּא לוֹ וּלְחַטָּאת.** הַמְמַלֵּא מַיִם, קְצָתָן רוֹצֶה לְצָרְכּוֹ וּבִקְצָתָן רוֹצֶה לִתֵּן אֵפֶר חַטָּאת:


הממלא לו ולחטאת – the person who fills/draws water, some of them he wants for his own needs and some of them he wants to place ashes for the rite of purification (i.e., sin offering).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:5:2)


**בָּאֵסֶל.** בַּמּוֹט. וְדֶרֶךְ שׁוֹאֲבֵי מַיִם שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים מוֹט עַל כְּתֵפָם וְכַד מָלֵא תָּלוּי בְּרֹאשׁ הַמּוֹט מִלְּפָנָיו וְכַד אֶחָד מֵאֲחוֹרָיו:


באסל (on a pole/yoke – fastening the bucket to the pole)- on a balancing pole/staff. And it was the practice of those who draw water that they would place a balancing pole/staff on their shoulders and a full pitcher was suspended on the top of the pole from in front of him and another pitcher from behind him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:5:3)


**וְאַחַר כָּךְ מִלֵּא אֶת שֶׁלּוֹ פָּסוּל.** מִשּׁוּם מְלָאכָה:


ואח"כ מילא את שלו, פסול- because of work/labor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:5:4)


**וְאֶת שֶׁל חַטָּאת לְפָנָיו.** כְּדֵי לַעֲשׂוֹת בָּהֶן שְׁמִירָה מְעֻלָּה:


ואת של חטאת לפניו – in order to do with them a greater level of guarding/protection.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:5:5)


**וְאִם נָתַן שֶׁל חַטָּאת לַאֲחוֹרָיו פְּסוּלָה.** דִּכְתִיב לְמִשְׁמֶרֶת לְמֵי נִדָּה, בִּזְמַן שֶׁהֵן שְׁמוּרִין הֵן מֵי נִדָּה, בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵינָן שְׁמוּרִין אֵינָן מֵי נִדָּה:


ואם נתן של חטאת לאחוריו פסולה – as it is written (Numbers 19:9): “to be kept for water of lustration for the Israelite community. It is for purification,” at the time that the are guarded/protected, they are the waters of lustration, [but] at the time that they are not guarded/protected, they are not the waters of lustration.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:5:6)


**הָיוּ שְׁנֵיהֶן שֶׁל חַטָּאת וְכוּ'.** דְּהוֹאִיל וּמַסִּיק אַדַּעְתֵּיהּ בְּשֶׁלְּפָנָיו נִזְכָּר נַמִּי בְשֶׁל אֲחוֹרָיו. וְהַיְנוּ מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר, כְּלוֹמַר אֶפְשָׁר לִשְׁמֹר שֶׁלַּאֲחוֹרָיו אַגַּב שֶׁלְּפָנָיו:


היו שניהן של חטאת כו' – for since he has in mind that which is in front of him, he is reminded also about what is behind him. And that is because it is possible, meaning to say, it is possible to guard/protect that which is in back of him on account of what is in front of him.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:6:1)


**הַמּוֹלִיךְ אֶת הַחֶבֶל בְּיָדוֹ לְדַרְכּוֹ.** לְאַחַר שֶׁמִּלֵּא אֶת הַדְּלִי בְּחֶבֶל הֶחֱזִיר אֶת הַחֶבֶל לַבְּעָלִים. לְדַרְכּוֹ, שֶׁלֹּא הֻצְרַךְ לְעַקֵּם אוֹ לְהַרְחִיב אֶת דַּרְכּוֹ אֶלָּא לְפִי הִלּוּכוֹ מָצָא בַעַל הַחֶבֶל וְהֶחֱזִיר לוֹ אֶת שֶׁלּוֹ, אֵין זוֹ מְלָאכָה וְכָשֵׁר הַמִּלּוּי:


המוליך את החבל בידו לדרכו (if one carries the rope – borrowed for drawing water) in his hand to restore it to the owner – on his way to perform the rite – and gave it to him) – after he had filled the bucket with a rope, he returned the rope to its owners, on his way, that it was not necessary to make a circuit/go out of his way or to lengthen his path, but rather, according to his walking , he found the owner of the rope and returned to him what was his, this is not labor (i.e., intermediate act of work) and the filling is kosher/fit/acceptable.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:6:2)


**שֶׁלֹּא לְדַרְכּוֹ.** שֶׁעִקֵּם אֶת דַּרְכּוֹ כְּדֵי לְהַחֲזִיר הַחֶבֶל, מְלָאכָה הִיא וּפָסוּל:


שלא לדרכו – that he went out of his way in order to return the rope, it is work/labor and the drawing [of water] is invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:6:3)


**זֶה הָלַךְ.** כְּלוֹמַר עַל דָּבָר זֶה הָלַךְ חָכָם אֶחָד לִשְׁאֹל מֵחַכְמֵי יַבְנֶה, הֵן סַנְהֶדְרִי גְדוֹלָה שֶׁגָּלוּ שָׁם:


זה הלך – meaning to say, that on this matter, one Sage went to ask from the Sages of Yavneh, the y are the Great Sanhedrin that were exiled there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:6:4)


**הוֹרָאַת שָׁעָה.** שְׁעַת הַדְּחָק הָיָה. הָא לָאו הָכִי לֹא:


הוראת שעה(a special dispensation) – it was a special case of an emergency. But if I was not this, it is not [allowed].



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:7:1)


**הַמְכַנֵּן אֶת הַחֶבֶל.** דֶּרֶךְ מְשַׁלְשְׁלֵי הַדְּלִי לְמַלְּאוֹת מַיִם, כְּשֶׁמַּעֲלֶה אֶת הַדְּלִי מְסַבֵּב אֶת הַחֶבֶל בְּיָדוֹ כְּמִין טַבַּעַת שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא נִגְרָר לָאָרֶץ. וּמְכַנֵּן לְשׁוֹן הָדְרָא דְכַנְתָּא [חֻלִּין פ"ג]. וְאִם בְּדֶרֶךְ הַעֲלָאַת הַדְּלִי מְכַנֵּן עַל יָד עַל יָד, צֹרֶךְ הַעֲלָאָה הִיא, וְאֵין זוֹ מְלָאכָה וְכָשֵׁר הַמִּלּוּי. וְאִם בָּאַחֲרוֹנָה לְאַחַר שֶׁהֶעֱלָהוּ כִּנְּנוֹ, חָשׁוּב מְלָאכָה וּפָסוּל:


המכנן את החבל (he who winds around his hand the rope – for drawing the bucket up – until a sufficient length is wound up) – it is the manner of those who let down a bucket to fill with water, that when he raises the bucket, he surrounds the rope in his hand like a kind of ring so that it is doesn’t drag on the ground. And [the word] "מכנן" – is he language of the coils of the ileum. But if in the midst of raising the bucket, he winds the rope around his hand more than once, it is the need of raising it and this is not work and the drawing/filling of water is kosher/fit/appropriate. But if he winds it after he has completed drawing [the water], it is considered work and is invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:7:2)


**לָזֶה הִכְשִׁירוּ הוֹרָאַת שָׁעָה.** מַה שֶּׁהִכְשִׁירוּ בְיַבְנֶה הוֹרָאַת שָׁעָה, לֹא לַמּוֹלִיךְ אֶת הַחֶבֶל שֶׁלֹּא כְדַרְכּוֹ, אֶלָּא לָזֶה שֶׁכִּנֵּן אֶת הַחֶבֶל בָּאַחֲרוֹנָה הִכְשִׁירוּ לוֹ עַל יְדֵי הַדְּחָק:


לזה הכשירו הוראת שעה – what they validated in Yavneh as a special dispensation, not to bring the rope while not on his way [to the rite], but rather for this [individual] who wound the rope [around his hand] after he finished drawing they validated for him as a special dispensation/emergency.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:8:1)


**הַמַּצְנִיעַ אֶת הֶחָבִית.** שֶׁהוּא דוֹלֶה בָהּ הַמַּיִם לָשׂוּם בַּשֹּׁקֶת, וּלְאַחַר שֶׁעֵרָה מִמֶּנָּה הִצְנִיעָהּ קֹדֶם שֶׁיְּקַדֵּשׁ הַמַּיִם בַּשֹּׁקֶת, אֵין הַצְנָעָה זוֹ חֲשׁוּבָה מְלָאכָה, לְפִי שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לָהּ עֲדַיִן לְמַלְּאוֹת בָּהּ, וְצֹרֶךְ מִלּוּי הוּא. וְכֵן כְּפָאָהּ עַל פִּיהָ נַמִּי לְנַגְּבָהּ לְפִי שֶׁרוֹצֶה לְמַלְּאוֹת בָּהּ עֲדַיִן, לָא חֲשִׁיב מְלָאכָה וְכָשֵׁר הַמִּלּוּי. אֲבָל אִם עָשָׂה כֵן לְהוֹלִיךְ בָּהּ אֶת הַקִּדּוּשׁ דְּהַיְנוּ מַיִם הַמְקֻדָּשִׁין, הוֹאִיל וְלָאו צֹרֶךְ מִלּוּי הוּא, חָשׁוּב מְלָאכָה וּפָסוּל הַמִּלּוּי:


המצניע את החבית – that he draws water with it to put in the trough, and after he emptied [the water] from it, he put it away/hid it prior to mixing the water [with the ashes] in the trough, this putting away [of the bucket] is not considered work, because he still needs it to fill it up [with water], and it is the need of drawing/filling water. And similarly, he inverted/bent it over on its mouth also to dry it because he still wants to fill [water] in it, it is not considered work, and it is fit/appropriate for filling/drawing. But, if he did so to bring the mixed ashes and water, that is, the water that is mixed [with ashes], since it is not for the purpose of filling/drawing water, it is considered work and the filling/drawing [of water] is invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:8:2)


**בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁתַּחֲזִיק מַיִם הַרְבֵּה.** לֹא חָשׁוּב פִּנּוּי זֶה מְלָאכָה:


בשביל שתחזיק מים הרבה – this emptying is not considered work.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:8:3)


**בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁהוּא זוֹלֵף.** שֶׁהוּא מַזֶּה:


בשעה שהוא זולף -when he sprinkles.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:8:4)


**פָּסוּל.** דְּפִנּוּי זֶה חָשׁוּב מְלָאכָה:


פסול – this emptying out is considered work.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:9:1)


**וְהוֹרָה הוֹרָאָה.** לְהַתִּיר אוֹ לֶאֱסֹר, לְטַמֵּא אוֹ לְטַהֵר, לִפְטֹר אוֹ לְחַיֵּב:


והורה הוראה – to permit or to forbid, to defile or to purify, to make exempt or to make liable.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:9:2)


**וְדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִשּׁוּם מְלָאכָה.** כְּגוֹן הוֹרָאָה וּמַרְאֶה אֶת הַדֶּרֶךְ:


ודבר שאינו משום הלכה – as for example, a lesson and showing [others] the way.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:9:3)


**אִם עָמַד.** שֶׁנִּתְעַכֵּב שָׁם בִּשְׁבִיל זֶה:


אם עמד – if he was delayed there because of this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:9:4)


**פָּסוּל.** מִשּׁוּם הֶסַּח הַדַּעַת. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


פסול – because of distraction. And this is the Halakha.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:10:1)


**הַמּוֹסֵר מֵימָיו לְטָמֵא.** לְשָׁמְרָן:


המוסר מימיו לטמא – to guard/protect them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:10:2)


**פְּסוּלִים.** בֵּין עָשׂוּ הַבְּעָלִים הָרִאשׁוֹנִים מְלָאכָה, בֵּין לֹא עָשׂוּ הַבְּעָלִים הָרִאשׁוֹנִים מְלָאכָה, דִּבְחֶזְקַת הַשּׁוֹמֵר קַיְמוּ וְהוּא אֵינוֹ מַקְפִּיד לְשָׁמְרָן מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא טָמֵא, וְהַתּוֹרָה אָמְרָה לְמִשְׁמֶרֶת לְמֵי נִדָּה:


פסולים – whether the first owners did work [or] whether the first owners did not do work, for it was in the possession of the guardian they exist and he was not strict to guard them because he is impure, but the Torah stated (Numbers 19:9): “to be kept for water of lustration.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:10:3)


**וּלְטָהוֹר כְּשֵׁרִים.** וַאֲפִלּוּ עָשׂוּ הַבְּעָלִים הָרִאשׁוֹנִים מְלָאכָה, שֶׁכְּבָר יָצְאוּ מֵרְשׁוּת בְּעָלִים וְקַיְמוּ בִרְשׁוּת שׁוֹמֵר. וּמִיהוּ אִם הַשּׁוֹמֵר עָשָׂה מְלָאכָה, פְּסוּלִים:


ולטהור כשרים – and even if the first owners did work, for they (i.e., the waters with the ashes) had already left the domain of the owners and existed in the domain of the guardian. But however, if the guard did work, they are invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:10:4)


**אַף לְטָמֵא כְּשֵׁרִים.** דְּסָבַר רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר הַמּוֹסֵר מֵימָיו לְטָמֵא, בְּחֶזְקַת הַבְּעָלִים הָרִאשׁוֹנִים קָיְמוּ, הִלְכָּךְ אִם לֹא עָשׂוּ הַבְּעָלִים מְלָאכָה כְּשֵׁרִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


אף לטמא כשרים – for Rabbi Eliezer holds that a person who gave his water over to someone who is unclean, they exist in the possession of the first owners, therefore, if the owners did not do work, they are fit/appropriate. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:11:1)


**וְהִגְבִּיהוּ זֶה עַל זֶה.** שֶׁסִּיְּעוּ זֶה אֶת זֶה לְהַגְבִּיהַּ:


והגביהו זה על זה – that they assisted each other to lift up/raise [the bucket].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:11:2)


**וְנָטַל זֶה לָזֶה קוֹצוֹ.** אוֹ שֶׁנָּטַל זֶה לָזֶה קוֹץ שֶׁנִּתְחַב לוֹ בְיָדוֹ:


ונטל זה לזה קוצו – or that that each one took the thorn that was inserted in his hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:11:3)


**בְּקִדּוּשׁ אֶחָד.** שֶׁהָיוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם מְמַלְּאִים לְצֹרֶךְ קִדּוּשׁ אֶחָד, כָּשֵׁר. שֶׁיָּכוֹל כָּל אֶחָד לְסַיֵּעַ לַחֲבֵרוֹ, וּנְטִילַת הַקּוֹץ שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ נַמִּי צֹרֶךְ מִלּוּי הִיא:


בקידוש אחד – that both of them were filling [water] for the purpose of one mixing, it is kosher/fit. For each one can assist his fellow, and removing the thorn that is in his hand also - requires filling/drawing water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:11:4)


**בִּשְׁנֵי קִדּוּשִׁין פָּסוּל.** שֶׁפָּסַל כָּל אֶחָד מֵימָיו בְּמַה שֶּׁסִּיֵּעַ לַחֲבֵרוֹ אוֹ שֶׁנָּטַל לוֹ קוֹץ, מִשּׁוּם מְלָאכָה:


בשני קידושין פסול – for each one of them invalidated his water in how he assisted his fellow or removed from him a thorn, because of work.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:11:5)


**אִם הִתְנוּ בֵינֵיהֶן.** שֶׁאָמַר כָּל אֶחָד לַחֲבֵרוֹ כְּשֶׁאַגְבִּיהַּ לְךָ יְהֵא שֶׁלִּי וְשֶׁלְּךָ בִּרְשׁוּתְךָ (וכמו כן אמר לו האחר), וְאַחַר שֶׁהִגְבִּיהוֹ לוֹקֵחַ כָּל אֶחָד מֵהֶן אַחַת מֵהֶן בִּרְשׁוּתוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


אם התנו ביניהן – that each one said to his fellow: “when I lift up for you, it will become mine yours in your domain (and similarly the other [person] said to him). But after he lifted/raised it up, each one acquires one of them in his domain. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:12:1)


**הַפּוֹרֵץ עַל מְנָת לִגְדֹּר.** כְּלוֹמַר אֲפִלּוּ עַל מְנָת לִגְדֹּר, לָא חֲשִׁיב מְלָאכָה וְכָשֵׁר הַמִּלּוּי:


הפורץ על מנת לגדור (he who breaks down a fence [while carrying water] on condition of putting up a fence) – meaning to say, even on the condition of putting up a fence, is not considered work, and the filling [of water] is kosher/fit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:12:2)


**הָאוֹכֵל עַל מְנָת לִקְצוֹת.** לְאַחַר שֶׁמִּלֵּא הַמַּיִם קֹדֶם שֶׁיְּקַדֵּשׁ הָיָה מְלַקֵּט לַתְּאֵנִים עַל מְנָת לְיַבְּשָׁן לַעֲשׂוֹת מֵהֶן גְּרוֹגָרוֹת, וּלְאַחַר לְקִיטָתָן אֲכָלָן, אֵין הַמַּחֲשָׁבָה שֶׁהָיָה רוֹצֶה לַעֲשׂוֹתָן קְצִיעוֹת פּוֹסֶלֶת בַּמַּיִם, אֶלָּא הֲרֵי הוּא כְאִלּוּ לָקַט לֶאֱכֹל, שֶׁאֵינוֹ פוֹסֵל, דּוּמְיָא דְפוֹרֵץ עַל מְנָת לִגְדֹּר שֶׁאֵין הַמַּחֲשָׁבָה שֶׁהוּא חוֹשֵׁב לִגְדֹּר פּוֹסֶלֶת עַד שֶׁיִּגְדֹּר, הָכָא נַמִּי:


האוכל על מת לקצות (the person who eats on the condition of storing up [dates and figs}- after he filled up water prior to his mixing [the water with ashes], he would collect/harvest figs on the condition to dry them on condition to make from them dried figs, and after he collected them, eat them, the thought that he wanted to make of them packed figs does not invalidate them in water, but they are as if he harvested/collected them to eat, and he do es not invalidate them, it is similar to someone who breaks down a fence [while carrying water] on the condition to put up a fence, for the thought that he thinks to to put up a fence does not invalidate it until he puts up the fence, the same applies here [as well].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:12:3)


**אִם קָצָה.** אִם הִנִּיחַ אוֹתָן בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁמַּנִּיחִין הַתְּאֵנִים לְיַבֵּשׁ, פָּסוּל:


אם קצה (if he stored) – if he placed them in a place where they put the figs to dry, it is invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 7:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/7:12:4)


**בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁלֹּא יֹאבַד פָּסוּל.** שֶׁהַמַּצְנִיעַ אֳכָלִים כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יֹאבְדוּ, חֲשִׁיבָא מְלָאכָה:


בשביל שלא יאבד פסול (in order that it not be wasted, it is unfit) – for the person who hides food in order that they not be wasted, is considered work.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:1:1)


**שְׁנַיִם שֶׁהָיוּ שׁוֹמְרִים אֶת הַשֹּׁקֶת.** שֶׁנִּתְמַלָּא לְחַטָּאת:


שנים שהיו שומרים את השוקת – that was filled for [mixing the ashes with the water] for the lustration/sin-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:1:2)


**טָהַר.** אוֹתוֹ שֶׁנִּטְמָא טָהַר מִטֻּמְאָתוֹ:


טהר – he who had become defiled was purified from his defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:1:3)


**וְנִטְמָא שֵׁנִי.** הַיְנוּ חֲבֵרוֹ שֶׁהָיָה טָהוֹר:


ונטמא שני – that is his companion who was ritually pure. -


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:1:4)


**כְּשֵׁרִים.** הַמַּיִם שֶׁבַּשֹּׁקֶת:


כשרים – the water that is in the trough.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:1:5)


**עָמַד וְעָשָׂה הַשֵּׁנִי מְלָאכָה.** עָמַד הָרִאשׁוֹן מִמְּלַאכְתּוֹ, וּבָא הַשֵּׁנִי וְעָשָׂה מְלָאכָה תַחְתָּיו:


עמד ושעה שני מלאכה – the first person arose from jis work, and the second did work in his place.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:2:1)


**נִטְמָא וְטִמְּאָהוּ.** נִטְמָא הַסַּנְדָּל וְטִמֵּא אֶת הָאָדָם לְחַטָּאת. שֶׁכֵּלִים שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ בְמַשְׁקִים מְטַמְּאִים אֶת הָאָדָם לְחַטָּאת, שֶׁאֵין מוֹנִין רִאשׁוֹן וְשֵׁנִי בְּחַטָּאת, כְּדִתְנַן לְקַמָּן פֶּרֶק כָּל הָרָאוּי (משנה ו). אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאִם נָפְלוּ הַמַּשְׁקִים הַלָּלוּ עַל בְּשָׂרוֹ, טָהוֹר, שֶׁהַטָּהוֹר לַפָּרָה אִם נָגְעוּ מַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִים בִּבְשָׂרוֹ, הוּא טָהוֹר כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה. לְבַד אִם נָגְעוּ בְיָדָיו, לְפִי שֶׁהַמַּשְׁקִין טְמֵאִים מְטַמְּאִים אֶת הַיָּדַיִם, וְגַבֵּי טָהֳרַת חַטָּאת קַיְמָא לָן דְּאִם נִטְמְאוּ יָדָיו נִטְמָא גוּפוֹ:


נטמא וטמאהו- the sandal became defiled and it defiled the person for the [waters of] lustration/purification waters. For the vessels that were defiled through liquids defile the person for the purification, that we don’t count the first and second for purification/sin-offering as it is taught in the Mishnah further on in the chapter כל הראוי/whatever is appropriate (Mishnah 6). Even though that if these liquids fell upon his skin, he is ritually pure, for a person who is ritually pure for the rite of the heifer, if impure liquids came in contact with his flesh, he is pure like he was, except if they touched his hands, because impure liquids defile the hands, and regarding the purity of the sin-offering, we hold that if his hands were defiled, his body is defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:2:2)


**מְטַמְּאֶיךָ לֹא טִמְּאוּנִי.** מַשְׁקִים שֶׁטִּמְּאוּ אֶת הַסַּנְדָּל לֹא הָיוּ יְכוֹלִים לְטַמֵּא הַטָּהוֹר לְחַטָּאת אִם נָגְעוּ בִבְשָׂרוֹ כְּדַאֲמַרַן, וְהַסַּנְדָּל הַבָּא מִכֹּחָם מְטַמְּאוֹ:


מטמאיך לא טמאוני (those things which made you unclean could not have made me unclean) – liquids that defiled the sandal were not able to defile the pure individual, if they came in contact with his flesh, as we have stated, but the sandal that comes from their power defiles him.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:3:1)


**הַשּׂוֹרֵף פָּרָה.** אֲדֻמָּה:


השורף פרה – the Red [Heifer].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:3:2)


**וּפָרִים.** הַנִּשְׂרָפִים, כְּגוֹן פַּר יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים וּפַר כֹּהֵן מָשִׁיחַ וּפַר הֶעְלֵם דָּבָר שֶׁל צִבּוּר, מְטַמְּאִים בְּגָדִים, דִּבְכֻלְּהוּ כְתִיב יְכַבֵּס בְּגָדָיו, וְלֹא שְׁנָא בְגָדִים שֶׁהוּא לָבוּשׁ וְלֹא שְׁנָא בְגָדִים שֶׁהוּא נוֹגֵעַ, וְהוּא הַדִּין לְכָל שְׁאָר כֵּלִים. דְּלֹא מְמַעֲטִינַן אֶלָּא אָדָם, וּכְלֵי חֶרֶס בִּלְבַד הוּא שֶׁאֵין מִטַּמְּאִין אִם נוֹגְעִים בָּהֶן:


ופרים – that are burned, such as a the bullock of Yom Kippur and the bull of the anointed priest [brought as a sin-offering by an anointed High Priest who unwittingly made an erroneous Halakhic decision] (see Tractate Horayot, Chapter 2, Mishnah 1) a bull for an unwitting communal sin [of an active transgression committed by the Jewish people, as a result of an erroneous halakhic decision handed down by the Great Sanhedrin] (see Tractate Horayot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 5), they defile clothing, for in all of them it is written [in regard to the Red Heifer] (Numbers 19:8): “[He who performed the burning] shall wash his garments [in water],” (see also Tractate Parah, Chapter 4, Mishnah 4 – that all who deal with the Red Heifer from the beginning to the end defile clothing and regarding the sacrifices of Yom Kippur, see Leviticus 16:28 and 16:26), but there is no difference regarding clothing that he touches, and the same law applies to all of the rest of the vessels. That we do not exclude anything other than a person and earthenware vessels alone, that do not defile if they come in contact with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:3:3)


**וּפָרָה וּפָרִים וְשָׂעִיר הַמִּשְׁתַּלֵּחַ עַצְמָן.** אִם נָגְעוּ בַבְּגָדִים הֲרֵי הֵן טְהוֹרִים. וְהַיְנוּ דְאָמְרִי לֵיהּ בְּגָדִים לָאָדָם, מְטַמְּאֶיךָ לֹא טִמְּאוּנִי וְאַתָּה טִמֵּאתָנִי:


ופרה ופרים עצמן ושעיר המשתלח – if they came in contact with clothing, they are ritually pure. And this is what the clothing said to the man: “Things that made you unclean could not have made me unclean, but you made me unclean.”



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:4:1)


**הָאוֹכֵל מִנִּבְלַת עוֹף טָהוֹר.** נִבְלַת עוֹף טָהוֹר אֵין לָהּ טֻמְאָה אִם נָגְעָה בְאָדָם אוֹ בִבְגָדִים, אֶלָּא בְּבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה מְטַמָּא אָדָם לְטַמֵּא בְגָדִים, כְּדִתְנַן בְּמַסֶּכֶת טָהֳרוֹת, וְהַיְנוּ דְאָמְרֵי לֵיהּ בְּגָדִים לָאָדָם מְטַמְּאֶיךָ לֹא טִמְּאוּנִי וְכוּ':


האוכל מנבלת העוף טהור – the carrion of a clean bird, has no defilement if it came in contact with a person or with clothing, but in the esophagus it defiles humans to defile clothing, as it is taught in the Mishnah of Tractate Taharot (Chapter 1, Mishnah 1), and that what the clothing says to a person: “The things which made you unclean could not have made me unclean, etc.”



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:5:1)


**כָּל וְלַד הַטֻּמְאָה.** כְּגוֹן רִאשׁוֹן וְשֵׁנִי, אֵין מְטַמֵּא כֵלִים. אֶלָּא אַב הַטֻּמְאָה. וּמַשְׁקֶה שֶׁנִּטְמָא בְרִאשׁוֹן וְשֵׁנִי מְטַמֵּא כֵלִים, גְּזֵרָה מִשּׁוּם מַשְׁקֵה זָב וְזָבָה, כְּגוֹן רֻקּוֹ וְזוֹבוֹ וּמֵימֵי רַגְלָיו שֶׁהֵן אַב הַטֻּמְאָה:


כל ולד הטומאה – as for example first-degree and second-degree, do not defile vessels, but the primary source(s) of ritual impurity. But liquid that was defiled in first-degree or second-degree defiles vessels, as a decree because of the liquid of a male with gonorrhea and/or a woman with a flux, such as his spittle, and his flux and his urine which are primary sources of ritual defilement.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:6:1)


**אֵין כְּלִי חֶרֶס מְטַמֵּא חֲבֵרוֹ.** שֶׁאֵין כְּלִי חֶרֶס נַעֲשֶׂה אַב הַטֻּמְאָה לְעוֹלָם, וְאֵין כְּלִי חֶרֶס מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה אֶלָּא מֵאַב הַטֻּמְאָה, אַלְמָא שֶׁאֵין כְּלִי חֶרֶס מְטַמֵּא חֲבֵרוֹ:


אין כלי חרס מטמא חברו – that an earthenware vessel does not ever become a primary source of ritual impurity, and an earthenware vessels is not susceptible to receive ritual impurity other than from a primary source of ritual impurity, so we see, that an earthenware vessel does not defile its fellow earthenware vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:6:2)


**אֶלָּא מַשְׁקִין.** אֲבָל מְטַמֵּא הוּא מַשְׁקִין וְהַמַּשְׁקִים חוֹזְרִים וּמְטַמְּאִים חֲבֵרוֹ, גְּזֵרָה מִשּׁוּם מַשְׁקֵה זָב וְזָבָה כְּדַאֲמַרַן:


אלא משקין – but if does defile liquids and the liquids return and defile its fellow earthenware vessel, as a decree because of the liquid of a male with gonorrhea/flux and a woman with flux as we have stated.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:7:1)


**כָּל הַפּוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה.** כְּגוֹן יָדַיִם שְׁנִיּוֹת עוֹשׂוֹת מַשְׁקִים תְּחִלָּה לְטַמֵּא חֻלִּין, וְיָדַיִם עַצְמָן שֶׁנָּגְעוּ בְאָכְלֵי חֻלִּין, טְהוֹרִים, שֶׁאֵין שֵׁנִי עוֹשֶׂה שְׁלִישִׁי בְחֻלִּין:


כל הפוסל את התרומה – as for example hands which are second-degree [of impurity], make liquids first-degree to defile non-holy things, but hands themselves that came in contact with non-holy foodstuffs, are pure, for something that is second-degree [in defilement] does not make non-holy things third-degree [uncleanness].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:7:2)


**חוּץ מִטְּבוּל יוֹם.** שֶׁלֹּא הֶעֱרִיב שִׁמְשׁוֹ. אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא פוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה, אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא הַמַּשְׁקִין לִהְיוֹת תְּחִלָּה. וַאֲפִלּוּ מַשְׁקִין שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה אִם נָגַע בָּהֶן טְבוּל יוֹם אֵינָן אֶלָּא פְסוּלִים בִּלְבַד לֹא טְמֵאִים:


חוץ מטבול יום - whose sunset had not yet arrived. Even though he defiles the heave offering, he does not defile liquids to become first-degree [uncleanness], and even the liquids of heave-offering if a person who had immersed himself that day had come in contact with them, other than those things which are invalid alone are not ritually impure (see also Tractate Kelim, Chapter 8, Mishnah 4).



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:8:1)


**כָּל הַיַּמִּים כְּמִקְוֶה.** לְכָל הָנֵי מִלֵּי דְּקָתָנֵי בְסוֹפוֹ. (וגם) אֵין מְטַהֲרִין בְּזוֹחֲלִין, דְּאֵין מִקְוֶה מְטַהֵר בְּזוֹחֲלִין אֶלָּא בִמְכֻנָּסִין, דִּכְתִיב [וַיִּקְרָא יא] אַךְ מַעְיָן, מַעְיָן מְטַהֵר בְּזוֹחֲלִין וְאֵין מִקְוֶה מְטַהֶרֶת בְּזוֹחֲלִין:


כל הימים כמקוה – for all of these words as it is taught at the conclusion [of this Mishnah]. (And also) they do not render clean when running like springs, for a Mikveh does not purify in running/moving waters, but rather in collected waters, as is written (Leviticus 11:36): “However, a spring/אך מעין [or cistern in which water is collected shall be pure,]” a spring purifies in moving/running waters but a Mikveh does not purify in moving/running waters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:8:2)


**וּפְסוּלִין לַזָּבִין.** דְּזָב בָּעֵי מַיִם חַיִּים, דִּכְתִיב [שָׁם טו] וְרָחַץ בְּשָׂרוֹ בְּמַיִם חַיִּים. וְלַמְּצֹרָע דִּכְתִיב [שָׁם יד] אֶל כְּלִי חֶרֶשׂ עַל מַיִם חַיִּים. וּלְמֵי חַטָּאת, דִּכְתִיב [בְּמִדְבָּר יט] מַיִם חַיִּים אֶל כֶּלִי:


ופסולין לזבין – for a man with gonorrhea/flux requires living waters, as it is written (Leviticus 14:9): “and bath his body in water; [then he shall be pure].” (see also Leviticus 16:26 and 16:28), and for the leper [as it is written (Leviticus 14:5): “[The priest shall order one of the birds slaughtered over] fresh water in an earthen vessel/אל-כלי-חרש על-מים חיים” [and for the waters of the sin offering], as it is written (Numbers 19:1): “and fresh water shall be added to them in a vessel.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:8:3)


**הַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל כְּמִקְוֶה.** לֹא קָרָא הַכָּתוּב מִקְוֶה אֶלָּא לַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל, דְּבֵיהּ מִשְׁתַּעֵי קְרָא בְּמַעֲשֵׂה בְרֵאשִׁית, שֶׁשָּׁם נִקְווּ כָל מֵימֵי בְרֵאשִׁית. וְלֹא נֶאֱמָר יַמִּים בִּלְשׁוֹן רַבִּים, אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמְּעֹרָבִים בּוֹ מִינֵי יַמִּים הַרְבֵּה, שֶׁכָּל הַנְּחָלִים הוֹלְכִים אֵלָיו:


הים הגדול כמקוה – Scripture did not call a Mikveh other than the Great Ocean, for in it Scripture refers in the Creation Story for there all the waters of Creation were gathered, and it doesn’t state “seas”/ימים – in the plural, other than because many seas are mixed in together in it, for all the streams travel to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:8:4)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר.** כָּל הַיַּמִּים וְיָם הַגָּדוֹל, תּוֹרַת מַעְיָן עֲלֵיהֶן לְעִנְיָן שֶׁהֵן מְטַהֲרִין בְּזוֹחֲלִין, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַנְּחָלִים הוֹלְכִין וְזוֹחֲלִין עֲלֵיהֶן. אֲבָל פְּסוּלִין הֵן לְתוֹרַת מַיִם חַיִּים, שֶׁהַכָּתוּב קוֹרְאָן מִקְוֶה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ר' יוסי אומר – all the seas and the Great Ocean have the status of a spring upon them regarding the fact that they purify in moving/running waters, because the streams go and flow upon them. But they are invalid regarding the status of living waters, for Scripture calls them Mikveh. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:1)


**מַיִם הַמֻּכִּין.** הַמַּזִּיקִין. כְּגוֹן שֶׁהֵן מְלוּחִין וּפוֹשְׁרִין:


מים המוכין – that damage, as for example, that they are salty and tepid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:2)


**פְּסוּלִין.** לְמֵי חַטָּאת:


פסולין – for the waters of the sin-offering/the Red Heifer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:3)


**מַיִם הַמְכַזְּבִין.** הַפּוֹסְקִין. כְּמוֹ אֲשֶׁר לֹא יְכַזְּבוּ מֵימָיו (ישעיה נח):


מים המכזבין (waters which fail at certain times) – that stop, like (Isaiah 58:11): “like a spring whose waters do not fail.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:4)


**פְּסוּלִין.** דְּמַיִם חַיִּים בָּעִינַן:


פסולין – for we require living/fresh waters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:5)


**אֶחָד בַּשָּׁבוּעַ.** אֲבָל אֶחָד בַּיּוֹבֵל, שַׁפִּיר מִקְּרוּ מַיִם חַיִּים:


אחד בשבוע (once in seven years) – but once in a jubilee/יובל is well/good as they are called “living waters.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:6)


**בְּפֻלְמְסָיוֹת.** חֲיָלוֹת שֶׁל גְּיָסוֹת שֶׁשּׁוֹתִין וּמְקַלְקְלִים בָּהֶן:


בפולמסיות – the soldiers of ravaging troops that drink and ruin them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:7)


**בִּשְׁנֵי בִצָּרוֹן.** שָׁנִים שֶׁל עֲצִירַת גְּשָׁמִים:


בשני בצרון – years where rain fall stops.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:9:8)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה פוֹסֵל.** אֲפִלּוּ אוֹתָן שֶׁאֵין מְכַזְּבִין אֶלָּא בְּפֻלְמְסָיוֹת וּבִשְׁנֵי בִצָּרוֹן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ור' יהודה פוסל – even those that do not fail other than in wartime or in years of drought. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:10:1)


**מֵי קַרְמְיוֹן וּמֵי פוּגָה.** נְהָרוֹת שֶׁבְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל הֵן:


מי קרמיון ומי פוגה – they are rivers in the land of Israel:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:10:2)


**מֵי בִצִּים.** לְשׁוֹן הֲיִגְאֶה גֹּמֶא בְּלֹא בִצָּה (איוב ח). כְּלוֹמַר, מַיִם שֶׁחֹמֶר וְטִיט מְעֹרָב בָּהֶן. וְרַחֲמָנָא אָמַר מַיִם חַיִּים אֶל כֶּלִי, וְהָכָא אִיכָּא חֲצִיצָה בֵּין מַיִם לַכֶּלִי:


the swamp waters {see also Talmud Bava Batra 74b, listing the four rivers: The Jordan, the Yarmukh, the Karmiyon and Fugah, whereas the Arukh in its entry on Karmiyon mentions a verse from Second Kings, Chapter 5, Verse 12: “Are not the Amanah and the Pharpar, the rivers of Damascus, better than all the waters of Israel? I could bathe in them and be clean!”). It is the language of (Job 8:11): “Can papyrus thrive without marsh? [Can rushes grow without water],” meaning to say water in which clay/mortar and plaster/mud is combined/mixed in them. But the All-Merciful stated (Numbers 19:17): “fresh water shall be added to them in a vessel,” but here there is an interposition/an intervening object between the water to the vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:10:3)


**מֵי תַעֲרֹבוֹת.** שֶׁמַּיִם פְּסוּלִים מִתְעָרְבִים בָּהֶם:


מי תערובת (mixed waters)- they are unfit waters for they are combined/mixed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:10:4)


**אֶחָד פָּסוּל וְאֶחָד כָּשֵׁר.** וְנִתְעָרְבוּ, אָסוּר לְקַדֵּשׁ מִמְּקוֹם הַתַּעֲרֹבוֹת וּלְמַטָּה:


אחד פסול ואחד כשר – and they combined, it is prohibited to mix from the place of combination/mixture and below.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:10:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה פוֹסֵל.** אֲפִלּוּ בִשְׁנֵיהֶם כְּשֵׁרִים פּוֹסֵל רַבִּי יְהוּדָה לִטֹּל מִמְּקוֹם הַתַּעֲרֹבוֹת, גְּזֵרָה אַטּוּ אֶחָד פָּסוּל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ר' יהודה פוסל – even if both of them are fit/appropriate, Rabbi Yehuda invalidates taking from the place of combination/mixture, as a decree on account of one that is unfit. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:11:1)


**בְּאֵר אַחְאָב.** לֹא אִתְפְּרַשׁ הֵיכָן הוּא, וְהָא קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּמַיִם חַיִּים הֵן:


באר אחאב – it is not explained where this is, but it comes to tell us that they are fresh waters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:11:2)


**וּמְעָרַת פַּמְיָס.** מְעָרָה הַסְּמוּכָה לָעִיר דָּן שֶׁמִּשָּׁם יַרְדֵּן יוֹצֵא. וּבִלְשׁוֹן יִשְׁמָעֵאל קוֹרִין לְדָן בניי"ס:


ומערת פמייס – a cave that is near to the city of Dan from where the Jordan goes out. And in the language of Ishmael (i.e., the Moslems), they call the Dan, Banias.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:11:3)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר הֲרֵי הִיא בְחֶזְקַת מֻתֶּרֶת.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ר' יהודה אומר הרי היא בחזקת מותרת – but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 8:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/8:11:4)


**עַד שֶׁתִּצַּל.** עַד שֶׁיַּחְזְרוּ הַמַּיִם צְלוּלִים כִּדְמֵעִקָּרָא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל:


עד שתצל – until the waters return to become clear/settled as at first. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yishmael.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:1)


**צְלוֹחִית.** כְּלִי שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים בּוֹ הַמַּיִם הַמְקֻדָּשִׁים לְאַחַר שֶׁהִשְׁלִיךְ הָאֵפֶר לְתוֹכָן, קָרוּי צְלוֹחִית, וְהַכְּלִי שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים בּוֹ הַמַּיִם כְּדֵי לְקַדְּשָׁן קֹדֶם שֶׁיִּתְּנוּ בָהֶן הָאֵפֶר, קָרוּי שֹׁקֶת:


צלוחית – a vessel that they place in it the waters mixed with ashes (i.e., waters of lustration) after he had cast the ashes into them, is called צלוחית/a flask [with a wide body and a narrow neck- containing the sprinkling water], and the vessel that they place in it the water in order to mix them prior to placing in the ashes is called שוקת/trough.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:2)


**מַיִם כָּל שֶׁהֵן.** שֶׁאֵינָן כְּשֵׁרִים לְהַזָּאָה:


מים כל שהן – which are not fit for sprinkling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:3)


**יַזֶּה שְׁתַּיִם.** שֶׁאִם מַזֶּה אַחַת, שֶׁמָּא אֵין כָּאן מִן הַכְּשֵׁרִים. אֲבָל כִּי מַזֶּה שְׁתַּיִם, אִי אֶפְשָׁר דְּלֵיכָּא בַחֲדָא מִנַּיְהוּ מִן הַכְּשֵׁרִים. וְהַזָּאָה אֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה שִׁעוּר:


יזה שתים – for if he sprinkles once, perhaps there isn’t here from the fit/appropriate [waters], but when he sprinkles twice, it is impossible that there isn’t in one of hem from the fit/appropriate [waters]. But the sprinkling does not need a [set] measure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים פּוֹסְלִים.** קָסָבְרֵי הַזָּאָה צְרִיכָה שִׁעוּר וְאֵין מִצְטָרְפִין הַהַזָּאוֹת. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ, יַזֶּה שְׁתֵּי הַזָּאוֹת וְיַשְׁלִיכֵם חוּץ לַכְּלִי וְיִשָּׁאֵר הַשְּׁאָר מֵי חַטָּאת כְּשֵׁרִים. וּפְלִיאָה הִיא בְעֵינַי, דִּבְפֶרֶק כָּל הַזְּבָחִים בַּגְּמָרָא [דַּף פ] מַשְׁמַע כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי:


וחכמים פוסלים – that they hold that sprinkling requires a [fixed] measure, and we don’t combine sprinklings. And the Halakha is according to the Sages. But Maimonides explained that he should make two sprinklings and cast them outside of the vessel, the rest should remain the waters of lustration which are fit/appropriate. And this is a hidden thing in my eyes, for in the Gemara in the chapter, "כל הזבחים"/All of the offerings (Chapter 8 of Tractate Zevakhim) [folio 80a], it is implied as I explained it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:5)


**יַנִּיחֶנָּה בַחַמָּה וְהַטַּל עוֹלֶה.** שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הַטַּל לַעֲלוֹת כְּנֶגֶד הַחַמָּה. וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר (שמות טז) וַיְהִי בַבֹּקֶר וַתַּעַל שִׁכְבַת הַטָּל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


יניחנו בחמה והטל עולה – that it is the manner of the dew to evaporate/to go up towards the sun. And similarly, it (i.e., Scripture) states (Exodus 16:13-14): “In the morning…when the fall of dew lifted, [there, over the surface of the wilderness lay a fine and flaky substance, as fine as frost on the ground].” But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:6)


**יְעָרֶה וְכוּ'.** מֵרִיק מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹךְ הַכְּלִי, וְאֵינוֹ כָשֵׁר לְהַנִּיחַ בּוֹ מֵי חַטָּאת עַד שֶׁיְּנַגֵּב מִן הַמַּשְׁקִים וּמֵי פֵרוֹת:


יערה וכו' – he empties out what is within the vessel, and it is not fit to leave in it the waters of purification until it dries from the liquids and fruit juice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:1:7)


**יְעָרֶה וְאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְנַגֵּב.** דְּאִם אִיתָא דְנִשְׁאָר מִמֶּנּוּ בַכְּלִי הָיָה רִשּׁוּמוֹ נִכָּר:


יערה ואינו צריך לנגב – and if there is that remains from it in the vessel, its mark is recognized.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:1)


**וְנִתְבַּקְּעוּ.** שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּהוּ בְתוֹךְ הַמַּיִם זְמַן מְרֻבֶּה עַד שֶׁנִּתְבַּקְּעוּ, אוֹ אֲפִלּוּ לֹא נִתְבַּקְּעוּ אֶלָּא שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּנּוּ מַרְאֵה הַמַּיִם, פְּסוּלִין:


ונתבקעו (burst forth) – that they (i.e., the insects and creeping things) within the water a long time until they burst, or even if they didn’t burst, but that the appearance of the water changed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:2)


**חִפּוּשִׁית.** תּוֹלַעַת שְׁחוֹרָה:


חפושים (scorpion, beetle/scarabee) – a black worm.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:3)


**בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ.** בֵּין נִתְבַּקְּעָה בֵּין לֹא נִתְבַּקְּעָה:


בין כך ובין כך – whether it burst or whether it didn’t burst.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:4)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהִיא כִשְׁפוֹפֶרֶת.** שֶׁל קָנֶה חֲלוּלָה וְהַמַּיִם נִכְנָסִים מִצַּד זֶה וְיוֹצְאִים מִצַּד זֶה וּמִתְעָרֵב עִמָּהֶם הַלַּחוּת שֶׁבְּקִרְבָּהּ:


מפני שהיא כשפופרת – [like the tube] of a hollow reed and the water enters from this side and exists from that side, and the moisture that is within it combines with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:5)


**דִּירָה.** תּוֹלַעַת שֶׁבַּחִטִּין:


דירה (name of a grain worm) – a worm that is in wheat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:6)


**כִּנָּה.** מִין תּוֹלַעַת נַמִּי הַגָּדֵל בַּתְּבוּאָה:


כנה (vermin/louse) – a species of worm that also grows in grain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:2:7)


**כְּשֵׁרִים.** אֲפִלּוּ נִתְבַּקְּעוּ. וְאֵין כֵּן הֲלָכָה:


כשרים – even if they burst. But this is not the Halakha.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:3:1)


**שָׁתָת מֵהֶן בְּהֵמָה אוֹ חַיָּה פְּסוּלִים.** מִשּׁוּם מַשְׁקֶה שֶׁבְּפִיהֶן הַחוֹזֵר, וְהַמַּיִם וְהָרֹק שֶׁבְּפִיהֶן מִתְעָרְבִין:


שתתה מהן בהמה או חיה פסולים – because of the liquid that is their mouths that returns, and the water and spittle that are in their mouths combine/mix.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:3:2)


**מוֹצֶצֶת.** וְאֵין הַמַּשְׁקֶה שֶׁבְּפִיהָ חוֹזֵר לַמַּיִם:


מוצצת (sucks) – but the liquid that is its mouth does not return to the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:3:3)


**מַלֶּקֶת.** לוֹקֶקֶת בִּלְשׁוֹנָהּ וּמִתְעָרֵב הָרֹק שֶׁבִּלְשׁוֹנָהּ עִם הַמַּיִם:


מלקת (laps it up) – it laps/licks up (and lets the water drip back out of its mouth) on its tongue and it combines the spittle that is on its tongue with the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:3:4)


**אַף הַנָּחָשׁ מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהִיא מְקִיאָה.** וְכֵן הֲלָכָה, וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


אף הנחש מפני שהיא מקיאה (and even the snake because it vomits) – And such is the Halakha, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer [who adds the mouse].



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:1)


**הַחוֹשֵׁב עַל מֵי חַטָּאת לִשְׁתּוֹת רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר פּוֹסֵל.** דְּאִפַּסְלוּ לְהוּ בַּמַּחֲשָׁבָה:


החושב על מי חטאת לשתות ר' אליעזר פוסל – for he invalidates it through intention/thought.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:2)


**כְּשֶׁיַּטֶּה.** הֶחָבִית לִשְׁתּוֹת אוֹ לִקַּח מִמֶּנָּה מַיִם לִשְׁתּוֹת. אֲבָל בְּמַחֲשָׁבָה גְרֵידָא לֹא פָסֵיל:


כשיטה – [that he turns up] the barrel to drink, or to take from it water to drink, but mere intention/thought does not invalidate it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:3)


**בְּמַיִם שֶׁאֵינָן מְקֻדָּשִׁין.** שֶׁמִּלְּאָן כְּדֵי לִתֵּן בָּהֶן אֶת הָאֵפֶר וַעֲדַיִן לֹא נָתַן:


במים שאינן מקודשין – that he filled them in order to place in them the ashes, but he did not yet place them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:4)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר כְּשֶׁיַּטֶּה.** וְהָכָא לֹא פָסֵיל לְהוּ בְּמַחֲשָׁבָה. דְּכֵיוָן דְּמַיִם קְדוֹשִׁים הֵן, אִמְּלוֹכֵי מִמְּלִיךְ וְחוֹזֵר בּוֹ מִמַּחֲשַׁבְתּוֹ, הִלְכָּךְ לֹא פָסֵיל רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר עַד שֶׁיַּטֶּה:


רבי אליעזר אומר כשיטה – but here he does not invalidate them with intention, for since they are mixed waters, he was persuaded/reconsidered to change his mind from his intention. Therefore, Rabbi Eliezer does not invalidate until he turns it up [to drink from it].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר כְּשֶׁיִּשְׁתֶּה.** מִפְּנֵי מַשְׁקֶה שֶׁבְּפִיו שֶׁחוֹזֵר וּמִתְעָרֵב עִם הַמַּיִם וּפָסֵיל לְהוּ:


רבי יהושע אומר כשישתה – because of the liquid that is in his mouth when he returns and combines/mixes with the water, and he invalidates them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:6)


**וְאִם גִּרְגֵּר.** לְשׁוֹן גַּרְגֶּרֶת. כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁלֹּא שָׁתָה כְּדֶרֶךְ הַשּׁוֹתִים אֶלָּא שָׁפַךְ הַמַּיִם בִּגְרוֹנוֹ:


ואם גרגר (if he poured it down his throat – as opposed to setting his lips to the vessel) – it is the language of a gullet/throat. That is to say, that he didn’t drink it in the manner of those who drink but rather, poured the water in his throat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:4:7)


**כָּשֵׁר.** דְּלֵיכָּא מַשְׁקֵה פִיו בֶּחָבִית. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בְּתַרְוַיְהוּ:


כשר – for there is no liquid from his mouth in the barrel. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua in both of them.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:5:1)


**שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲשֵׂם תַּקָּלָה לַאֲחֵרִים.** שֶׁיִּגְּעוּ בַטִּיט וְיִטַּמְּאוּ בְמֵי חַטָּאת שֶׁבּוֹ:


שלא יעשם תקלה לאחרים – that they reach the plaster/mud and they will be defiled in the waters of purification that are in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:5:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר בָּטְלוּ.** בַּטִּיט, וְתוּ לֹא מְטַמְּאֵי מֵאַחַר שֶׁגִּבְּלָן:


ר' יהודה אומר בטלו – in the plaster/mud. But further, he does not defile after they have been trampled/stamped [in the mud].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:5:3)


**מֵעֵת לְעֵת.** אִם שְׁחָטָהּ בְּתוֹךְ מֵעֵת לְעֵת שֶׁל שְׁתִיָּה. אֲבָל שָׁהֲתָה טְפֵי מֵהָכִי, נִתְעַכְּלוּ בְמֵעֶיהָ:


מעת לעת – if it was slaughtered within twenty-four hours of its drinking. But it delayed more than this, they became consumed in its intestines.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:5:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר בָּטְלוּ בְמֵעֶיהָ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ שְׁחָטָהּ בְּתוֹךְ מֵעֵת לְעֵת בְּשָׂרָהּ טָהוֹר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה בְּתַרְוַיְהוּ:


רבי יהודה אומר באלו במעיה – and even if he slaughtered it within twenty-four hours, its flesh is pure. But the Halakah is not according to Rabbi Yehuda in both of these.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:1)


**מֵי חַטָּאת.** בִּמְקֻדָּשִׁין אַיְרֵי. אֲבָל מַיִם שֶׁאֵינָן מְקֻדָּשִׁין שָׁרֵי, כִּדְקָתָנֵי סֵיפָא:


מי חטאת – we are speaking of mixed purification waters. But waters that are not mixed are permitted, as it is taught in the concluding clause [of the Mishnah].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:2)


**אֵפֶר חַטָּאת.** אֲפִלּוּ אֵפֶר עַצְמוֹ בְּלֹא מַיִם:


ואפר חטאת – even the ash itself without water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:3)


**לֹא יַעֲבִירֵם בְּנָהָר וּבִסְפִינָה.** דְּגַזּוּר מִשּׁוּם מַעֲשֶׂה בְאָדָם אֶחָד שֶׁהָיָה מַעֲבִיר מֵי חַטָּאת וְאֵפֶר חַטָּאת בִּסְפִינָה בַיַּרְדֵּן וְנִמְצָא כַזַּיִת מֵת תָּחוּב בְּקַרְקָעִיתָהּ שֶׁל סְפִינָה:


לא יעבירם בנהר ובספינה – that a decree was made because of an incident of one person who was taking the waters of purification and the ashes of purification in a boat on the Jordan [River] and it was found that an olive’s bulk of a corpse was inserted in the bottom of the boat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:4)


**וְלֹא יְשִׁיטֵם עַל פְּנֵי הַמַּיִם.** דְּדָמֵי לִסְפִינָה. וְכֵן לֹא יַעֲמֹד בְּצַד זֶה שֶׁל נָהָר וְיִזְרְקֵם לְצַד שֵׁנִי, דְּגַם זֶה דָמֵי קְצָת לִסְפִינָה:


ולא ישיטם על פני המים (he should not float them on the water) – for it is similar to a boat, and similarly, he should not stand on this side of the river and cast them to the other side, and also that this is similar a bit to a boat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:5)


**אֲבָל עוֹבֵר הוּא.** בְּרַגְלָיו:


אבל עובר הוא – on his feet.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:6)


**בַּמַּיִם.** עִם מֵי חַטָּאת שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ:


במים – with the waters of purification that are in his hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:6:7)


**עוֹבֵר הוּא הַטָּהוֹר לְחַטָּאת.** בֵּין בִּסְפִינָה בֵּין שָׁט עַל פְּנֵי הַמַּיִם, וְאֵינוֹ חוֹשֵׁשׁ. וְכֵן נוֹשֵׂא בְיָדוֹ אוֹ מֵשִׁיט עַל פְּנֵי הַמַּיִם כְּלִי שֶׁהוּא טָהוֹר לָתֵת בּוֹ מֵי חַטָּאת כְּשֶׁהוּא רֵיקָם, אוֹ שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ מַיִם שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא נִתְקַדְּשׁוּ בְאֵפֶר פָּרָה. שֶׁלֹּא גָזְרוּ אֶלָּא עַל הַמַּיִם הַמְקֻדָּשִׁים וְעַל הָאֵפֶר בִּלְבַד, לֹא עַל הָאָדָם וְהַכֵּלִים הַטְּהוֹרִים (ומים שאינן מקדשין):


עובר הוא הטהור לחטאת – whether in a boat, whether he floats over the water a vessel which is pure/clean to place in it the waters of purification when they are empty, or that he has water in it water that had not yet been mixed with the ashes of the [Red] Heifer. For they did not decree other than on the mixed waters and on the ashes alone, not on the man or the pure vessels (and the waters that are not mixed).



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:7:1)


**אֵפֶר כָּשֵׁר.** אֵפֶר פָּרָה הַכָּשֵׁר לְהַזָּאָה:


אפר כשר – the ashes of a [Red] Heifer that are suitable/appropriate for sprinkling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:7:2)


**שֶׁנִּתְעָרֵב בְּאֵפֶר מִקְלֶה.** בְּאֵפֶר כִּירָה:


שנתערב באפר מקלה (that were mixed with wood-ashes) the ashes of a portable stove on feet with caves for two pots.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:7:3)


**הוֹלְכִים אַחַר הָרֹב לְטַמֵּא.** אִם רֹב אֵפֶר פָּרָה, מְטַמֵּא. אִם רֹב אֵפֶר מִקְלֶה, אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא:


הולכים אחר הרוב לטמא – if the majority are the ashes of the [Red] Heifer, it defiles, [but] if the majority is the ashes of a portable stove on feet with caves for two pots, it does not defile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:7:4)


**וְאֵין מְקַדְּשִׁין בּוֹ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ רֹב אֵפֶר חַטָּאת, אֵין מְקַדְּשִׁין בּוֹ, הוֹאִיל וְנִתְעָרֵב בּוֹ אֵפֶר כִּירָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ כָל שֶׁהוּא:


ואין מקדשין בו- even if the majority are the ashes of lustration/expiation, but they don’t mix with it, since it was combined with the ashes of a portable stove on feet with caves for two pots and even a little bit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:7:5)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר מְקַדְּשִׁין בְּכֻלָּן.** דְּסָבַר רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר לֹא בָעִינַן שִׁעוּרָא בְּהַזָּאָה, וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר דְּלֵיתָא פוּרְתָּא מֵאֵפֶר חַטָּאת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ר' אליעזר אומר מקדשין בכולן – for Rabbi Eliezer holds that we don’t require a measurement for sprinkling, and it is impossible that there isn’t a little bit from the ashes of lustration/expiation. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:8:1)


**מֵי חַטָּאת שֶׁנִּפְסְלוּ.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּנּוּ מַרְאֵיהֶן שֶׁלֹּא מֵחֲמַת עַצְמָן, וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָזֶה מִן הַדְּבָרִים שֶׁפּוֹסְלִים מֵי חַטָּאת:


מי חטאת שנפסלו – as for example, that their appearance changed not on account of themselves, and in a similar manner , from the things that invalidate the waters of purification/expiation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:8:2)


**מְטַמְּאִים אֶת הָאָדָם הַטָּהוֹר לִתְרוּמָה.** בֵּין שֶׁנָּגַע בָּהֶן בְּיָדָיו בֵּין שֶׁנָּגַע בָּהֶן בְּגוּפוֹ, דִּכְמֵי חַטָּאת כְּשֵׁרִים חֲשִׁיבֵי. וְטֻמְאָה זוֹ דְּרַבָּנָן הִיא, דְּמִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא כֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּפְסְלוּ פָּרְחָה מֵהֶן טֻמְאָתָן הַחֲמוּרָה. וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי תָּנֵי מְטַמְּאִין אֶת הָאָדָם


מטמאין את – the person (see also Tractate Kelim, Chapter 1, Mishnayot 1-2).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:8:3)


**הַטָּהוֹר לִתְרוּמָה.** דְּמַשְׁמַע דַּוְקָא לִתְרוּמָה אֲבָל לֹא לְחֻלִּין:


הטהור לתרומה – whether he touched them with his hands or whether he touched them with his body, they are considered like suitable/appropriate like waters of expiation/purification, and this defilement is from the Rabbis, for from the Torah, since they were made unfit/invalid, their greater defilement spread all over/blossomed, and because of this, it (i.e., the Mishnah text) teaches that “it renders unclean the person who is clean for heave-offering,” which implies especially for heave-offering but not for non-holy offerings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:8:4)


**וְאֶת הַטָּהוֹר לְחַטָּאת לֹא בְיָדָיו.** כְּלוֹמַר אֵין מְטַמְּאִין הַטָּהוֹר לְחַטָּאת בֵּין נָגַע בָּהֶן בְּיָדָיו בֵּין נָגַע בָּהֶן בְּגוּפוֹ, דִּכְמֵי חַטָּאת כְּשֵׁרִים חֲשִׁיבֵי, וּנְהִי דְהַנּוֹשֵׂא מֵי חַטָּאת שֶׁיֶּשׁ בָּהֶן כְּדֵי הַזָּיָה אוֹ נוֹגֵעַ בָּהֶן, טָמֵא לְכָל מִלֵּי, לְחַטָּאת מִיהָא טָהוֹר:


ואת הטהור לחטאת לא בידיו – that is to say, it does not defile the pure person for a sin/purification offering whether he touched them with his hands or whether he touched them with his body, for the they are considered appropriate/suitable like the waters of purification/expiation, and these that one carries the waters of expiation that have in them [enough] in order for sprinkling or he touches them, he is impure for all things, but for a sin-offering/expiation, however, he is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:8:5)


**נִטְמְאוּ מְטַמְּאִים אֶת הַטָּהוֹר לִתְרוּמָה.** כְּמֵי חַטָּאת כְּשֵׁרִים. אֲבָל אֶת הַטָּהוֹר לְחַטָּאת מְטַמְּאִים אוֹתוֹ אִם נָגַע בְּיָדָיו, דְּכֵיוָן דְּנִטְמְאוּ לֹא גְרִיעֵי מִשְּׁאָר אֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִים טְמֵאִים דִּמְטַמְּאִים אֶת הַיָּדַיִם, וְקַיְמָא לָן דִּבְטָהֳרַת מֵי חַטָּאת מִי שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ יָדָיו נִטְמָא גוּפוֹ:


נטמאו מטמאין את הטהור לתורמה – like the waters of suitable/acceptable sin-offerings/expiation offerings, but a person who is clean for the purification offering, it defiles him if he touched it with his hands, for since they were defiled, they are not worse than the rest of the impure foods and liquids that defile the hands, for we hold that the purification of the waters of purification/sin-offering, when his hands were defiled/made unclean, his body was made unclean.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:9:1)


**אֵפֶר כָּשֵׁר שֶׁנְּתָנוֹ עַל גַּבֵּי הַמַּיִם שֶׁאֵינָם רְאוּיִים לְקַדֵּשׁ.** הָוֵי דִינָא כְּדִין מֵי חַטָּאת שֶׁנִּפְסְלוּ:


אפר כשר שנתנו על גבי מים שאינם ראוים לקדש – their law is like the law of the waters of purification/sin-offering that were invalidated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/9:9:2)


**מְטַמְּאִין אֶת הַטָּהוֹר לִתְרוּמָה בְיָדָיו וּבְגוּפוֹ.** כְּדִין כָּל שְׁאָר מֵי חַטָּאת הַכְּשֵׁרִים. וְאֵין מְטַמְּאִים הַטָּהוֹר לְחַטָּאת, שֶׁאֵין מֵי חַטָּאת מְטַמְּאִין הַנּוֹשְׂאָן וְהַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּהֶן אֶלָּא לִשְׁאָר טֻמְאוֹת, לֹא לְטָהֳרַת מֵי חַטָּאת:


מטמאין את הטהור לתרומה בידיו ובגופו – like the law of all the rest of the suitable/appropriate waters of expiation/sin-offering, but they do not defile the clean person for a sin-offering, for the waters of expiation/sin-offering do not defile the person who carries them, and the person who touches them, other than the rest of the defilements, not for the purification of the waters of expiation/sin-offering.



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:1:1)


**כֹּל הָרָאוּי לִטַּמֵּא מִדְרָס.** כְּגוֹן כֵּלִים הַמְיֻחָדִים לְמִשְׁכָּב וּמוֹשָׁב וּמֶרְכָּב:


כל הראוי לטמא מדרס (all that is appropriate to be made ritually impure by treading – by someone with gonorrhea, a woman in flux, a woman after childbirth and a menstruating woman) – as for example, designated vessels for lying, sitting and riding.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:1:2)


**מַדָּף לְחַטָּאת.** אִם הַטָּהוֹר לְמֵי חַטָּאת הֱסִיטָן. אֲפִלּוּ הֵן טְהוֹרִין לִתְרוּמָה נִטְמָא הַמְּסִיטָם כְּאִלּוּ הֵן טְמֵאִים מִדְרָס. וְחֻמְרָא בְעָלְמָא הִיא כִּדְאָמְרִינַן [בַּחֲגִיגָה יח] בִּגְדֵי אוֹכְלֵי תְרוּמָה מִדְרָס לַקֹּדֶשׁ. וְקָרֵי לֵיהּ מַדָּף, לְפִי שֶׁהִיא חֻמְרָא דְרַבָּנָן בְּעָלְמָא. לְשׁוֹן קוֹל עָלֶה נִדָּף (ויקרא כו):


מדף לחטאת (indirect contact for conferring ritual impurity – based upon Rabbinic decree – for objects above a man with gonorrhea for the purification rite) – if the pure person with the waters for purification/sin-offering shook them [so as to move it from its place]. Even they are pure for heave-offering [for a Kohen], those who shake them are defiled as if they are defiled through treading, and it is a mere stringency as we state [in Tractate Hagigah 18b -Mishnah Hagigah 2:7]: "בגדי אוכלי תרומה מדרס לקודש"/”The clothing of those who eat heave offering is in the status of Midras/treading uncleanness for those who eat Holy Things {i.e., officiating priests). We call it מדף/indirect contact by shaking, because it is a mere stringency of the Rabbis. It is he language of (Leviticus 26:36): “The sound of a driven leaf [shall put them to flight].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:1:3)


**וְאָדָם כַּיּוֹצֵא בָהֶן.** שֶׁאִם הַטָּהוֹר לְחַטָּאת הֵסִיט אָדָם הַטָּהוֹר לִתְרוּמָה, נִטְמָא לְחַטָּאת, שֶׁהַטְּהוֹרִים לִתְרוּמָה נֶחְשָׁבִים כְּזָבִים לְגַבֵּי חַטָּאת:


ואדם כיוצא בהן – if a person who is clean/pure for a purification/sin-offering shook a person who is pure for heave-offering, the purification/sin-offering is defiled and those who are pure for heave-offering are considered as men who have gonorrhea/a flux regarding the sin-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:1:4)


**וְכֹל הָרָאוּי לִטַּמֵּא טְמֵא מֵת.** כְּגוֹן כֵּלִים דְּלָאו בְּנֵי מִדְרָס:


וכל הראוי ליטמא טמא מת - as for example, vessels that are not trodden subject to Levitical uncleanness through the immediate contact of someone with gonorrhea by treading or leaning against.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:1:5)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר אֵינוֹ מַדָּף.** וְאִם הֱסִיטָן לֹא נִטְמָא לְחַטָּאת:


ר' אליעזר אומר אינו מדף – if he shook them it was not defiled for the purification/sin-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:1:6)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר מַדָּף.** אִם הֵסִיט אֲפִלּוּ הַטְּהוֹרִים, נִטְמָא לְחַטָּאת. וְרַבָּנָן פְּלִיגֵי וּמְחַלְּקִים בֵּין טְמֵאִים לִטְהוֹרִים. וְשָׁלֹשׁ מַחֲלוֹקוֹת בַּדָּבָר. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


ר' יהושע אומר מדף – if he shook them, even those that are pure are defiled for the purification/sin-offering. But the Rabbis dispute and divide it between those that are impure and those that are pure. There are three disputes in this matter, and the Halakha is according to the Sages (i.e., that which is [actually] unclean [because of the corpse] is [regarded as unclean with indirect contact/Maddaf uncleanness, and that which is [actually] clean is not [regarded as] unclean with Maddaf/indirect contact uncleanness).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:2:1)
