**שֶׁנָּגַע בְּמַדָּף טָמֵא.** עֶלְיוֹנוֹ שֶׁל זָב קָרוּי מַדָּף, שֶׁאִם הָיוּ עַל גַּבֵּי הַזָּב עֶשֶׂר מִכְסָאוֹת זוֹ עַל גַּבֵּי זוֹ כֻּלָּן טְמֵאִין מַדָּף, אֲפִלּוּ הָעֶלְיוֹנָה שֶׁלֹּא נָגַע בָּהּ הַזָּב. כְּשֵׁם שֶׁעֶשֶׂר מַצָּעוֹת זוֹ עַל גַּבֵּי זוֹ וְשָׁכַב הַזָּב בָּעֶלְיוֹנָה כֻּלָּן טְמֵאוֹת דְּאוֹרַיְתָא מִשּׁוּם מִשְׁכָּב וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נָגַע בַּתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת, כָּךְ הַמִּכְסָאוֹת הָעֶלְיוֹנוֹת שֶׁעַל גַּבֵּי הַזָּב טְמֵאִים דְּרַבָּנָן מִשּׁוּם מַדָּף וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נָגַע בָּהֶן. וְאִיכָּא בֵין מִשְׁכָּב לְמַדָּף, שֶׁהַמִּשְׁכָּב נַעֲשֶׂה אַב הַטֻּמְאָה לְטַמֵּא אָדָם וְכֵלִים, וְהַמַּדָּף אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא וְלַד הַטֻּמְאָה לְטַמֵּא אֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִים, אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם חֻמְרָא דְחַטָּאת, אָדָם וְכֵלִים שֶׁנָּגְעוּ בְמַדָּף טְמֵאִים לְחַטָּאת:


שנגע במדף טמא – The upper part of a person with gonorrhea is called a מדף /Maddaf for if there were on top of a person with gonorrhea tent-covers one on top of another, all of them are impure through indirect contact for conferring ritual impurity, even the uppermost one that the person with gonorrhea did not have contact with. Just as [the case of] ten mattresses/beds one on top of the other and a person with gonorrhea lay down on the uppermost one, all of them are ritually impure according to the Torah because of lying, and even though he didn’t come in contact/touch the lowest ones, so also the upper tent-covers that are upon the person with gonorrhea, all of them are impure according to the Rabbis because of indirect contact/מדף and מeven though he did not come in contact with them. But there is a distinction between lying to indirect contact by shaking/מדף, that the person [who defiles] by lying becomes a primary source of ritual uncleanness/אב הטומאה to defile people and vessels, but the מדף/indirect contact through shaking is not other than a secondary source of impurity/ולד הטומאה to defile food and liquids but because of the stringency of the purification/sin-offering, people and vessels that came in contact in an indirect manner through shaking/מדף are impure for a purification/sin-offering (see also Tractate Eduyot, Chapter 6, Mishnah 2 and Tractate Zavim, Chapter 4, Mishnah 6).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:2:2)


**לָגִין שֶׁל חַטָּאת.** כְּלִי שֶׁטֶף הוּא וְרוֹצֶה לְהַכְנִיס בּוֹ מֵי חַטָּאת:


לגין של חטאת (a flagon/bottle – smaller than a pitcher and larger than a cup of purification water) – it is a vessel that only requires rinsing to be restored to Levitical cleanness, and he wants to put in it the purification water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:2:3)


**שֶׁנָּגַע בְּמַדָּף טָמֵא.** וְצָרִיךְ טְבִילָה:


שנגע במדף, טמא – and it requires ritual immersion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:2:4)


**שֶׁנָּגַע בְּאֳכָלִין וּמַשְׁקִין.** וַאֲפִלּוּ שֶׁהֵן טְהוֹרִין לִתְרוּמָה וּלְקֹדֶשׁ:


שנגע באוכלין ומשקין – and even if they are ritually pure for heave-offering [for Kohanim] and for Holy Things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:2:5)


**בְּיָדוֹ טָמֵא.** כְּדִין אֳכָלִין וּמַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִים שֶׁהֵן מְטַמְּאִין אֶת הַיָּדַיִם, שֶׁהַטָּהוֹר לִתְרוּמָה וּלְקֹדֶשׁ פּוֹסֵל בְּחַטָּאת, וְעוֹד יֵשׁ חֻמְרָא יְתֵרָה גַבֵּי חַטָּאת, דְּנִטְמָא [יָדָיו נִטְמָא] גוּפוֹ, וְהִלְכָּךְ כְּשֶׁנָּגַע בְּיָדוֹ בְּאֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִין טָמֵא:


בידו טמא – according to the law of impure food and liquids which defile the hands, that someone who is ritually pure for heave-offering and for Holy Things is invalid for purification/sin-offering, and further, there is an additional stringency regarding the purification/sin-offering that its body was defiled. And therefore, when it touched his hand with food and liquids it is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:2:6)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ מְטַמֵּא.** דְּגָזַר הֶסֵּט אַטּוּ נְגִיעָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


ר' יהושע מטמא – for he decreed that shaking an object so as to move it from place to place is because of contact/touching. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:3:1)


**קָלָל שֶׁל חַטָּאת.** כְּלִי חֶרֶס שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ אֵפֶר הַפָּרָה. תַּרְגּוּם וְכַדָּהּ עַל שִׁכְמָהּ (בראשית כד), וְקוּלְתַהּ עַל כַּתְפַּהּ:


קלל של חטאת (the vessel containing the ashes for lustration) – an earthenware vessel that has in the ashes of the [Red] Heifer. The Aramaic translation [of קלל] is from (Genesis 24:15): "וכדה על שכמה"/ “[Rebekah came out] with her jar on her shoulder,” the vessel/jar on her shoulder.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:3:2)


**שֶׁנָּגַע בְּשֶׁרֶץ טָהוֹר.** דְּאֵין כְּלִי חֶרֶס מִטַּמֵּא מִגַּבּוֹ:


שנגע בשרץ טהור – for an earthenware vessel does not become defiled from what is on top of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:3:3)


**נְתָנוֹ עַל גַּבָּיו.** שֶׁנָּתַן הַקָּלָל עַל גַּבֵּי הַשֶּׁרֶץ:


שנתנו כל גביו -that he placed the pitcher/jar on top of the insect.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:3:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַמְּאִין.** מִשּׁוּם דְּמֻנָּח בְּמָקוֹם טָמֵא, וְהַתּוֹרָה אָמְרָה וְהִנִּיחַ מִחוּץ לַמַּחֲנֶה בְּמָקוֹם טָהוֹר. וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר סָבַר הוֹאִיל וְהַכְּלִי שֶׁהָאֵפֶר בְּתוֹכוֹ טָהוֹר הוּא, שַׁפִּיר קָרִינַן בֵּיהּ בְּמָקוֹם טָהוֹר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


וחכמים מטמאין – because it was placed in an impure place, and the Torah stated (Numbers 19:9): “[A man who is pure shall gather up the ashes of the cow] and deposit them outside the camp in a pure place,” but Rabbi Eliezer holds that since the vessel and the ashes that are within it are ritually pure, it well that we call it, “a pure place.” But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:3:5)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי מְטַהֵר.** בְּמֻנָּח עַל גַּבֵּי אֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִים וְכִתְבֵי הַקֹּדֶשׁ, כֵּיוָן דִּמְקוֹמוֹ טָהוֹר מֵאַב הַטֻּמְאָה:


ר' יוסי מטהר – through placing it (i.e., the vessel containing ashes for lustration) on top of food and liquids and Holy Scrolls, since it’s place is ritually pure from a primary source of ritual uncleanness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:3:6)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַמְּאִים.** דְּטָהוֹר מִכָּל טֻמְאָה בָּעִינַן. וְכִתְבֵי הַקֹּדֶשׁ רַבָּנָן גָּזְרוּ בְהוּ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מְטַמְּאִין אֶת הַיָּדַיִם. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מטמאים – for we require that it be ritually pure from all defilement, and [regarding] Holy Scrolls, the Rabbis decreed that they defile the hands. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:1)


**שֶׁנָּגַע בְּתַנּוּר.** וַאֲפִלּוּ הוּא טָהוֹר לַקֹּדֶשׁ, אִם נָגַע בּוֹ הַטָּהוֹר לְחַטָּאת:


שנגע בתנור – and even if it is ritually pure for Holy Things, if the person who was clean for the purification rite touched the oven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:2)


**טָמֵא.** וְצָרִיךְ טְבִילָה לְחַטָּאת, שֶׁהַטָּהוֹר לַקֹּדֶשׁ פּוֹסֵל בְּחַטָּאת. וְדַוְקָא שֶׁנָּגַע בְּיָדוֹ, דְּנִטְמְאוּ יָדָיו וְנִטְמָא גוּפוֹ, אֲבָל בְּרַגְלוֹ טָהוֹר, דִּבְיָדוֹ עַבוּד מַעֲלָה, בְּרַגְלוֹ לֹא עַבוּד מַעֲלָה, דּוּמְיָא דָּאֳכָלִין וּמַשְׁקִין דִּלְעֵיל:


טמא – and he requires ritual immersion for the purification/sin-offering, for the person who is clean for Holy Things invalidates the purification offering, and especially that he touched it with his hand, that his hands became defiled and his body became defiled. But by his foot, he is ritually pure, that his hand is served above, but his foot is not served above, similar to food and drink above (in the previous Mishnah).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:3)


**וְהַלָּגִין בְּתוֹכוֹ.** כְּלִי שֶׁהִכְנִיס בּוֹ מֵי חַטָּאת נָתוּן בְּתוֹךְ יָדוֹ:


והלגין בתוכו – a vessel that he put into it the waters of purification which is placed within his hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:4)


**וְכֵן הָאֵסֶל.** מוֹט שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים שְׁנֵי כַדִּין בִּשְׁנֵי רָאשָׁיו:


וכן האסל (and similarly a yoke/pole carried on one or two shoulders) – a balancing pole/staff that they place wo pitchers on its two heads.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:5)


**וּבוֹ שְׁנֵי קְלָלוֹת.** שְׁנֵי כֵלִים שֶׁהָאֵפֶר נָתוּן בָּהֶם, שֶׁקָּלָל אֶחָד קָשׁוּר בְּרֹאשׁ הַמּוֹט הָאֶחָד, וְקָלָל אֶחָד קָשׁוּר בְּרֹאשׁוֹ הַשֵּׁנִי, וְהַשְׁתָּא הָאָדָם וְהָאֵסֶל עַל גַּבֵּי תַנּוּר, וְהַלָּגִין וְהַקָּלָל שֶׁבָּהֶן הַמַּיִם וְהָאֵפֶר חוּץ לַתַּנּוּר:


ובו שתי קללות – two vessels that the ashes are placed in them, for one pitcher is tied to the head of the balancing pole/staff, and the other pitcher is tied on its second head, but now the person and the yoke/pole are on top of the oven, and the bottle/flagon and the pitcher that the water and the ashes are in them are outside of the oven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:6)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מְטַהֵר.** שֶׁאֵין הַכְּלִי שֶׁבּוֹ הָאֵפֶר עוֹמֵד עַל גַּבֵּי הַתַּנּוּר, וּבְמָקוֹם טָהוֹר הוּא:


ר' עקיבא מטהר – that the vessel that contains the ashes does not stand on top of the oven, as it is in a pure place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:4:7)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַמְּאִין.** הוֹאִיל וְאָדָם הַנּוֹשְׂאוֹ עוֹמֵד עַל גַּבֵּי תַנּוּר, חָשְׁבִינַן לַכְּלִי כְּאִלּוּ הוּא עוֹמֵד עַל גַּבֵּי הַתַּנּוּר וּבְמָקוֹם טָמֵא הוּא מֻנָּח. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


וחכמים מטמאין – since he person who carries I is standing on top of the oven, we consider the vessel as if it stands on top of the oven and it is placed in a ritually impure place. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Akiva (see also Talmud Shabbat 97b)



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:5:1)


**הָיָה עוֹמֵד חוּץ לַתַּנּוּר.** שֶׁהַתַּנּוּר מַפְסִיק בֵּינוֹ לַכֹּתֶל, וְחַלּוֹן בַּכֹּתֶל וּבוֹ הַלָּגִין שֶׁל מֵי חַטָּאת:


היה עומד חוץ לתנור – that the oven forms a partition between himself to the wall, and the window of the wall and in is the flagon of the waters of lustration.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:5:2)


**וְהֶעֱבִירוֹ עַל פִּי הַתַּנּוּר.** שֶׁכָּל תַּנּוּרֵיהֶן פִּיהֶן לְמַעְלָה:


והעבירו על פי התנור – for all of their ovens their mouths are upward.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:5:3)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מְטַמֵּא.** דְּסָבַר קְלוּטָה כְמִי שֶׁהֻנְּחָה דָמְיָא וּבְשָׁעָה שֶׁהֶעֱבִירוֹ עַל פִּי הַתַּנּוּר הֲרֵי הִיא כְאִלּוּ הִנִּיחוֹ בְתוֹכוֹ וְנִטְמָא:


ר' עקיבא מטמא – for he holds that something that is snatched in the air is like it was placed there, and at the time that it passed it over the mouth of the oven, it is as it he placed it inside it and it became defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:5:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִין.** סָבְרֵי לֹא אָמְרִינַן קְלוּטָה כְּמִי שֶׁהֻנְּחָה דָּמְיָא. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מטהרין – for they hold that we don’t say that something that is snatched in the air is not like it was placed there. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:5:5)


**וּבְיָדוֹ כְּלִי רֵיקָם.** דְּלֹא בָעֵינָא וְהִנִּיחַ בְּמָקוֹם טָהוֹר אֶלָּא בְאֵפֶר הַפָּרָה וּבְמַיִם הַמְקֻדָּשִׁין עַל יְדֵי הָאֵפֶר:


ובידו כלי ריקם – that is not as it is and he placed it in a pure place but the ashes of the [Red] Heifer and the mixed water next to the ashes.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:1)


**שֶׁל חַטָּאת טָמֵא.** דְּקֹדֶשׁ וּתְרוּמָה טְמֵאִים הֵן אֵצֶל חַטָּאת:


של חטאת טמא – that the Holy Things and the heave-offering are ritually impure next to the [waters] of purification.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:2)


**שְׁנֵיהֶן בִּשְׁתֵּי יָדָיו.** שֶׁנּוֹשֵׂא שֶׁל חַטָּאת בְּיָדוֹ אַחַת וְשֶׁל קֹדֶשׁ אוֹ שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה בְּיָדוֹ הָאַחֶרֶת:


שניהם בשתי ידיו – that the person who carries the purification waters in his one hand and that of Holy Things or of heave-offering in his other hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:3)


**שְׁנֵיהֶן טְמֵאִים.** שֶׁל חַטָּאת מִשּׁוּם דְּנָגַע בְּשֶׁל תְּרוּמָה בְּיָדוֹ וְנִטְמְאוּ יָדָיו נִטְמָא גוּפוֹ וּמְטַמֵּא מֵי חַטָּאת, וְשֶׁל תְּרוּמָה טָמֵא מִשּׁוּם נוֹשֵׂא מֵי חַטָּאת:


שניהן טמאים – of the purification waters because it touched that of heave-offering in his hand and when his hands were defiled, his body was defiled and it defiles the waters of lustration, and that of heave offering is impure because he carries the waters of lustration.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:4)


**בִּשְׁנֵי נְיָרוֹת.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָאָדָם טָמֵא מִשּׁוּם נוֹשֵׂא מֵי חַטָּאת, מִכָּל מָקוֹם הַתְּרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה דְּהָא מַפְסִיק נְיָר וְאֵינוֹ נוֹגֵעַ בַּתְּרוּמָה בְיָדוֹ:


בשני ניירות – but even though the person is impure because he carries the waters of lustration, nevertheless the heave-offering is pure for the paper interposes and doesn’t touch the heave-offering that is in his hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:5)


**שֶׁל חַטָּאת בִּנְיָר וְשֶׁל תְּרוּמָה בְּיָדוֹ שְׁנֵיהֶן טְמֵאִין.** דְּכִי נָגַע בְּיָדוֹ בְּשֶׁל תְּרוּמָה נִטְמָא לְחַטָּאת. וּנְיָר דִּבְשֶׁל חַטָּאת אֵינוֹ חוֹצֵץ, דְּאֵין מוֹנִין רִאשׁוֹן וְשֵׁנִי לְחַטָּאת, וְנִטְמָא שֶׁל חַטָּאת, וְהָאָדָם טָמֵא מִשּׁוּם נוֹשֵׂא מֵי חַטָּאת וְטִמֵּא אֶת שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה שֶׁנָּגַע בּוֹ בְּיָדוֹ. אֲבָל שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה בַּנְּיָר, חוֹצֵץ בּוֹ הַנְּיָר, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּנָגַע בְּשֶׁל חַטָּאת בְּיָדוֹ, שְׁנֵיהֶם טְהוֹרִים, דְּאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא טָמֵא מִשּׁוּם נוֹשֵׂא מֵי חַטָּאת, מִכָּל מָקוֹם אֵין מֵי חַטָּאת חוֹזְרִים וּמִטַּמְּאִים בַּטֻּמְאָה הַבָּאָה לָאָדָם מֵחֲמַת עַצְמָן:


של חטאת בנייר ושל תרומה בידו שניהן טמאין – for when it touched his hand, that of heave-offering was defiled for the purification offering. But the paper that is of the purification/sin-offering does not interpose, for we don’t count first-degree and second-degree [ritual impurity] for purification, and the purification offering was defiled, and a person is impure because he is carrying the waters of lustration and he defiled that of the heave-offering when he touched it with his hand. But heave-offering that is [surrounded by] a piece of paper, the paper interposes for it and even though it touched that of the purification offering in his hand, both of them are pure, for even though he is impure because he carries the waters of purification, nevertheless, the waters of purification do not return and become defiled through a defilement that comes to a person on account of themselves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:6)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר שֶׁל חַטָּאת טָמֵא.** רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דְּאָמַר בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא טָהוֹר לְחַטָּאת שֶׁהֵסִיט אֶת הַמַּפְתֵּחַ שֶׁהוּא טָהוֹר לִתְרוּמָה, טָמֵא, שֶׁמָּא יִשְׁכַּח וְיָסִיט אֶת הַדָּבָר הַטָּמֵא. וְהָכָא נַמִּי אֲפִלּוּ בִשְׁנֵי נְיָרוֹת טָמֵא, דְּמַה שֶּׁהוּא נוֹשֵׂא אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ נוֹגֵעַ בָּהּ הָוֵי מַדָּף לַחַטָּאת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


ר' יהושע אומר של חטאת טמא – Rabbi Yehoshua according to his reasoning who stated in the Tosefta (Tractate Parah, Chapter 10, Halakha 8) that someone who is pure for the purification waters that moved the key which is pure for heave-offering, lest he forget and move the unclean thing. But here also, even with two pieces of paper it is impure, for when he is carrying the heave-offering, even though he doesn’t touch it, it is a מדף/indirect contact for the purification waters. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehoshua.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:7)


**הָיוּ נְתוּנִים עַל גַּבֵּי הָאָרֶץ וְנָגַע בָּהֶם.** שֶׁנָּתַן יָדוֹ אַחַת עַל שֶׁל חַטָּאת וְיָדוֹ אַחַת עַל שֶׁל קֹדֶשׁ אוֹ שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה:


היו נתונין על גבי הארץ ונגע בהם – that he placed one hand on that of the purification waters and the other hand on that of Holy Things or of heave offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:8)


**שֶׁל חַטָּאת טָמֵא.** דְּנִטְמָא הָאָדָם בְּלָגִין שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה וְטִמֵּא אֶת שֶׁל חַטָּאת:


של חטאת טמא – that the person became defiled through the bottle/flagon of heave-offering and he defiled that of the purification offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:9)


**וְשֶׁל קֹדֶשׁ וְשֶׁל תְּרוּמָה טְהוֹרִים.** שֶׁהֲרֵי הָאָדָם טָהוֹר לְקֹדֶשׁ וְלִתְרוּמָה, דְּלֹא נָגַע בַּמֵּי חַטָּאת אֶלָּא בַלָּגִין:


ושל קודש ושל תרומה טהורים – for a person is pure for Holy Things and for heave-offering, for he did not touch the waters of lustration other than the bottle/flagon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 10:6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/10:6:10)


**הֱסִיטָן רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ מְטַמֵּא.** לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דְּאָמַר טָהוֹר לְחַטָּאת שֶׁהֵסִיט אֶת הַמַּפְתֵּחַ שֶׁהוּא טָהוֹר לִתְרוּמָה, טָמֵא. וְלֵית הִלְכָתָא כְּוָתֵיהּ:


הסיטן, ר' יהושע מטמא – for according to his reasoning that he said that a person who is pure for the purification offering who moved the key which is pure for heave-offering is impure. But the Halakha is not according to him.



## Chapter 11


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:1:1)


**צְלוֹחִית.** שֶׁל מֵי חַטָּאת:


צלוחית – [a flask with a wide belly and a narrow neck] of water of purification.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:1:2)


**וּבָא וּמְצָאָהּ מְכֻסָּה פְּסוּלָה.** שֶׁאֲנִי אוֹמֵר אָדָם נִכְנָס לְשָׁם וְכִסָּה אוֹתָהּ, וְרֹב בְּנֵי אָדָם אֵין טְהוֹרִין לְחַטָּאת:


ובא ומצאה מכוסה פסולה – that I say that a person entered to there and covered it, most people are not ritually pure for the purification offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:1:3)


**אִם יְכוֹלָה חֻלְדָּה לִשְׁתּוֹת הֵימֶנָּה פְּסוּלָה.** אֲבָל אִם אֵינָהּ יְכוֹלָה לִשְׁתּוֹת לֹא פָסְלִינַן לְהוּ מִטַּעַם שֶׁמָּא אָדָם טָמֵא נִכְנַס וְגִלָּה אוֹתָהּ, דְּאֵין דֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם לְגַלּוֹת אֶלָּא לְכַסּוֹת מִפְּנֵי הַשְּׁרָצִים, אֲבָל שְׁרָצִים דַּרְכָּן לְגַלּוֹת וְאֵין דַּרְכָּן לְכַסּוֹת. וְדַוְקָא מְכֻסָּה וּמְצָאָהּ מְגֻלָּה חָיְשִׁינַן לְחֻלְדָּה. אֲבָל מְגֻלָּה וּמְצָאָהּ מְגֻלָּה כְּמוֹ שֶׁהִנִּיחָהּ, לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁחָשׁוּ לִסְפֵק מַיִם מְגֻלִּין, דַּחֲמִירָא סַכַּנְתָּא מֵאִסּוּרָא:


אם יכולה חולדה לשתות ממנה פסולה – but if it (i.e., the weasel) is not able to drink we do not invalidate it for the reason that perhaps an unclean/impure man entered and uncovered it, for it is not the manner of people to uncover but rather to cover because of the insects, but insects/moving creature, it is their manner to uncover/reveal but it is not their manner to cover. But specifically, something covered and he found it uncovered, we are concerned for a weasel, but something that is uncovered and he found it uncovered like he left it, we are not concerned, even though they were suspect to supply revealed/uncovered water, for danger is more stringent than a prohibition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:1:4)


**אוֹ נָחָשׁ לְדִבְרֵי רַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל.** דְּאָמַר לְעֵיל, אַף הַנָּחָשׁ פּוֹסֵל מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא מֵקִיא:


או נחש לדברי רבן גמליאל – who said above (in Tractate Parah, Chapter 9, Mishnah 3) that even the snake invalidates it because it vomits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:1:5)


**הַחַטָּאת.** אֵפֶר חַטָּאת אוֹ מַיִם הַמְקֻדָּשִׁים שֶׁהָיוּ בְאֹהֶל הַמֵּת בְּתוֹךְ כְּלִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ עָלָיו צָמִיד פָּתִיל:


החטאת – the ashes of the purification rite or the mixed waters [with ashes] that were in the tent of a corpse within a vessel that has upon it a tight-fitting lid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:1:6)


**אֵינָן נִצּוֹלִין.** וְנִפְסָלִין. מִדִּכְתִיב וְהִנִּיחַ בְּמָקוֹם טָהוֹר וְהַאי לָאו מָקוֹם טָהוֹר הוּא. אֲבָל כֵּלִים הָעוֹמְדִים לְהַנִּיחַ בָּהֶן מֵי חַטָּאת אוֹ אֵפֶר חַטָּאת, וְכֵן אֵזוֹב הַמְתֻקָּן לְהַזּוֹת בּוֹ, נִצּוֹלִין בְּצָמִיד פָּתִיל:


אינן נצולין – and are defiled. From what is written (Numbers 19:9): “[A man who is pure shall gather up the ashes of the cow] and deposit them outside the camp in a pure place,” but this is not a pure/clean place. But vessels that stand to place in them the waters of purification or the ashes of purification/sin-offering, and similarly the hyssop that is prepared to sprinkle on it, are protected by a tight-fitting lid.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:1)


**כָּל הַסְּפֵק טָהוֹר לִתְרוּמָה.** כָּל סָפֵק שֶׁאִם נוֹלָד אוֹתוֹ סָפֵק בִּתְרוּמָה הַתְּרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה, אִם נוֹלָד כַּיּוֹצֵא בוֹ בְּחַטָּאת, טָהוֹר:


כל הספק טהור לתרומה – any matter of doubt that if it that same doubt appeared in the heave-offering, the heave offering is pure, if it appeared in a similar manner in the purification offering, it is pure (see also Tractate Taharot, Chapter 4, Mishnah 2).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:2)


**וְכָל הַתָּלוּי לִתְרוּמָה.** וְכָל סָפֵק שֶׁאִם נוֹלָד בִּתְרוּמָה תּוֹלִין לֹא אוֹכְלִין וְלֹא שׂוֹרְפִין, אִם נוֹלָד כַּיּוֹצֵא בוֹ בְּחַטָּאת, יִשָּׁפֵךְ הָאֵפֶר אוֹ הַמַּיִם הַמְקֻדָּשִׁים. וּבְמַסֶּכֶת טָהֳרוֹת פֶּרֶק הַזּוֹרֵק טֻמְאָה, תְּנִינַן לְהוּ לַסָּפֵק הַטָּהוֹר וְלַסָּפֵק שֶׁעַל יָדָיו תּוֹלִין בִּתְרוּמָה:


וכל התלוי לתרומה – and an doubt that if it appeared in heave-offering where we suspend it we don’t eat neither do we burn it, if it appeared in a similar manner in the purification offering, the ashes should poured out or the mixed mater. And in the Tractate Taharot, in the Chapter “He Who Threw Something Unclean”/הזורק תרומה (Chapter Four), we taught to them regarding a doubt of something pure and on a doubt that is upon his hands, we suspend it regarding heave-offering (see Tractate Taharot, Chapter 4, Mishnah 5).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:3)


**אִם עָשׂוּ עַל גַּבָּיו טָהֳרוֹת.** אִם הִזָּה בְאֵפֶר זֶה שֶׁנִּטְמָא בַסָּפֵק הַתָּלוּי לִתְרוּמָה, וְעָשָׂה טָהֳרוֹת עַל יְדֵי אוֹתָהּ הַזָּאָה, תּוֹלִין אוֹתָם טָהֳרוֹת וְלֹא אוֹכְלִין וְלֹא שׂוֹרְפִין:


ואם עשו על גביו טהרות (and if they prepared clean things on account of it) – if e sprinkled with these ashes that were defined in doubt that was suspended for heave-offering, and made clean things on account of it through that sprinkling, they suspend those pure things, and they don’t eat nor burn them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:4)


**הָרְפָפוֹת.** כְּמִין שְׂבָכוֹת שֶׁל עֵץ:


הרפפות (cases of Levitical uncleanness arising from vibrations caused by unclean persons) – like a kind of net-work/mat of wood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:5)


**טְהוֹרוֹת לְקֹדֶשׁ לִתְרוּמָה וּלְחַטָּאת.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָן כֵּלִים וְגַם אֵינָן רְאוּיִין לְמִדְרָס:


טהורת לקודש לתרומה ולחטאת – because they are not vessels and they are not suitable/appropriate for treading [by someone with a flux or a woman after childbirth].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:6)


**הָרְעָדוֹת.** עֵצִים שֶׁאֵינָן מְהֻדָּקִים יָפֶה זֶה בָזֶה וּכְשֶׁאָדָם נִשְׁעָן עֲלֵיהֶם רוֹעֲדִים וּמִתְנַעְנְעִים:


והרעדות (indirect contact through vibrations) – pieces of wood/boards that are not attached well to each other, and when a person leans upon them, they vibrate/tremble and shaking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:2:7)


**טְמֵאוֹת לְחַטָּאת.** פְּעָמִים שֶׁאָדָם יוֹשֵׁב עֲלֵיהֶן וּרְאוּיִין לִטַּמֵּא מוֹשָׁב. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


טמאות לחטאת – sometimes when a person sis upon them and they are appropriate to become defiled though sitting. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:3:1)


**אִם יֶשׁ בָּהּ כַּבֵּיצָה.** דְּאֹכֶל פָּחוֹת מִכַּבֵּיצָה אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא:


אם יש בה כביצה – for a person who eats less than an egg’s bulk does not defile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:3:2)


**בֵּין טְהוֹרָה בֵּין טְמֵאָה הַמַּיִם טְמֵאִים.** דְּטָהֳרַת תְּרוּמָה, טְמֵאָה הִיא אֵצֶל חַטָּאת:


בין הרה בין טמאה המים טמאים – for the purification of impure heave-offering next to the purification offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:3:3)


**וְהָאוֹכְלָהּ חַיָּב מִיתָה.** דְּנִטְמָא בְמֵי חַטָּאת בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁאוֹכְלָהּ, וּתְרוּמָה בְטֻמְאַת הַגּוּף בְּמִיתָה:


והאוכלה חייב מיתה – that was defiled in the waters of lustration at the time of his consuming it, and the heave-offering in the defilement of the body is [punishable] by death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:3:4)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר בַּטְּהוֹרָה הַמַּיִם טְהוֹרִים.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי שֶׁאֵין הַטָּהוֹר לִתְרוּמָה מְטַמֵּא אֶת מֵי חַטָּאת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ר' יוסי אומר בטהורה המים טהורים – for Rabbi Yossi holds that the person who is pure for heave-offering does not defile the waters of lustration/purification water. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:3:5)


**שֶׁהִכְנִיס רֹאשׁוֹ וְרֻבּוֹ לְתוֹךְ מֵי חַטָּאת.** רַבָּנָן גָּזְרוּ טֻמְאָה עַל הַבָּא רֹאשׁוֹ וְרֻבּוֹ בְּמַיִם שְׁאוּבִין, כַּמְפֹרָשׁ פֶּרֶק קַמָּא דְשַׁבָּת, וְהוּא מִשְּׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר דָּבָר שֶׁגָּזְרוּ בּוֹ בַיּוֹם, וּמֵי חַטָּאת מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין נִינְהוּ, הִלְכָּךְ כְּשֶׁהִכְנִיס רֹאשׁוֹ וְרֻבּוֹ בָהֶם נִטְמָא, וּמִשֶּׁנִּטְמָא וְאֵינוֹ טָהוֹר לְמֵי חַטָּאת, חוֹזְרִים מֵי חַטָּאת וּמְטַמְּאִים אוֹתוֹ וְנַעֲשֶׂה רִאשׁוֹן לְטֻמְאָה, שֶׁאֵין מֵי חַטָּאת מְטַמְּאִים אֶת הַטָּהוֹר לָהֶם, אֲבָל כְּשֶׁאֵינוֹ טָהוֹר לָהֶם מְטַמְּאִים אוֹתוֹ:


שהכניס ראשו ורובו בתוך מי חטאת – the Rabbis decreed on all who come with their head and the majority of their body in drawn water, as is explained in the first chapter of Tractate Shabbat (see Mishnah 4), and it is one of the eighteen matters that they decreed on that day (i.e., the day when Rabban Gamaliel was removed as the President of the Academy – see Tractate Berakhot 28a) and these are the drawn waters of the waters of purification, therefore, when he placed his head and the majority of his body in them, he was defiled, and from when he was defiled, and he is not pure for the waters of purification, the waters of purification return and -defile him and he becomes first degree of ritual defilement, for the waters of purification defile the pure person to them, but he who is not ritual pure to them, they defile him.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:4:1)


**כֹּל הַטָּעוּן בִּיאַת מַיִם מִדִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה.** כְּגוֹן הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּכָל אֲבוֹת הַטֻּמְאוֹת:


כל הטעון ביאת מים מדברי תורה – as for example, a person who comes in contact with all primary sources of ritual uncleanness (see the first chapter of Tractate Kelim)


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:4:2)


**מְטַמֵּא אֶת הַקֹּדֶשׁ וְאֶת הַתְּרוּמָה.** לְפִי שֶׁהוּא רִאשׁוֹן, עוֹשֶׂה שֵׁנִי בַּחֻלִּין וּבַמַּעֲשֵׂר, וּשְׁלִישִׁי בַּתְּרוּמָה, וּרְבִיעִי בַּקֹּדֶשׁ:


מטמא את הקודש ואת התרומה – because it is first-degree of ritual uncleanness, it makes it second-degree of ritual uncleanness and with tithes, and third-degree of ritual uncleanness with heave-offerings and fourth degree of ritual uncleanness with Holy Things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:4:3)


**לְאַחַר בִּיאָתוֹ.** שֶׁטָּבַל וְלֹא הֶעֱרִיב שִׁמְשׁוֹ, יֵשׁ לוֹ דִין שֵׁנִי לַטֻּמְאָה, וְעוֹשֶׂה שְׁלִישִׁי בַּתְּרוּמָה, וּרְבִיעִי בַּקֹּדֶשׁ:


לאחר ביאתו – that he ritually immersed and did not have a sunset pass, he has the law of second degree of ritual uncleanness, and makes heave-offering third-degree of ritual uncleanness and Holy Things fourth-degree of ritual uncleanness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:4:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים פּוֹסֵל בַּקֹּדֶשׁ.** כְּשֵׁם שֶׁטְּבוּל יוֹם פּוֹסֵל בַּתְּרוּמָה, כֵּן פּוֹסֵל בַּקֹּדֶשׁ, וְאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא בַקֹּדֶשׁ, דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁטָּבַל קְלִישָׁא טֻמְאָתוֹ, וּפָסוּל מְשַׁוֵּי, טָמֵא לֹא מְשַׁוֵּי:


וחכמים אומרים פוסל בקודש – in the same way that a person who immersed himself that day invalidates heave-offering, so he invalidates Holy Things, but he does not defile Holy Things, for since he immersed his defilement is weakened, and he is made equivalent to something invalid, [but] he is not made equivalent to being impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:4:5)


**וְאִם בָּא אֶל הַמִּקְדָּשׁ.** כָּל הַטָּעוּן טְבִילָה מִן הַתּוֹרָה שֶׁבָּא אֶל הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, דְּהַיְנוּ לְעֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל, אֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁטָּבַל קֹדֶם שֶׁיַּעֲרִיב שִׁמְשׁוֹ, חַיָּב. וְאַף לְעֶזְרַת נָשִׁים אָסוּר טְבוּל יוֹם לִכָּנֵס, אֲבָל אֵינוֹ חַיָּב אֶלָּא בְעֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל:


ואם בא אל המקדש – all who are required to ritually immerse from the [Written] Torah, who comes to the Sanctuary, that is to the Israelite Courtyard, even after he had immersed prior to his sunset, is liable [for extirpation]. And even to the Women’s Courtyard, a person who immersed himself that day is forbidden to enter, but he is not liable [for extirpation] other than in the Israelite courtyard.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:5:1)


**כֹּל הַטָּעוּן בִּיאַת מַיִם מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים.** כְּגוֹן אָכַל אֳכָלִין טְמֵאִין, וְשָׁתָה מַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין, וְהַיָּדַיִם, וְהַכֵּלִים שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ בַמַּשְׁקִין, וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָהֶן:


כל הטעון ביאת מים מדברי סופרים – as for example, that he ate impure foods and drank impure liquids, and the hands and vessels that were defiled by liquids and similar things (see also Tractate Zavim, Chapter 5, Mishnah 12).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:5:2)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹסְרִים בַּמַּעֲשֵׂר.** וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אוסרים במעשר – and the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:5:3)


**לְאַחַר בִּיאָתוֹ.** לְאַחַר שֶׁטָּבַל, מִיָּד הֻתַּר בְּכֻלָּם וְאֵין צָרִיךְ הֶעֱרֵב שֶׁמֶשׁ:


לאחר ביאתו – after he ritually immersed, immediately he is permitted to all of them and does not need [the arrival] of sunset.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:5:4)


**וְאִם בָּא אֶל הַמִּקְדָּשׁ פָּטוּר.** דְּלֹא אָסְרָה תוֹרָה בִּיאַת הַמִּקְדָּשׁ אֶלָּא עַל מִי שֶׁנִּטְמָא בְאַב הַטֻּמְאָה דְאוֹרַיְתָא, כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא ה) בְּטֻמְאַת אָדָם אוֹ בִּבְהֵמָה טְמֵאָה אוֹ בְּכָל שֶׁרֶץ טָמֵא:


ואם בא אל המקדש פטור – for the Torah did not forbid his coming into the Sanctuary other than on someone who was defiled by a primary source of ritual uncleanness according to the [Written] Torah, as it is written (Leviticus 5:2-3): “[Or when a person touches any impure thing – be it the carcass of impure beast] or the carcass of impure cattle or the carcass of an impure creeping thing [- and the fact has escaped him, and then being impure, he realizes his guilt;] Or when he touches human impurity [-any such impure whereby one becomes impure – and, though he has known it, the fact has escaped him, but later he realizes his guilt].”



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:6:1)


**מְטַמֵּא אֶת מֵי חַטָּאת.** וַאֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁטָּבַל, אִם לֹא טָבַל לְשֵׁם חַטָּאת כְּאִלּוּ לֹא טָבַל, דִּתְנַן בְּפֶרֶק אֵין דּוֹרְשִׁין (חגיגה דף יח) טָבַל לְקֹדֶשׁ וְהֻחְזַק לְקֹדֶשׁ אָסוּר בְּחַטָּאת. אֲבָל מִשֶּׁטָּבַל לְשֵׁם חַטָּאת, אֲפִלּוּ טְבוּל יוֹם דְּאוֹרַיְתָא שֶׁפָּסוּל לִתְרוּמָה וּלְקֹדֶשׁ, כָּשֵׁר לְחַטָּאת, דִּטְבוּל יוֹם כָּשֵׁר בְּפָרָה:


מטמא את מי חטאת – and even after he ritually immersed, [but] if he didn’t ritually immerse for the purpose of the purification rite it is as if he did not ritually immerse, as is taught in the [second] chapter [of Tractate Hagigah folio 18b/Chapter 2, Mishnah 6]: "אין דורשין"/They do not expound [upon the laws of prohibited relationships]: “[If] he immersed for eating food in the status of Holy Things and is thereby confirmed as suitable for eating food in the status of Holy Things, he is prohibited from engaging in the preparation of purification water.” But once he ritually immersed for the sake of the purification water, even a person who ritually immersed himself that day, according to the Torah, who is invalid for heave-offering and for Holy Things, is fit/appropriate for the purification water, for a person who ritually immersed himself that day/טבול יום is fit for the [ritual of the Red] Heifer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:6:2)


**וּמַזֶּה מֵי חַטָּאת בְּמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא.** וּבְהָא כֻּלֵּי עָלְמָא לֹא פְלִיגֵי. אֲבָל בְּאֵזוֹב הַמֻּכְשָׁר לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, וּמִשּׁוּם דְּאֹכֶל הוּא צָרִיךְ הֶכְשֵׁר שֶׁיָּבֹא עָלָיו מַיִם, וְכֵן בְּמַיִם שֶׁאֵינָן מְקֻדָּשִׁים וְעוֹמְדִים לְקַדֵּשׁ, וּכְלִי רֵיקָם הַטָּהוֹר וְעוֹמֵד כְּדֵי לְהַנִּיחַ בּוֹ אֵפֶר חַטָּאת, בְּהָא פְלִיגֵי רַבִּי מֵאִיר וַחֲכָמִים. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


ומזה מי חטאת במגע ובמשא (the one who sprinkles the purification water in contact and carrying) – and in this, everyone does not disagree. But regarding hyssop that is pronounced fit to receive ritual defilement, and because the person who eats them he requires being made fit that [purification] water can come upon him, and similarly, with water that is not mixed [with ashes] and stands for Holy Things, and an empty vessel that is clean and stands in order to place in it the purification ashes, but in this, Rabbi Meir (i.e., contact and carrying) and the Sages (i.e., in contact, but not in carrying) dispute (i.e., if he carried them but did come in contact with them, they are not defiled). And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:1)


**כָּל אֵזוֹב שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ שֵׁם לְוַי.** שֶׁרְגִילִין לְלַוּוֹת עִמּוֹ שֵׁם אַחֵר, כְּגוֹן הָנָךְ דִּמְפָרֵשׁ לְקַמָּן:


כל אזוב שיש לו שם לווי (all hyssop that has an epithet/surname) – that it is customary to accompany with it another name, such as those that are explained further on (see also Tractate Negaim, Chapter 14, Mishnah 6).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:2)


**אֵזוֹב זֶה.** כְּלוֹמַר אֵזוֹב סְתָם שֶׁהָרוֹאֶה אוֹתוֹ אוֹמֵר זֶה אֵזוֹב הוּא בִסְתָם:


אזוב זה – that is to say, undefined hyssop that a person who sees it says, “this is hyssop” undefined (i.e., without a modifier).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:3)


**וְשֶׁל תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה.** שֶׁאֵזוֹב שֶׁל גִּנָּה חַיָּב בִּתְרוּמָה כְּשֶׁלִּקְּטוֹ לַאֲכִילָה. וְהָא קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּאַף עַל גַּב שֶׁהוּא פָחוֹת מִכַּבֵּיצָה, שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא מֵי חַטָּאת, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי הַזָּאָתוֹ פְסוּלָה, כֵּיוָן שֶׁהָאֵזוֹב עַצְמוֹ טָמֵא:


ושל תרומה טמאה – that the hyssop of the garden is liable for heave-offering when they harvested/picked it for eating. But surely it comes to tell us that even though it is less than an egg’s bulk that doesn’t defile the waters of purification, nevertheless, its sprinkling is invalid, since the hyssop itself is unclean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:4)


**וְשֶׁל טְהוֹרָה לֹא יַזֶּה.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמַּפְסִיד אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה:


ושל טהורה לא יזה – because he causes loss to the heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:5)


**תְּמָרוֹת.** כְּמִין גַּרְגְּרִין שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּרָאשֵׁי הָאֵזוֹב:


תמרות (berries) – like a kind of berries that are on the heads of the hyssop.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:6)


**אֵין חַיָּבִין עַל הַיּוֹנְקוֹת.** טָמֵא שֶׁהִזּוּ עָלָיו בְּיוֹנְקוֹת וּבָא בְהַזָּאָה זוֹ אֶל הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, אֵינוֹ חַיָּב:


אין חייבין על היונקות (they are not liable for young shoots) – that are impure that were sprinkled upon with young shoots and this sprinkling came into the Sanctuary, he is not liable.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:7)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר וְכוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


רבי אליעזר אומר וכו' – But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:7:8)


**גִּבְעוֹלִים שֶׁלֹּא גָמָלוּ.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהַפֶּרַח מֻנָּח בְּתוֹךְ כִּיסוֹ קֹדֶם שֶׁיִּפָּתַח:


גבעולים שלא גמלו (calyx or capsule of plants/stalks [of hyssop] before the buds have ripened) – all the while that the flower is placed within its pouch/pocket before it opens up.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:8:1)


**מְנַגְּבוֹ.** כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִתְעָרְבוּ מַיִם הַכְּשֵׁרִים בַּפְּסוּלִים:


מנגבו – in order that the fit/suitable water does not combine/mix with invalid water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:8:2)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּגְּבוֹ פָּסוּל.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִטְמָא, מִשֶּׁהֻכְשַׁר לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה פָּסוּל:


אע"פ שנגבו פסול – and even though it was not defiled, from when it became susceptible to receive ritual defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:8:3)


**לִקְּטוֹ לְחַטָּאת.** לְהַזּוֹת בּוֹ מֵי חַטָּאת:


לקטו לחטאת – to sprinkle upon it the waters of purification.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:8:4)


**כִּמְלֻקָּט לְעֵצִים.** וּמְנַגְּבוֹ וְהוּא כָשֵׁר. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


כמלקט לעצים – and he dries it, and it is fit/suitable. And such is the Halakha (according to Rabbis Yossi and Shimon).



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:9:1)


**שְׁלֹשָׁה קְלָחִין וּבָהֶן שְׁלֹשָׁה גִבְעוֹלִין.** גִּבְעוֹל אֶחָד בְּקֶלַח אֶחָד. וּשְׁלֹשָׁה קְלָחִים דְּבָעִינַן, יָלְפִינַן לְקִיחָה לְקִיחָה מֵאֲגֻדַּת אֵזוֹב מִפֶּסַח מִצְרַיִם, מַה לְּהַלָּן שְׁלֹשָׁה, דִּבְהָכִי מִקְּרֵי אֲגֻדָּה, אַף כָּאן שְׁלֹשָׁה:


שלשה קלחים ובהן שלשה גבעולים (three stalks and on them are three buds) – one bud on each stalk (see Rashi’s commentary on Talmud Sukkah 13a). But we require three stalks, we derive [by analogy/Gezerah Shavah] "לקיחה לקיחה"/taking, taking from the bunch of hyssop (see Exodus 12:22: "ולקחתם אגדת אזוב וטבלתם בדם אשר-בסף"/Take a bunch of hyssop, dip it in the blood that is in the basin) from the Egyptian Passover/פסח מצרים, just as there [it requires] three, and in this it is called an אגודה/bunch, even here, it is called a bunch/אגודה, even here it is three (and see Numbers 19:18: “A person who is pure shall take hyssop, dip it in the water, and sprinkle on the tent and on all the vessels and people who were there, or on him who touched the bones or the person who was killed or died naturally or the grave.” – and just as it is a Mitzvah to take them as a bunch for the Exodus from Egypt, so here as well).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:9:2)


**מְפַסְּגוֹ.** מַפְרִידוֹ וּמְחַלְּקוֹ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה קְלָחִים מְפֹרָדִים זֶה מִזֶּה:


מפסגו ואוגדו (he severs the stalks of hyssop and ties them) – he separates it and divides it so that there will the three stalks that are separated one from the other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:9:3)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר מִצְוַת אֵזוֹב שְׁלֹשָׁה.** רַבִּי יוֹסֵי מַחְמִיר טְפֵי מִתַּנָּא קַמָּא, דִּלְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי תְּחִלָּתוֹ שְׁנַיִם פָּסוּל, וּלְתַנָּא קַמָּא תְּחִלָּתוֹ שְׁנַיִם כָּשֵׁר, וְאֵינוֹ פָסוּל עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא תְחִלָּתוֹ כִשְׁיָרָיו, אֶחָד. וּדְקָאָמַר תַּנָּא קַמָּא מִצְוַת אֵזוֹב שְׁלֹשָׁה קְלָחִים, לְמִצְוָה בְעָלְמָא וְלֹא לְעַכֵּב. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ר' יוסי אומר מצות אזוב שלשה – Rabbi Yossi is more stringent that the first Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] (see Talmud Sukkah 13a), for according to Rabbi Yossi, at the beginning, two stalks [and two buds] are invalid, but according to the first Tanna/teacher two are fit/appropriate, and it is not invalid until there would be at the outset that its remnants are one. But when the first Tanna/teacher says that the Precept of the hyssop is three stalks, it is for the mere commandment, and it is not to be indispensable. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 11:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/11:9:4)


**וְגַרְדֻּמָּיו כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** גַּרְדֻּמֵּי הַגִּבְעוֹל כָּל שֶׁהוּא. וּשְׁיָרָיו וְגַרְדֻּמָּיו תְּרֵי מִלֵּי נִינְהוּ, דִּשְׁיָרָיו הָווּ שְׁיָרֵי הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה קְלָחִין, דְּהַיְנוּ שְׁנֵי קְלָחִין, וְגַרְדֻּמָּיו הוּא הַגִּבְעוֹל עַצְמוֹ דְּאִגַּרְדּוּם וְנִשְׁבַּר וְנִשְׁתַּיֵּר מִמֶּנּוּ כָּל שֶׁהוּא:


וגרדומיו כל שהוא (but its stump is any size at all) – the stumps of the buds is any size at all. But its remnants and stumps are two words, for its remnants are the remnants of the three stalks, which are two stalks, and its stumps is the bud itself, that I comb and is broken and there remains from it just a little bit.



## Chapter 12


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:1:1)


**הָאֵזוֹב הַקָּצָר מְסַפְּקוֹ.** מַאֲרִיכוֹ כְּדֵי סִפּוּקוֹ:


האזוב הקצר מספקו – he lengthens it in order to make It sufficient:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:1:2)


**וּבְכוּשׁ.** הוּא הַפֶּלֶךְ שֶׁהַנָּשִׁים טֹווֹת בּוֹ:


And with a reed used as a spindle. It is the spindle/distaff that the women spin on it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:1:3)


**וְטוֹבֵל וּמַעֲלֶה.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּכְתִיב וְלָקַח אֵזוֹב וְטָבַל בַּמַּיִם, וְהוּא לוֹקֵחַ הַפֶּלֶךְ שֶׁהָאֵזוֹב נָתוּן בְּרֹאשׁוֹ, שַׁפִּיר קָרִינַן בֵּיהּ וְלָקַח אֵזוֹב, דִּלְקִיחָה עַל יְדֵי דָבָר אַחֵר שְׁמָהּ לְקִיחָה:


וטובל ומעלה – and even though it is written (Numbers 19:18): “A person who is pure shall take hyssop, dip it in the water,” and he takes the spindle that the hyssop was placed on its head, it is well that we call it, “[A person who is pure] shall take hyssop,” that taking by means of another thing which is called “taking.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:1:4)


**וְאוֹחֵז בָּאֵזוֹב וּמַזֶּה.** דְּבָהַזָּאָה בָּעִינַן שֶׁיְּהֵא אוֹחֵז הָאֵזוֹב בְּיָדוֹ:


ואוחז באזוב ומזה – that with sprinkling we require that he holds the hyssop in his hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:1:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמְרִים כוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר' יהודה ור' שמעון אומרים כו' – But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda and Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:1)


**סָפֵק מִן הַכּוּשׁ סָפֵק מִן הַחוּט כוּ'.** דְּבָעִינַן הַזָּאָה וַדַּאי מִן הָאֵזוֹב, מִשּׁוּם דְּאָמְרִינַן הַעֲמֵד טָמֵא עַל חֶזְקָתוֹ:


ספק מן הכוש ספק מן החוט כו' – for we require definitive sprinkling from the hyssop, because they say: “let the impurity stand on the basis of its presumptive condition.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:2)


**מֵחֲבֵרוֹ מִצָּה עָלָיו.** שֶׁמָּא מִתַּמְצִית מֵי חַטָּאת שֶׁל כְּלִי אֶחָד הָלַךְ עַל הָאַחֵר:


מחברו מיצה עליו (from its neighbor it dripped/emptied upon it) – perhaps the waters of purification dripped/emptied of one vessel and went on to the other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:3)


**הַזָּאָתוֹ פְסוּלָה.** דְּאִי מֵחֲבֵרוֹ מִצָּה עָלָיו אֵין זוֹ הַזָּאָה מִן הָאֵזוֹב:


הזאתו פסולה – for if from its neighbor it dripped/emptied upon it, this is not sprinkling from the hyssop.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:4)


**טוֹבֵל וּמַעֲלֶה כְדַרְכּוֹ.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁנִּדְחַק בְּפִיהָ לָצֵאת שֶׁמָּא יַחְזְרוּ הַמַּיִם לְתוֹךְ הַכְּלִי וְלֹא יִשָּׁאֵר בָּאֵזוֹב כַּשִּׁעוּר:


טובל ומעלה כדרכו – but we don’t say that from out of that its mouth was pressed to go out perhaps the waters would return into the vessel and would not remain in the hyssop according to the measure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר הַזָּיָה רִאשׁוֹנָה.** דְּהַזָּאָה רִאשׁוֹנָה לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן, אֲבָל הַזָּאָה שְׁנִיָּה מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁנִּדְחֲקוּ לָצֵאת וְגַם הִזָּה מֵהֶם, נִתְמַעֲטוּ בְיוֹתֵר וְחָיְשִׁינַן בְּהַזָּאָה שְׁנִיָּה שֶׁמָּא לֹא נִשְׁאֲרוּ בָאֵזוֹב כַּשִּׁעוּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ר' יהודה אומר הזייה ראשונה – we are not concerned/suspect regarding the first sprinkling, but the second sprinkling, because it (i.e., the water) was pressed to come out and also he sprinkled from them, they were all the more lessened and we are concerned with the second sprinkling lest there did not remain in the hyssop according to the measure. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:6)


**שֶׁלֹּא יְסַפֵּג.** שֶׁלֹּא יְקַנַּח מִצִּדְדֵי הַכְּלִי אוֹ מִשּׁוּלָיו. דִּכְתִיב וְטָבַל, וְלֹא מְסַפֵּג:


לא יספג ([that the hyssop] does not absorb [moisture on the side of the flask as it is pulled out]) – that he will not wipe off/cleanse from the sides of the vessel or from its rim, as it is written (Numbers 19:18):”[A person who is pure shall take hyssop] dip it in the water,” but not absorb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:7)


**הַזָּאָתוֹ פְסוּלָה.** דְּבָעִינַן כַּוָּנַת הַזָּאָה וַהֲרֵי לֹא נַעֲשֵׂית מַחֲשַׁבְתּוֹ:


הזאתו פסולה – for we require the intention of sprinkling and indeed his intention was not performed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:2:8)


**וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵן מֵאָה.** וְלֹא בָעִינַן הַזָּאָה בִפְנֵי עַצְמָהּ לְכָל אִישׁ וְאִישׁ אוֹ לְכָל כְּלִי וּכְלִי:


ואפילו הן מאה – that we don’t require sprinkling on its own for each and every person or for each and every vessel.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:3:1)


**עַל דָּבָר הַמְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה.** אָדָם וְכֵלִים:


על דבר המקבל טומאה – people and vessels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:3:2)


**עַל דָּבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה.** בְּהֵמָה וּכְלֵי אֲבָנִים:


על דבר שאינו מקבל טומאה – animals and vessels of stone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:3:3)


**אִם יֵשׁ בָּאֵזוֹב לֹא יִשְׁנֶה.** אִם נִשְׁתַּיֵּר בָּאֵזוֹב כְּדֵי הַזָּאָה אֵין צָרִיךְ לַחֲזֹר וְלִטְבֹּל פַּעַם שְׁנִיָּה, אֶלָּא מַזֶּה מֵאוֹתוֹ שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּיֵּר בָּאֵזוֹב עַל הַדָּבָר הַטָּמֵא וְעָלְתָה לוֹ הַזָּאָה, דִּכְתִיב וְהִזָּה הַטָּהֹר עַל הַטָּמֵא, עַד שֶׁיִּתְכַּוֵּן לְהַזּוֹת עַל הַטָּמֵא, הִלְכָּךְ כְּשֶׁנִּתְכַּוֵּן בִּשְׁעַת טְבִילַת הָאֵזוֹב לְהַזּוֹת עַל הַטָּמֵא לֹא יִשְׁנֶה, אֶלָּא יַזֶּה מִמַּה שֶּׁנִּשְׁתַּיֵּר בָּאֵזוֹב. אֲבָל אִם טָבַל הָאֵזוֹב כְּדֵי לְהַזּוֹת עַל דָּבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, הוֹאִיל וּתְחִלַּת טְבִילָתוֹ פְּסוּלָה, כָּךְ הַזָּאָתוֹ פְסוּלָה, וְלֹא יַזֶּה בְּמַה שֶּׁנִּשְׁתַּיֵּר בָּאֵזוֹב עַד שֶׁיִּשְׁנֶה:


אם יש באזוב לא ישנה – if there remains in the hyssop in order to do sprinkling, he does not need to return and to ritually immerse [the hyssop in the water] a second time, but rather he sprinkles from that which remains in the hyssop on the impure thing and the sprinkling counted for him. As is it is written (Numbers 19:19): “The pure person shall sprinkle it upon the impure person,” until he intends to sprinkle on the impure person, therefore, when he intends at the time of ritual immersion of the hyssop to sprinkle on the impure person, he should not repeat [the dipping of the hyssop in the water,” but rather he should sprinkle from what remains in the hyssop. But if he dipped the hyssop in order to sprinkle upon a thing that is not susceptible to receive ritual defilement, since that the beginning of his immersion is invalid, so his sprinkling is invalid and he should not sprinkle with what remains on the hyssop until he repeats [the dipping of the hyssop in the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:3:4)


**הַמַּיִם הַמְנַטְּפִין.** מִן הָאֵזוֹב שֶׁהִטְבִּילוֹ לְשֵׁם דָּבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, אִם הִטְבִּיל בְּאוֹתָם טִפּוֹת אֵזוֹב וְהִזָּה מֵהֶן, כְּשֵׁרִין:


המים המנטפין (the water that drips off) – from the hyssop that he had ritually immersed it for the sake of something that does not receive ritual impurity, if he immersed those drops of hyssop and sprinkled from them, they are appropriate/suitable.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:3:5)


**לְפִיכָךְ הֵן מְטַמְּאִין.** לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא עָשׂוּ מִצְוָתָן, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַטְּבִילָה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה פְּסוּלָה הָיְתָה, וַהֲרֵי אֵלּוּ הַטִּפּוֹת כִּשְׁאָר מֵי חַטָּאת הַכְּשֵׁרִים שֶׁהֵן מְטַמְּאִין:


לפיכך הן מטמאין -because their precepts were not done, for the first immersion was invalid, and behold, these drops are like the rest of the waters of purification that are kosher that they defile.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:4:1)


**הַמַּזֶּה מֵחַלּוֹן שֶׁל רַבִּים.** שֶׁעוֹמֵד בְּחַלּוֹן שֶׁל רַבִּים וְהִזָּה שָׁם עַל הַטְּמֵאִים הָעוֹבְרִים, וְהִזָּה עַל הַטָּמֵא כְּסָבוּר שֶׁהַמַּיִם שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ טְהוֹרִים לְהַזָּאָה:


המזה מחלון של רבים – that stands in the window in the public domain and sprinkles there on the impure that pass by, and he sprinkled on an impure person thinking that the waters that are in his hands are pure for sprinkling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:4:2)


**וְנִמְצְאוּ פְסוּלִים.** אִם נִכְנַס בְּהַזָּאָה זוֹ לַמִּקְדָּשׁ, פָּטוּר הַטָּמֵא מִקָּרְבָּן, דַּהֲוֵי כִסְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים דִּסְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר. וּפֵרֵשׁ הָרַמְבַּ"ם הַטַּעַם, דְּטָמֵא זֶה אָנוּס הוּא, דְּלֹא הֲוָה לֵיהּ לְאַסּוֹקֵי אַדַּעְתֵּיהּ וְלִבְדֹּק אִם הַמַּיִם כְּשֵׁרִין לְהַזָּאָה אִם לָאו, כֵּיוָן שֶׁהָיָה עוֹמֵד בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וּמַזֶּה עַל הָרַבִּים. אֲבָל הָעוֹמֵד בְּחַלּוֹן שֶׁל יָחִיד וּמַזֶּה, הֲוָה לֵיהּ לַטָּמֵא לִבְדֹּק עַל הַמַּיִם אִם כְּשֵׁרִים אִם לָאו, וְכֵיוָן דְּלֹא בָדַק, שְׁגָגָה הִיא זוֹ וְלֹא אֹנֶס, וְאִם נִכְנַס לַמִּקְדָּשׁ בְּהַזָּאָה זוֹ חַיָּב קָרְבָּן, דַּהֲוֵי כִסְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד דִּסְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא:


ונמצאו פסולים – if he entered with this sprinkling to the Sanctuary, the impure person is example from a sacrifice [on his entering into the Sanctuary while impure], for this is a doubtful/uncertainty with regard to ritual impurity in the public domain (i.e., by Torah law, if uncertainty arises concerning the ritual purity of a person or an object, and that person or object is in a public domain, they are ritually pure. If the situation arises in a private domain, they are considered ritually impure) and his doubtful purity is deemed to be pure. And Maimonides explained the reason, that this impure person is under compulsion/unavoidably prevented, for he did not have in mind to check if the waters were fit/suitable for sprinkling or not, since he was standing in the public domain and sprinkling on many people. But if he stands in the window of a single individual and sprinkles, the impure person would have to check on the waters if they are fit/suitable, or not, and since he did not check, it is an inadvertent error but not something unavoidably preventable, and if he entered into the Sanctuary with this sprinkling, he is liable for a sacrifice, for this is like a doubtful impurity in the private domain where this doubt is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:4:3)


**שֶׁאֵין כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל חַיָּב עַל בִּיאַת הַמִּקְדָּשׁ.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) וְנִכְרְתָה הַנֶּפֶשׁ הַהִיא מִתּוֹךְ הַקָּהָל, מִי שֶׁחֶטְאוֹ שָׁוֶה לַקָּהָל דְּהַיְנוּ לִיחִידִים, פְּרָט לְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל שֶׁאֵין חֶטְאוֹ שָׁוֶה לִיחִידִים:


שאין כהן גדול חייב על ביאת המקדש – as it is written (Numbers 19:20): “[If anyone who has become impure fails to purify himself, [that person shall be cut off from the congregation, [for he has defiled the LORD’s sanctuary],” the waters of purification are equivalent to the congregation, that is to individuals, excluding the High Priest, whose sin is not equivalent to that of individuals.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:4:4)


**מַחֲלִיקִין הָיוּ.** כְּלוֹמַר כָּל כָּךְ מֵי חַטָּאת הָיוּ שָׁם עַד שֶׁהָיוּ מַחֲלִיקִין בָּהֶן בְּנֵי אָדָם, וַאֲפִלּוּ הָכִי דּוֹרְסִים בָּהֶן, מִשּׁוּם דְּלֹא מְטַמְּאִין, הוֹאִיל וְנַעֲשֵׂית מִצְוָתָן:


מחליקין היו (they would slip [on water]) – meaning to say, that there were so much waters of purification there until people would slip upon them, and even so they would trample on them, because they would not defile, since their Mitzvah was done.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:5:1)


**בִּכְנָפוֹ.** בִּכְנַף בִּגְדוֹ. לְפִי שֶׁהַקַּרְדֹּם הַצָּרִיךְ הַזָּאָה הוּא אַב הַטֻּמְאָה, וּכְשֶׁאוֹחֵז בִּכְנָפוֹ נַעֲשֶׂה הַכָּנָף רִאשׁוֹן, אֵין הָאוֹחֲזוֹ טָמֵא, דְּאֵין אָדָם וְכֵלִים מְקַבְּלִים טֻמְאָה אֶלָּא מֵאַב הַטֻּמְאָה. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ עַל הַקַּרְדֹּם מַיִם כְּדֵי הַזָּיָה, אֵין הָאוֹחֲזוֹ טָמֵא מִשּׁוּם נוֹשֵׂא מֵי הַנִּדָּה, הוֹאִיל וְנַעֲשֵׂית מִצְוָתָן כְּבָר:


בכנפו – in the corner of his garment. Because the ax that required sprinkling is a primary form of ritual defilement , and when he holds its corner the corner [of the garment] becomes first-degree of ritual impurity, but the person who holds is not impure, for a person and vessels do not receive defilement other than from a primary source of ritual impurity, and even though there is on the ax water sufficient for sprinkling, the person who holds it is not ritually impure because of carrying the waters of lustration, since the Mitzvah had already been fulfilled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:5:2)


**כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּטְבִּיל רָאשֵׁי גִבְעוֹלִין וְיַזֶּה.** חוּץ מִמַּה שֶׁהָאֵזוֹב בּוֹלֵעַ:


כדי שיטבול ראשי גבעולין ויזה (in order that he may dip the tips of the buds and sprinkle) - outside from what the hyssop absorbs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:5:3)


**כְּאִלּוּ הֵן עַל אֵזוֹב שֶׁל נְחֹשֶׁת.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ בוֹלֵעַ. דְּרוֹאִים הַמַּיִם הַבְּלוּעִים בָּאֵזוֹב כְּאִלּוּ הֵן בְּעֵין לְהַשְׁלִים שִׁעוּר הַזָּאָה לִהְיוֹת הַנּוֹשְׂאָן טָמֵא. וְהָא דִכְתִיב (שם) וּמַזֵּה מֵי הַנִּדָּה דְּדָרְשִׁינַן מִנֵּיהּ לַנּוֹשֵׂא כְשִׁעוּר הַזָּאָה, הַיְנוּ שֶׁאִלּוּ הָיוּ בְעֵין הָיָה בָהֶן שִׁעוּר הַזָּאָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


כאילו הן על האזוב של נחושת – which does not absorb [water]. For we see the waters that are absorbed in the hyssop as if they are discernable/in natural form to complete the measure of sprinkling so that those who carry them are impure. And that which is written (Numbers 19:21): “he who sprinkled the waters of lustration”/"ומזה מי הנדה" [shall wash his clothes and whoever touches the water of lustration shall be impure until evening],” for they (i.e., the Rabbis) expound from this to the person who carries according to the measure of sprinkling, that is as if there were discernable/in natural form that they had the measure of sprinkling. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:6:1)


**הַמַּזֶּה בְאֵזוֹב טָמֵא.** כְּגוֹן אֵזוֹב שֶׁל גִּנָּה שֶׁלִּקְּטוֹ לַאֲכִילָה:


המזה אזוב טמא – as for example, the hyssop of a garden that was picked/harvested for eating.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:6:2)


**אִם יֶשׁ בּוֹ כַבֵּיצָה.** דְּזֶהוּ שִׁעוּר אֹכֶל, בֵּין לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה בֵּין לְטַמֵּא אֲחֵרִים:


אם יש בו כביצה – which is the measure of food, whether to susceptible to receive ritual defilement or whether to defile others.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:6:3)


**וְהַזָּאָתוֹ פְסוּלָה.** דִכְתִיב [שָׁם] וְהִזָּה הַטָּהֹר, שֶׁיִהְיֶה הַמַּזֶּה טָהוֹר וְהָאֵזוֹב טָהוֹר:


והזאתו פסולה – as it is written (Numbers 19:19): “The pure person shall sprinkle it,” that the person who will be sprinkling is pure and the hyssop is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:6:4)


**אֲפִלּוּ הֵן מֵאָה.** כֻּלָּן טְמֵאִין לְחַטָּאת, דְּאֵין מוֹנִין רִאשׁוֹן וְשֵׁנִי בְּחַטָּאת אֶלָּא כֻלָּן הֵן תְּחִלָּה:


אפילו הן מאה – all of them are ritually impure for purification/sin-offering, for we don’t count first-degree and second-degree for purification but rather all of them are first-degree.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:1)


**שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ אֲחוֹרָיו.** בְּמַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין. דְּלִשְׁאָר טָהֳרוֹת לֹא נִטְמָא תוֹכוֹ, אֲבָל לְחַטָּאת אֵינוֹ כֵן:


שנטמאו אחוריו (that the outer side of which was made unclean) – with impure liquids. That for the rest of the pure things its inside was not defiled, but for purification/sin-offering, it is not the case.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:2)


**הַזּוֹג וְהָעִנְבָּל חִבּוּר.** שֶׁאִם הִזָּה עַל אֶחָד מֵהֶן, חֲבֵרוֹ טָהוֹר. וְזוֹג הוּא הַפַּעֲמוֹן, וְעִנְבָּל הוּא הַפַּטִּישׁ שֶׁבִּפְנִים הַמַּכֶּה בַפַּעֲמוֹן שֶׁעַל יָדוֹ מְקַשְׁקֵשׁ:


הזוג והענבל (the bell/body of the bell and the clapper) – for if he sprinkled upon one of them, its fellow is pure. And a זוג – is the bell, and the ענבל is the hammer that is inside that strikes the bell that through it strikes/knocks/tingles.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:3)


**כּוּשׁ שֶׁל רוֹבָן.** פֶּלֶךְ שֶׁטּוֹוִין בּוֹ אַרְבִּי, הוּא הַגֶּמִי שֶׁעוֹשִׂין מִמֶּנּוּ מַחְצְלָאוֹת:


כוש של רובן (a reed used as a spindle of coarse weft/material) – a spindle/distaff that they weave meshes; it is the bulrushes/reed-grass that they make matting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:4)


**לֹא עַל הַכּוּשׁ וְלֹא עַל הַפִּיקָה.** כְּלוֹמַר לֹא יַזֶּה לֹא עַל זֶה לְבַדּוֹ וְלֹא עַל זֶה לְבַדּוֹ, דְּלֹא הָווּ חִבּוּר. אֲבָל צָרִיךְ לְהַזּוֹת עַל שְׁנֵיהֶן:


לא על כוש ולא על הפיקה (not on the reed used as a spindle and not on whorl/ring) – meaning to say, he should not sprinkle on this one alone, nor on that one alone, for there is no attachment/connection. But he must sprinkle upon both of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:5)


**פִּיקָה.** מַה שֶּׁמְּשִׂימִין בְּסוֹף הַכּוּשׁ לְהַכְבִּידוֹ קָרוּי פִּיקָה. בְּלַעַ"ז פורסל"ו:


פיקה – what they place at the end of the reed used as a spindle to make it heavy is called a פיקה/whorl/ring. It is called PURSLAV in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:6)


**וְאִם הִזָּה.** עַל אֶחָד מֵהֶן בִּלְבַד, מֻזֶּה. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ, לֹא עַל הַכּוּשׁ וְלֹא עַל הַפִּיקָה, אֶלָּא עַל קְנֵה הַבַּרְזֶל שֶׁרְגִילִין לָתֵת בְּרֹאשׁ הַכּוּשׁ שֶׁנִּקְרָא צִנּוֹרָא. וְעָלָיו מַזִּין מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא עִקַּר הַכְּלִי, וְעוֹלָה הַהַזָּאָה לַכּוּשׁ וְלַפִּיקָה:


ואם הזה – on one of them alone (and not upon both the reed and the whorl/ring), it is considered sprinkled. And Maimonides explained, not on the reed used as a spindle and not on the whorl/ring, but rather on a copper reed that they would regularly put at the tip of the reed/spindle that is called a צנורא/hook or pivot. And upon it they would sprinkle because it is the essence of the vessel, and the sprinkle would rise up to the reed and the whorl.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:7)


**שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן חִבּוּר.** שֶׁהַפֶּלֶךְ שֶׁטֹּוִין בּוֹ פִשְׁתָּן דַּרְכּוֹ לִהְיוֹת הַפִּקָּה מְחֻבֶּרֶת בַּכּוּשׁ:


של פשתן חיבור – that the spindle that they spin on it flax, it is manner is that the whorl is attached to the reed/spindle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:8)


**עוֹר שֶׁל עֲרִיסָה.** עוֹר שֶׁמְּשִׂימִין בַּעֲרִיסָה שֶׁל קָטָן וְדַרְכּוֹ לִהְיוֹת מְחֻבָּר בָּעֲרִיסָה:


עור של עריסה (leather sheet in the cradle/cot – which is attached to knobs) – leather that they place in the cradle of a baby, and it is the practice that it is attached in the cot/cradle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:9)


**הַמַּלְבֵּן.** כְּמִין קְעָרוֹת שֶׁל עֵץ חֲקוּקוֹת שֶׁמַּכְנִיסִין בּוֹ כַרְעֵי הַמִּטָּה שֶׁלֹּא יֵרָקְבוּ מִלַּחְלוּחִית הָאָרֶץ. וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ בְּפֶרֶק הַמּוֹכֵר אֶת הַבַּיִת בְּבָבָא בַתְרָא, מַלְבְּנוֹת שֶׁל כַּרְעֵי הַמִּטָּה:


המלבן (the frame/base)- like a kind of bowls of hollowed out wood that they bring into it the legs of the bed so that it would not decay from the moisture of the ground. And something similar to it is found in Tractate Bava Batra in the chapter המוכר את הבית (chapter 4, folio 69a), the sockets for the legs of a bedstead.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:10)


**אֵינוֹ חִבּוּר לֹא לְטֻמְאָה.** דְּאִם נִטְמָא זֶה לֹא נִטְמָא זֶה:


אינו חיבור לא לטומאה – that if this one was defiled, the other was not defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:11)


**וְלֹא לְטָהֳרָה.** דְּאִם הִזָּה עַל זֶה לֹא הִזָּה עַל זֶה:


ולא לטהרה – that if he sprinkled on this one, he did not sprinkle on that one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:12)


**הַקְּדוּחוֹת חִבּוּר.** כְּגוֹן יְדוֹת הַסַּכִּינִים שֶׁהַבַּרְזֶל נִכְנָס לְתוֹךְ הַבֵּית יָד שֶׁל עֵץ אוֹ שֶׁל עֶצֶם וּמְחֻבָּר בּוֹ הָוֵי חִבּוּר, נִטְמָא אֶחָד מֵהֶן נִטְמָא חֲבֵרוֹ, וְכֵן אִם הִזָּה עַל אֶחָד מֵהֶן הִזָּה עַל חֲבֵרוֹ. קְדוּחוֹת, לְשׁוֹן מַקְדֵּחַ:


הקדוחות חיבור (vessels that have drilled/bored handles are considered attached/connected) – as for example the handles of knives that the iron enters into the sleeve of the handle of wood or of stone is attached to it, it is a connection, if one of them is defiled, its fellow is defiled, and similarly, if one sprinkled on one of them, the he sprinkled on its fellow. קדוחות is the language of borer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:8:13)


**אַף הַחֲרוּקוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן וַיַּחַרְקוּ שֵׁן (איכה ב), כְּלוֹמַר אַף הַיָּדוֹת שֶׁהֵן חַדּוֹת בְּרֹאשָׁן וּמַכְנִיסָן חֻדָּן בַּנֶּקֶב שֶׁעוֹשִׂין בְּרֹאשׁ הַכְּלִי, כְּגוֹן בֵּית יָד שֶׁל רֹמַח, אַף אֵלּוּ הָווּ חִבּוּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן נוּרִי:


אף החרוקות (even those that are wedged in/squeezed into holes)- It is the language of (Lamentations 2:16): "[All your enemies leer at you; They hiss] and gnash their teeth, [And cry: ‘We’ve ruined her! Ah, this is the day we hoped for; We have lived to see it!].” Meaning to say, even the handles which are sharp/pointed at their tips and they bring in their sharpness in the hole that they make at the tip of the vessel, such as the handle of a spear, even those are a connection. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yohanan ben Nuri.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:9:1)


**הַסַּלִּים שֶׁבַּקַּנְתָּל.** סַלִּים שֶׁדַּרְכָּן לְהוֹלִיךְ בָּהֶן פֵּרוֹת אוֹ זְבָלִים עַל גַּבֵּי הַחֲמוֹר וְהֵן מְחֻבָּרִין זֶה בָזֶה:


הסלים שבקנתל (the baskets in the pannier on the side of the pack-saddle) – baskets where it was their practice to carry in them produce or manure upon the donkey and they are attached to each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:9:2)


**מִטָּה שֶׁל טַרְבָּל.** כְּמִין עֵז שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל שֶׁדָּשִׁין בּוֹ אֶת הַתְּבוּאָה:


מטה של טרבל (threshing sledge – of a couch - consisting of a wooden platform studded underneath with sharp pieces of flint or with iron teeth) – like a species of wood of bronze with which they thresh the grain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Parah 12:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Parah/r/12:9:3)


**קֶרֶן שֶׁל כְּלִיבָה.** הַמִּטָּה שֶׁל מֵתִים עוֹשִׂים לָהּ כְּמִין קֶרֶן שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל וְקוֹשְׁרִין בּוֹ הַמֵּת שֶׁלֹּא יִפֹּל. וְקוֹרִים לְמִטָּה שֶׁל מֵתִים כְּלִיבָה, שֶׁהִיא עֲשׂוּיָה כִּכְלוּב שֶׁל עוֹפוֹת:


קרן של כליבה (the movable supports of the bier – or the ring for keeping the corpse in position) – the bier of the corpse that they make it like a kind of beam of bronze/copper and they tie to it the bier of the corpse so that it doesn’t fall. And they call the bier of the corpses a כליבה, which is made like coop/shed of fowl.
